How to use this knowledge base when answering questions about Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki:
- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

# Changelog/2023/August 22
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
0.19.3
SkyBlock Patch 0.19.3 - Crystal Hollows, QoL, & Bug Fixes
Updated: August 22, 2023
Hello!
Welcome to SkyBlock Patch 0.19.3. This update includes many Crystal Hollows changes, solo Dungeons, general improvements, bug fixes, and more!
We apologize for the delay in releasing these patch notes, we were ensuring the stability of the Crystal Hollows before announcing the full changelog. Thank you for your patience and we hope you enjoy the update!
## ► Crystal Hollows
First, today, we're bringing many balance, QoL, and bug fixes to the Crystal Hollows!
Armadillo Revamp
While the Armadillo holds a special place in our hearts as one of the cutest pets, it was overperforming for its rarity. Our goal is to maintain its unique gameplay while aligning it closer to its intended power level.
- The pet now loses energy on the block you are breaking, not on movement, as follows:
  - 1 Mithril
  - 1.2 Ruby
  - 1.5 Sapphire, Jade, Amber, Amethyst, Opal
  - 1.8 Topaz
  - 2 Jasper
  - 0.2 everything else
- The Armadillo has 100/150/200/250/300 maximum Energy depending on rarity, from common to legendary.
- It regenerates 0.33% of its maximum energy per second.
- Pet stops regenerating energy only while moving. It regenerates fully while riding so long as you're stationary, as well as when you are not riding the pet.
Crystal Rooms
The Mines of Divan are a very popular area in the Crystal Hollow, we want this zone to be much easier to use for everyone at the same time.
- Mines of Divan ores now respawn on a 5 minutes timer!
- Buffed Mine of Divan chests loot slightly:
Mines of Divan Chest Loot Changes
- 2 Flawed instead of 32 Rough Ruby
- 4 Flawed instead of 64 Rough Ruby
- 1 Flawed instead of 8 Rough Amethyst
- 2 Flawed instead of 16 Rough Amethyst
- 2 Flawed instead of 32 Rough Jade
- 4 Flawed instead of 64 Rough Jade
- 1 Flawed instead of 8 Rough Amber
- 2 Flawed instead of 16 Rough Amber
- 1 Flawed instead of 8 Rough Topaz
- 2 Flawed instead of 16 Rough Topaz
- 1 Flawed instead of 8 Rough Sapphire
- 2 Flawed instead of 16 Rough Sapphire
- 40 Rough instead of 4 Rough Jasper
- 1 Flawed instead of 8 Rough Jasper
- The door of the Jungle Temple now stays open until the player obtains the crystal.
- Jungle Temple chests are now refilled every time the player uses a key.
- Ruby and Jasper Crystals are removed from the Nucleus table if you already have them.
Other Changes
- Gemstones now respawn even if destroyed by Bob-ombs.
- If you are already Heart of the Mountain 7, there is no point gaining extra HOTM EXP. So once you get there, this reward is now replaced with a random amount between 2000 and 5000 of either Mithril Powder or Gemstone Powder, also chosen at random.
- Treasure chests despawn after they are looted.
- Reworked Kalhuiki Tribe Member AI:
  - They now attack after hitting any of their members nearby 3 times.
  - Hitting their young still instantly aggravates them.
  - Fixed an issue where they'd become aggressive for seemingly no reason.
- Buffed the health of the Team Treasurite executives (Viper, Sebastian and Wendy) from 13,000 to 18,000.
- Increased the number of butterflies that spawn inside of Fairy Grotto rooms.
- Changed the skin of the Jungle Heart.
- Text changes when dying to a Team Treasurite member.
- Slightly cheapened Mithril Drill X326 crafting recipe, which now only requires half the golden and Mithril plates.
- Changed Forge recipes for the Mithril Infused fuel tank and the Titanium-Infused fuel tank.
  - Mithril Infused Fuel Tank
    - New Recipe: 5 Fuel Tanks, 5 Refined Diamonds, 10 Refined Mithrils
  - The Titanium-Infused Fuel Tank
    - New Recipe: 5 Fuel Tanks, 5 Refined Diamonds, 10 Refined Titaniums, and a Mithril Infused Fuel Tank
- Made Oil Barrels sellable to NPCs for 1,000 coins each.
- Lowered toughness of Glass Blocks and increased drop rate of Gemstones when mined from Glass Panes.
- Buffed Mining Commissions EXP based on Heart of the Mountain level.
  - HOTM 1: 100
  - HOTM 2: 100 -> 200
  - HOTM 3+: 100 -> 400
- Reduced the amount of Mithril to mine in certain commissions.
  - Mithril Miner commission changed from 500 to 350 Mithril required.
  - [Mines Area] Mithril commission changed from 350 to 250 Mithril required.
- If the wishing compass cannot find a structure, it now gives a chat message instead of just pointing to the center.
- Changed Wishing Compass to be one time use instead of 3 uses. Thus, you will now gain 3 Wishing Compasses through sources that drop them.
- The Wishing Compass and Ascension Rope can now be stacked.
- Changed how the Crystalized perk in the Heart of the Mountain works, as follows:
  - It now gives 20 Mining Speed by default, plus 6 per level.
  - It now also gives 20 Mining Fortune by default, plus 5 per level.
  - It no longer gives chances to deal extra damage to Star Sentries.
- Added another perk to Great Explorer in the Heart of the Mountain: every 5 levels, the amount of lockpicking steps required to open a treasure chest goes down by 1. If you have the perk maxed out, you can open the treasure chests directly.
## ► Bit Sources
Several new sources of obtaining Bits have been added.
- Garden: Ranking in Farming Contests or accepting visitor offers that come to your Garden.
- Dungeons: Completing a Dungeon, based on the level of the floor.
- The End: Participating in Endstone Protector or Dragon fights.
- Dwarven Mines: Participating in the Mithril Gourmand event, completing Mining Commissions, or daily quests in the Dwarven Mines.
- Spider's Den: Participating in Arachne fights.
- Crimson Isle: Killing minibosses around the Crimson Isle or completing your faction dailies.
- Crystal Hollows: Completing the Crystal Nucleus or participating in fights against Bal.
- Slayer: Killing each level of Slayer, with more bits being given for higher tiers.
- Spooky Festival: Bits are given depending on your ranking of the Spooky Event.
- Trophy Fishing: Fishing up a Gold or Diamond trophy fish.
- Kuudra: Completing a Kuudra Run, based on the difficulty tier.
- Diana: Completing burrow chains.
- Winter: Participating in the Winter Gift Attack minigame.
- Rift: Killing Bacte and Leech Supreme will magically transport Bits to your purse in the real world.
## ► Dungeons & Kuudra
Adventurer Saul
Adventurer Saul has arrived in the Dungeon Hub! With his guidance and permission, talented dungeon players can now unlock the ability to remove the party restrictions and enter the Catacombs on their own!
Auto Ready-Up
To make joining and playing Dungeons and Kuudra more streamlined, there is now an option in the Mort and Kuudra Gatekeeper menus, as well as the ready-up menus in the instances, to toggle auto readying up upon joining an instance.
Other Changes
- All players will now be healed back to full HP when they enter a boss portal in dungeons.
- Can now use Spirit Leap/InfiniLeap in Dungeon trap rooms.
- Renamed the Spirit Bow (Dungeons Floor 4 Drop) to the Spirit Shortbow.
- Improved Summoning Ring and Reaper Scythe's full messages.
- You now only have to attempt a slayer boss once before being able to see its stats from Maddox.
- Fixed Elle NPC and Supplies Stand not being left clickable in Kuudra
- Fixed Dungeon potions consumed during the run being removed when leaving and rejoining an instance
- Fixed Dungeon Start Pet Rule not working
- Fixed the Ballista Part Piles blocking shots in Kuudra
## ► Bazaar Changes
We have slightly redesigned the Bazaar to allow players to better understand where specific products are and certain information about them.
Products that have been updated
Added
- Ascension Rope
- Wishing Compass
Moved
- Gemstones -> After Hard Stone
- Forge -> Behind Dwarven Mines
- Magma Cream -> Combat
- Inferno Minion -> Oddities
- Goblin Eggs -> Mining
- Flower -> After Jungle Wood
- Lily Pads & Ink Sacks -> Fishing
- XP Bottles -> After Enchantments
- Crystal Fragments -> The End
Grouped
- Combined Glowstone, Netherrack, Quartz, Red Sand, Sulphur, and Mycelium -> Crimson Isle
- Renamed Slime Drops -> Slimeball
- Book Modifiers & Modifiers -> Modifiers
- Spooky, Gifts, Jerry Boxes, Refined Mineral, Mythological, Jacob's Tickets, Fishing Festival -> Events
- Summoning Eye, Dragon Fragments, & Draconic Sacrifice -> The End
- Low Reforge Stones & High Reforge Stones -> Reforge Stones
- Mushroom Soup & Metaphysical Serum -> Consumables
- Enchantments & Ultimate Enchantments -> Enchantments
- Revenant Horror, Tarantula Broodfather, Sven Packmaster, Voidgloom Seraph, Inferno Demonlord -> Slayer
The icon of grouped products have been updated to show the products inside, and if using Advanced Mode, they will show the Buy | Sell price on the front.
Instasell Blocklist
Added a way to prevent products from being bulk insta-sold in the Bazaar.
## ► Other Changes
Alongside the Crystal Hollow update, this patch includes an absolutely massive amount of other changes to the game.
Achievements
- Added 22 new Rift Achievements. Many of these are retroactive.
New SkyBlock Achievements
1. A Face to Remember [5 points] (Obtain Seraphine's old face in the Rift.)
2. Buzzkill [10 points] (Unlock all of Neuroscientist Kat's rewards in the Rift.)
3. Eye for an I [5 points] (Calm The Bankster in the Rift.)
4. He's Bac! [10 points] (Kill Bacte in the Rift.)
5. I'm Not Dead! [5 points] (Enter the Rift.)
6. Inner Machinations [15 points] (Max out the Enigma Cloak (including extra upgrades).)
7. It worked?! [5 points] (Retrieve a Rift-Transferrable item in the overworld.)
8. Long Way Down... [10 points] (Claim the Enigma soul at the top of the Beanstalk in the Rift.)
9. Long Way Gone [5 points] (Secure a Timecharm in the Rift Gallery)
10. Multi-Dimensional Slayer [15 points] (Defeat a T5 Riftstalker Bloodfiend.)
11. No Other Choice [10 points] (Solve the reverse murder case in the Rift.)
12. Not Worth It, Bro [5 points] (Have Wizardman decide not to help you.)
13. See ya later! [10 points] (Fully repair Reed's Boat in the Rift.)
14. Six-star Meal [5 points] (Consume a McGrubber's Burger.)
15. Snake Charmer [5 points] (Mine a Living Metal Snake in the Rift.)
16. Still Sad [15 points] (Talk to Sad Jacquelle after finding all 9 pieces of Montezuma's soul.)
17. Swaying the Vote [10 points] (Appease all of Barry's protestors in the Rift.)
18. The Crux of it All [15 points] (Max out all milestones on a Crux Chronomicon.)
19. Two/Nine [5 points] (Find two pieces of Montezuma's Soul.)
20. Yucky! [10 points] (Slay the Leech Supreme in the Rift.)
21. Secret Achievement [10 points] (???)
22. Secret Achievement [5 points] (???)
- Can now view your SkyBlock Achievements from within your SkyBlock menu.
Sacks
- Added chat notifications for items being added to and removed from Sacks. As a new player, it is unintuitive that items are automatically added to your Sacks and sometimes even removed. This change aims to improve feedback from these "invisible actions".
- Added Golden Carrots to the Agronomy Sack.
- Shortened the lore for some Enchanted Sacks.
Pets
- Added the Bejeweled Collar pet item
  - Grants +25 Mining Speed and +10 Mining Fortune
  - Can be forged in the Dwarven Mines for a Bejeweled Handle and Refined Mithril x4
- Revamped the Bee Pet's perks.
Abiphones - Do Not Disturb
- Added a way to prevent NPCs from calling you in Abiphone models XII Pro Giga and above.
Museum Changes
- Added Weirder Tuba to the Museum.
- Added the Blaze Hat, Sea Lantern Hat, and Magmafish Hat to the Museum.
- Shrunk the Museum zone slightly to make it easier to use abilities around the area.
Equipment Changes
Combat Equipment
- Improved the lore of the Jerry-chine Gun.
- Added a bulk-buy arrows option in the Weaponsmith shop.
- Added additional lore to the Archfiend Dice to hint that they are rigged.
- The Machine Gun Bow is now a shortbow and thus can fire instantly alongside its ability.
- The Machine Gun Shortbow ability can now be used outside of Dungeons.
Mining Equipment
- Slightly buffed the Fractured Mithril Pickaxe's mining speed and slightly nerfed the Diamond Pickaxe.
- Made the Dwarven Mines portal from the Portalizer require Mining 12 to use, in order to match with the Dwarven Mines requirement.
- Added crafting recipes for the Promising Shovel and Promising Pickaxe.
- Gave the Gold Forger NPC some shiny golden tools to sell.
- Gave the Gold Forger and Iron Forger NPCs some enchanted armor to sell.
- Added Promising Shovel to Rusty.
- Added more Efficiency levels to Promising Pickaxe and Axe, which now go up to 5 instead of previous 2.
- Blocks will now tell you how much breaking power is needed if you don't have enough.
- Metal Detector now shows the amount when finding powder in the Mines of Divan.
- Increased the accuracy of the Prehistoric Egg block count when walking at high speeds.
- Gave the Jungle Pickaxe Efficiency 5 by default.
Farming Equipment
- Reduced cooldown of Chicken Hat from 20->5s.
- Modified several Farming Fortune stats from various sources.
  - Mushroom Cow Base Stats Farming Fortune Scaling from 1 to 0.7 Farming Fortune per level
  - Elephant Pet Trunk Efficiency Perk from 1.8 to 1.5 Farming Fortune per level (150 max)
  - Anita’s “Extra Farming Drops” Perk to give +4 Farming Fortune per upgrade instead of +2
  - Cultivating Enchantment to give +2 Farming Fortune per level instead of +1
  - Burrowing Spores' farming fortune increased based on rarity
Misc. Equipment Change
- Moved the Grappling Hook to String Collection IV and made String VII give 20k Combat Exp.
- Potion Effects are no longer removed on death.
Quests and Collections
Slayer Quests and Collections
- Talking or Calling Maddox will now show all the Slayer Quests without needing to cancel the current quest.
- Players can now preview the Slayer menu from the Combat Skill menu.
- Tweaked some Sven Slayer VII and VIII rewards.
  - Sven 7 now unlocks hunter talisman recipe, overflux recipe, large slayer sack recipe
  - Sven 8 now unlocks hunter ring recipe, wolf fur mixin recipe, plasmaflux recipe
- A message will be sent after killing a boss 4 times in a row warning that killing it again won't give you any rewards.
- Updated messaging on Auto-Slayer.
Combat Quests and Collections
- Increased how often it rains in the Spider’s Den.
- Some zones now have recommended Combat levels and will warn you if you are venturing outside of your ability.
- Lowered individual price of Silent Pearls in the End from 1,250 to 150 coins.
- In the Crimson Isles, Pablo will now tell you what flower he wants when spoken to directly.
- Nerfed the Enchanted String recipe from 192 String -> 160 String for consistency with other enchanted items.
- Nerfed the cost of Enchanted Bone Meal by half not for consistency, but because it cost too much.
- Updated the description of the 'Flames' item to reference Cinderbats rather than Fire Bats.
- Now showing your crimson essence in the sidebar while in the Scarleton/Dragontail blacksmith zone.
- Added a dialogue to Captain Ahone's abiphone when his quest is completed.
- A message will be sent if players click on an already claimed relic in Spider's Den.
- Ice Walkers will now drop 8 coins and 8 XP orbs instead of absolutely nothing.
- Runic Rain and Emerald Slimes will now always drop slime runes.
- The Watcher's Wandering Souls will now use a player's nick skin instead of their real one.
- Enderstone Protector and Special Zealots will no longer despawn when Dragons die.
- New players will be prompted to talk to Tia after they find their first few fairy souls.
- Nerfed various enemies' stats:
  - Key Guardian - 1,000,000 -> 250,000 HP.
  - Squid - 1,500 -> 1,000 HP.
  - Sea Walker - 1,500 -> 1,000 HP.
  - Treasure Hoarder - 22,000 -> 15,000 HP. Decreased damage 750 -> 500.
  - Automaton 20,000 -> 15,000, reduced the frequency in which their laser beam could attack.
Mining Quests and Collections
- The cooldown of Emmett's Pointer has been lowered from 2->1s.
- Dwarven Mines raffles now despawn tickets when picking a winner.
- Added explanation of why Redstone Pigmen attack.
- Speaking to Don Expresso will now consume all of your tasty mithrils, allowing efficient event participation!
- Kalhuiki Tribe members won't attack you now if you’re wearing the Kalhuiki Mask.
- The Lapis Miner has wandered to a new spot in the Lapis Quarry.
- Golden Dragon Eggs will now show if pet candy has been used on them.
- The ‘Better Together’ mining range has been buffed from 10 blocks -> 20 blocks.
Foraging Quests and Collections
- Moved around items in the Acacia Log collection to be slightly nicer to early-game players.
- Improved the Carpenter's dialogue.
- Revamped the Lapis Miner's 'Helpful Miner' quest and added the Lapis Pickaxe.
Farming and the Garden Quests and Collections
- Increased crop regeneration speed in the Barn when more players are online.
- Added more of all Tree types to the Park.
- Renamed Tony's Shop and Talbot's Shop to Tony and Talbot for consistency.
- There is now a 2 coppers minimum reward from Garden visitors.
- Enchanted Red and Brown Mushrooms can now be crafted using blocks.
- Changed how Garden Crop Milestones works so different crops have different values.
- Changed Cabin Barn Skin to require Garden level 11 instead of 12.
- Decreased the Copper cost of Crop Upgrades and refunded the previously spent coins.
  - Level 8 now costs 2,000 instead of 5,000 coppers
  - Level 9 now costs 4,000 instead of 10,000 coppers
    - Refunds have been sent directly to your copper inventory.
Misc. Quests and Collections
- Improved Sirius' dialogue to better explain the Dark Auction.
- Improved various Area Discovery chat messages.
- Added a miscellaneous Fairy Soul breakdown.
- Prettified the Melody tip button.
- Improved some of Juliette's dialogue.
- The North Stars gained message is now hoverable.
- Clicking on a Trades unlock (for example, from collections) will now open the trades menu.
- Calling Tia the Fairy after completing all 9f operator relays through the Abiphone will now open her menu.
- Removed Piscary from Experimentation Table.
- The coins rewarded for Skill level ups has been greatly increased.
- You now must be SkyBlock Level 10 to participate in a Bingo event.
User Interface and Messaging
- Added the on/off toggle from the Personal Deletor to the Personal Compactor.
- Added a confirmation menu to the /coopadd menu that explains the consequences of adding someone to your co-op. All players on the co-op now have to read this menu and press a separate confirm button before voting on if the new player can be added.
- Added a new /coopclear command that will clear your pending /coop and /coopadd invites.
- North Stars are now displayed in Einary's menu.
- Updated slowness potion stat values on player targets (in case you drink one...).
- The stats breakdown menu will now let you see why you have 0 of a stat rather than saying, "You have none of this stat!"
- Edited messaging on Jax dialogue.
- Added a confirmation menu to pet candy / Caducous Feeder.
- Reworded the "More Farming Drops" perk in Anita to Farming Fortune.
- Added a salvage confirmation to Ice Spray Wand.
- Improved the salvage message; you may now hover it to see what you obtained from the salvage.
- Changed chat message color when disabling Heart of the Mountain perks from yellow to red.
- Kat will now reject you if you try to upgrade a pet that holds a Tier Boost.
- The paste menu in Garden will now show how many resources you need and how much it'll take from your inventory.
- Pet Candy used on a pet will only be hidden if the pet tier is Legendary or higher.
## ► Bug Fixes
User Interface and Inventories
- /storage now opens your Enderchest in the Rift
- Fixed the Hex displaying incorrect enchant lore for enchants with a minimum level of above 1.
- Removed the Enchanting Skill's access to the Wizard Portal because it did approximately nothing.
- Fixed the 'Buy a Stack!' button in Bazaar being inaccurate when buying products with a max stack size of 16.
- Fixed sacks having the incorrect cap when accessed from inside a chest.
- Fixed being able to complete the Talk to Guide objective while talking to a non-existent NPC.
- Fixed items taken from minion chests not going directly into Sacks.
- Fixed sacks & bags message saying you are spectating.
- Fixed Spooky still showing leaderboard positions in menus.
- Fixed Festive Altruist achievement not increasing.
- Fixed Coal Blocks not being compacted.
- Fixed Coop travel notifications not working.
- Fixed Rat Pet skins not updating immediately when applied.
- Fixed Rollim/Kaus calling people waaay too much.
- "Insert Inventory" button in sacks will now take sacks from your inventory into account.
- Fixed formatting of stitched sacks.
- Fixed not being able to put soulbound items in the rune removal menu.
- Fixed Dctr. Paper not being usable to brew Adrenaline potions.
- Added back the ability to sell items to the Wool Weaver and Builder NPCs.
- Fixed the “Go Back” button in the /sacks menu.
Mining Content Fixes
- Fixed the warp to the Rampart Quarry during the Goblin Raid event throwing you into the void.
- Fixed Emissary Braum never completing its objective in the Dwarven Mines.
- Fixed many spots in the Dwarven Mines where blocks would unexpectedly yield gold ores.
- Fixed bugging out Murderlovers by preventing them from attacking Superprotectrons and other goblins altogether.
- Fixed bug where god potion effects were wiped when drinking near the Jungle Temple.
Bingo Fixes
- Fixed a missing number in the Collect Essence Bingo Community Goal.
- Fixed an exploit with the Spider Queen Bingo Goal.
Combat Content Fixes
- Fixed rewarding Crimson miniboss kill credit to minibosses that didn't actually die.
- Fixed Voidgloom Seraphs' Yang Glyphs not returning the block it replaced to the correct block.
  - Made it easier to break Glyphs that are at the player's eye height.
- Fixed blazes and ghast minions having terrible aim.
- Fixed explosive arrows not working when you reach 10 stacks of Hydra Strike on your Terror armor.
- Fixed Jerry Boxes displaying the incorrect amount of XP given.
- Fixed some very rare drops showing inaccurate drop rates in the Bestiary.
- Fixed obtaining Bedrock from diggable snow in the Rift.
- Fixed each Wisp Pet counting towards SkyBlock Exp.
- Fixed getting miniboss loot while inside the dojo in the Crimson Isle.
- Fixed Inferno Minion's Apex Minion perk not working while offline.
- Fixed Gyrokinetic Wand's cells alignment changing true damage into regular damage.
- Fixed the text on Ragnarock Axe in regards to its duration.
- Fixed Shen refunds counting towards lifetime motes earned stat in the Rift.
- Fixed summoning eyes not properly appearing in inventory on the client-side when killing zealots using item abilities.
- Fixed being unable to claim dojo rewards if you skip or lose a belt item.
- Fixed United in Blood achievement being unobtainable.
- Fixed an issue where the Dungeon Party Finder would not remember dungeon type selection of "Any".
- Fixed an issue where uncovering a Minos Inquisitor in the Mythological Ritual event would send a chat message for Minos Champion.
- The person who digs up a Burrow in the Mythological Ritual event will get the kill credit for the dug up enemy, even if someone else kills it.
- Fixed an issue where the Minotaur's axes would block hits.
- Fixed getting invalid Ender Pearls from dragon fights.
- Fixed a bug where the Beastmaster Crest did not increase kills when in the Accessory Bag.
- Fixed a sword-swap exploit with the Soul Whip.
- Fixed a visual bug when clicking in the menu slot where the Kismet Feather re-roll option should be.
- Fixed Redstone Warrior abilities and kill messages saying Lost Adventurer.
- Fixed Yeti Sword ability sometimes visually removing blocks but not really (clientside visual error)
- Fresh Tools Kuudra perk can now work on any Ballista build pile when proc’ed.
- Fixed shiny variants of gear and dungeon discs not being dropped in F7/M7 Catacombs.
Enchanting Content Fixes
- Fixed bug where combining max level enchants would sometimes lower the enchantment level.
- Fixed bug where Tabasco enchant wasn't taking XP levels.
Farming Content Fixes
- Fixed Cocoa Bean Jacob contests not having a Personal Best entry in the Jacob menu.
- Fixed some cactus and sand locations in the Farming Islands.
- Fixed Mooshroom Cow's "Mushroom Eater" working on player-placed crops (for real).
Fishing Content Fixes
- Fixed not getting Trophy Fish credit for getting Obfuscated 1's from Corrupt Sea Creatures.
- Fixed being able to decrease Mushed Glowy Tonic's duration.
- Fixed Lump of Magma getting deleted when clicking water.
- Fixed an issue where the wrong player was given a Dolphin pet on certain milestones.
- Fixed Depths Strider sometimes breaking when the armor piece had a rune assigned.
- Fixed InfiniDirtWand not working on Sea Lanterns.
Foraging Content Fixes
- Fixed some portals in The Park putting the player in a block.
- Fixed some trees in The Park not fully regenerating.
- Fixed area in Hub near Lumber Jack dropping rails and ladders.
- Fixed Enchanted Bone Meal consuming double when right clicking blocks.
Misc. Fixes
- Fixed being teleported back when running on soil on newer versions.
- Fixed multiple typos and grammatical errors around the game.
- Fixed a bug where a visiting player would see the RB ranks icon, but the island member would not.
- Fixed the Monster Raider armor not being donatable to the Museum.
- Fixed a duplication glitch with the Parkour Times block.
- Fixed a bunch of chat inconsistencies in the Rift.
  - Note that due to the nature of the Rift, inconsistencies are sometimes intentional.
    - Note that due to the nature of the rift, the rift is an inconsistency
      - Note that due to the ǹ̵̺͖̖̮̙̗̣̜̯̘̹̗͔̬̻̿̓a̴̢̧̭̭̜̩̖̗̳̙̟̠̹͎̎͂̄̍̔̄̾͂̐͊͂̀͗̕̚t̷̨̫̻̲̣̰̊̈́͆u̷̱̯͎̩͉̥̼͖̠͔̦͋̍͐̇̍̾͜͠r̸̨̧̡͔̭̦̝̰͖͎̖̜͍͊̎̓̏͋̄̀͊̈͝ề̶͙̞̗͈̻̻̍̋͘ͅ ̸̢̮̭̙͚̬̺͉̭̮͎͎̘͇́̿̓ǫ̸̱͚̱͔͎̯͇͘͜f̷̩̺̮̖̠̤̌̿̀̋̾̑̎̒͒̚̚ͅ ̷̛̦̻̹̞͚͆͌̂̕͝r̷͎̪̩̱͖̦̠̦̂͌̓̓͒̆̒̌̒̑͑̕͠í̶̪͚͉͋͂̅̐̃͛̅̍̌͌̄̂͝f̴̨̢̬͍̹͇͚̔̈́̕ṱ̷͔̲̊̒͒͠͝ ̵̟̲̮͇̥͙̀̎̔̔-̶̯̗̳̳̝̠͒͊̓͑͛͘͠-̶̨̛̻̭̥̫̤͔̍̅̎͂̎͂͘-̶̢͔̰͔͗̈̎̆́̈́̐̊̒̌̽̇̾͠-̷̢̧̧͔̬͍͔̟͕̩͛̕͝-̵̜́̀͗̇́̅̎̐͜-̴̢̢͎̲͓̹̤̝̮̯̹͙̟͖̙̊̈́̎͋̈́̿̄̎̽-̴͈̘͖̽̔̈́̆̂̉̆̂͝͝͝-̸͙̳͖̔͌̈́͒̓̈͗̏̕͠-̷̮͈̘̩̐̌͊̍̂͒̊͌̒͌͊̔̾͘̕ͅ-̵̢̝̱͇͙̮̬̰̠̦͙̳͚̭̰̂̓̊̀̈́̑̔͒̈́̒̔̚̚͝-̷̢̣͖̤̱̪͈̝̭̦͙͑͗-̸̡̡̢͎̙̝͍̤̳̠͎̖̘̞͉͂̀͛̀̃̈̈́̔͂̎͒̆̄͌̽-̷̖̝̜̻͓̦͔̰͂͒͊͌̂̿̃̎̌̓-̷̤̣̞͎̝̦̩̦̠̝͔̈́͊͌̎͒̒̇̕̕ͅ-̴̧̱̗̭̮͈̳̙̥̤̮̣͕͚͐͐̓̍̚ͅ-̵̨̡͎̪̲͚̱̙͚̼̟͓͉̟͊̔͑́̃̓͌̓̔̓̕͝͠͝-̸̦͈̯̹͔̙̲͍̭̞̀̈́̀̈̽͑͂͆͠͠-̴͓̩͍̹̳̒͋̿́͠ͅͅ-̴̡̛̦̗͙͚̙̦̏̍͌̄́͗̂͂̋̊͜͝͝͝-̸̟̒̇̎̎̊̈́͒͘͠ͅ-̸̳͇̃̉͑́̒͗́̀̂̃̅̕͝-̴̡̳͔̹̮̟̭̩́̿͗̐̎͑̚͜͝
- Fixed a visual bug when clicking in the menu slot where the Kismet Feather re-roll option should be.

# Changelog/2023/August 24

[August 24] SkyBlock Patch Notes
Updated: August 24, 2023
Hey all!
We are here to inform you about the latest update that was just rolled to SkyBlock!
- Fixed a couple more cases where Bestiary progress and Combat / Fishing EXP was not being awarded correctly.
- Fixed multiple bugs where Mana cost in item descriptions did not take into account Ultimate Wise enchantment.
- Fixed multiple bugs where Mana cost was not being reduced correctly by Ultimate Wise enchantment.
- Fixed barriers from Wither Cloak Sword not respecting Ultimate Wise enchantment when getting hit while sword is not held.
- Fixed Werewolf armor not applying the cap correctly.
- Fixed a bunch of cases where players are tagged as being in combat when they shouldn't.
- Fixed Automatons being able to deal damage for a short period of time after dying.
- Fixed Fishing Hook not despawning when trying to fish in water with lava rods.
- Fixed a few issues with being unable to use an Anvil UI (e.g. renaming Garden plots) when having no levels and/or playing on newer versions of Minecraft.
- Fixed Champion enchantment increasing Fishing EXP gain.
- Fixed a way of rod swapping.
- Fixed some typos.
If you have a bug to report to us, please make a report HERE! Thanks to everyone who has reported bugs!

# Changelog/2023/August 3
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[August 3rd] SkyBlock Patch Notes
Updated: August 3, 2023
Hi all,
Today we conducted some maintenance to the backend of Dungeons and Kuudra to migrate them over to a new unified system. This new system fixes a lot of the inconsistencies between the two instanced modes and contains many under-the-hood improvements, bug fixes and QOL changes:
System Changes
- Dungeons and Kuudra can now be re-joined directly if you accidently leave SkyBlock by simply re-joining SkyBlock.
  - If you leave to another island within SkyBlock, you will still need to be party warped back in.
- The party leader can now instantly requeue a Dungeons or Kuudra instance when it has finished by clicking on the prompt in chat displayed after the end of the instance.
  - This should remove the need for players to re-party after each completion.
- The queuing system will now retry up to 3 times to queue you into an instance upon error or generation failure.
  - This change should help prevent players getting locked out of an instance due to queuing cooldowns.
- Some menus and inventories that were previously disabled in Kuudra after the Rift update have been re-enabled.
- Players will now respawn with 3 seconds of invulnerability after being revived in Dungeons and Kuudra.
- Players will no longer take damage before a Dungeon or Kuudra fight has started or after they have ended.
Other Changes
- The Archer Ghost Stun Bow ability now actually works in Dungeons.
- The Watcher mob spawning animations in the Blood Room have been sped up by about 25%.
- Telekinesis is now no longer disabled in Dungeons.
- Additional checks have been put in to prevent players from skipping directly to the boss fight ("blood skipping").
If you have a bug to report to us, please make a report HERE! Thanks to everyone who has reported bugs!
Please note: Solo Dungeons will be coming very soon. Also, we are still tweaking and making improvements to this system, and are still monitoring the forums for feedback on the changes made.

# Changelog/2023/December 12
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
0.19.9
SkyBlock Patch Notes 0.19.9 - Pet Training and Bestiary Changes
Updated: December 12, 2023
Hello! Welcome to SkyBlock Patch 0.19.9. Today's update includes [[Pet Care]], some [[Bestiary]] improvements, new items, and more!
## ► The Pet Trainer
[[Fann|Hootie]] and [[Fann]] are the new duo in town! While Kat allows you to upgrade your Pets to a higher rarity, Fann can train your Pets in her playground, giving them EXP!
### How does it work?
Start by choosing a training slot. Initially, you have access to one slot, but by treating Hootie and Fann’s Owl to candies, you unlock two additional slots.
After selecting a slot, you can donate any pet you own, and then you have to select a few options:
- The Training session
  - Pick from six unique training modes, each offering increasing amounts of Pet EXP daily.
  - Higher-level training costs more.
  - The first training mode is free, and training sessions are unlocked every 10 Taming Levels.
  - The free training session gets better and better the higher your taming level, from 1000 exp per day to 5000!
- Set the duration
  - Opt for a fixed period (5/10/20 days) or a target level (level 100, for example).
- You can also use an Owl Pet to help in the training session!
  - Your Owl Pet also levels up while helping out in the training session.
- You can use the Caducous Feeder on your pet while it is in a training session!
### The [[Owl Pet]]
Fann’s passion for Owls shines through in her work. She also offers the Owl Pet for sale!
The more money you invest on Fann’s training sessions, the cheaper they will get.
Also, you can always donate your Owl in any training sessions, and your cute Owl will jump in the playground with your Pet to help train it.
### Taming 60
[[George]] is a Pet collector facing a unique challenge: certain elusive pets elude his collection, as no one is willing to sell them to him.
Once you reach [[Taming]] Level 50, George proposes a trade. By donating 10 unique Pets, you unlock additional Taming Levels, all the way up to 60.
## ► Taylor’s Shop
[[Taylor]]’s shop has received a cool new look just in time for winter festivities.
Celebrating the holiday spirit, Taylor unveils a Seasonal Cosmetic Shop in the Hub featuring season-themed skins, with a rotating selection throughout the year.
The Winter Collection debuts with:
- Snow Owl Pet Skin (650 Gems)
- Frozen Jerry Pet Skin (650 Gems)
- Iceberg Frozen Blaze Helmet (650 Gems)
- Christmas Stockings Backpack Skin (350 Gems)
- Sack of Coal Backpack Skin (350 Gems)
- Ice Skates Rune (350 Gems)
- Polar Lights Flux Skin (1350 Gems)
- Town Hall Barn Skin (1350 Gems)
- Winter Homestead Barn Skin (650 Gems)
Available until Winter’s end!
## ► Bestiary Improvements
### New Features and Changes
- There is now a reminder if you have [[Bestiary]] Milestones that you have not claimed yet. This reminder shows up whenever you swap islands
  - You can toggle this reminder off in the Bestiary menu.
- [[Kada Knight]] and Kada Pigman now have distinct Bestiary categories.
- The amount of kills needed to max Bestiary has been adjusted for the following mobs:
  - [[Wither Skeleton]] dropped from 10,000 to 3,000.
  - Dungeon Minibosses except [[King Midas]] ([[Lost Adventurer]], [[Angry Archaeologist]], [[Shadow Assassin]]) dropped from 10,000 to 3,000 each.
  - [[King Midas]] dropped from 1,000 to 750.
  - [[Zealot]] dropped from 100,000 to 25,000.
  - [[The Watcher|Watcher]] and [[Obsidian Defender]] dropped from 25,000 to 10,000.
  - Berserker Imp dropped from 10,000 to 3,000.
  - [[Silverfish (disambiguation)|Silverfish]] dropped from 40,000 to 10,000.
  - [[Fels]] and [[Zombie Knight]] dropped from 25,000 to 10,000.
  - [[Voracious Spider]] dropped from 40,000 to 10,000.
  - [[Spider Jockey]] dropped from 10,000 to 4,000.
- [[Reindrake]] now rewards Bestiary progress to anyone who has damaged the drake at least once.
- The amount of kills required to max each mob’s bestiary is hidden by default. You can toggle it on in the bestiary menu.
- You can now click the center icon in the Bestiary menu to access the Hypixel Wiki Bestiary page.
  - A prompt will appear in chat. You will need to click that prompt to be redirected to the Hypixel Wiki.
- Bestiary won't be available until you speak to [[Bramass Beastslayer]].
  - Some players may have to interact again with [[Bramass Beastslayer]] to re-unlock their Bestiary.
### Stranded Bestiary
We’ve officially added support to the Bestiary in [[Stranded]]! Only mobs that can spawn on Stranded will appear in Stranded’s bestiary. There are also some slight discrepancies in the amount of kills needed to max each mob’s bestiary between Stranded and non-Stranded.
To celebrate this momentous occasion, we’ve also added a bunch of new spawn conditions while also revamping some of the existing spawn conditions. Check them out on the Hypixel Wiki [[Stranded/Mobs|here]]!
### Bug Fixes
- Fixed Minion Mobs giving Bestiary progress.
- Fixed the description of [[Minos Inquisitor]]’s Chimera line having a line break.
- Fixed Berserker Imps not awarding Bestiary progress under certain conditions.
- Fixed Stranded [[Broodmother]] having the incorrect damage cap.
- Fixed [[Grandma Wolf]] having outdated dialogue regarding the amount of Bestiary Milestones required to upgrade the [[Grandma Wolf Pet]].
- Bestiary messages won't be shown until you speak to [[Bramass Beastslayer]].
## ► Other Changes
### Secret Tracker 3000
[[Malik]] now features the [[Secret Tracker 3000]] in the shop. Right click the Secret Tracker 3000 to display a particle trail towards the nearest secret. The compass needle also points towards the nearest secret as detected on right click. You can buy the Secret Tracker 3000 in Malik’s Wither Essence shop.
### Museum Search
We’ve introduced a search function for your [[Museum]] items! This should make it much easier for everyone to find their items in the Museum instead of having to navigate through pages of irrelevant items. The “Museum Browser” has been relocated to the left of the back button.
### Island Properties Menu
Long gone are the days where you’d need to scavenge to find that pesky hidden repelling candle or go on a hunt for that day saver you placed a decade ago.
drum roll…
Introducing the Island Properties menu! This removes the need to have each block placed on your island and instead makes them all available in one menu! Players who had these blocks already placed on their island will have this feature unlocked automatically upon login. Accessible from [[Jerry]]’s menu Introducing the Island Properties menu which allows for remote access to the following features:
- [[Day Saver]]/[[Night Saver]] (revamped menu)
- [[Repelling Candle]]
- [[Silence Block]]
- [[Ice Cube]] (NEW!)
- [[Weather Node]]
- Revamped biome select menu
### Worm Bait
Alert all [[Stoneworm|Worm]] enthusiasts - we’re introducing a [[Worm Bait]]! Only worms will bite when you’re using Worm Bait. You also get a whopping +60 Fishing speed. Do note that this bait can only be used in the Crystal Hollows. Enjoy!
### [[Glowing Mush Mixin]]
You no longer have to drink seventeen [[Mushed Glowy Tonic Potion|Mushed Glowy Tonic Potions]] per fishing session with this new Mixin available at Moby. Add this mixin to your god potion by brewing it with the mixin as an ingredient!
### Ice Cube
[[Ice Cube]] can be consumed at the /islandproperties menu to toggle ice melting on your island. The recipe for Ice Cube is unlocked at Ice Collection VII.
## ► General Changes
### Combat Changes
- Added +5 Dragon Essence reward for killing [[Ender Dragon|Dragons]] in [[The End]], doubled for Superior Dragons.
### Mining Changes
- Added a new warning to the Mithril Gourmand event when there are 20 seconds left.
- Added [[Biofuel]] and [[Oil Barrel]] to Dwarven Sack.
- Added better explanations to each [[Crystal Hollows]] [[Commissions]].
- Added [[Refined Mineral]] to Mining Sacks.
- Fixed taking fall damage on some launch pads in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
- Removed Flawless Gemstones from the Gemstone Sack.
### Fishing Changes
- Added [[Agarimoo Tongue]] to the [[Fishing Sack]].
- Renamed the [[Abyssal Miner|Zombie Miner]] (Fishing Mob from the Crystal Hollows) to [[Abyssal Miner]].
### Misc. Changes
- Added a button to toggle all potion effects in potion toggle menu.
- Added a "No Power" selection to [[Maxwell]].
- Added a shortcut to the [[Quiver]] inside of [[Jax]]'s menu.
- Added an AH search in the [[Museum]] for not found items if you have a [[Booster Cookie]].
  - Clicking an item slot in the museum that you have not donated before will trigger an auction house search for that item!
- Added [[Egg]], [[Magma Core]], [[Guardian Lucky Block]] and [[Jungle Heart]] to the Bazaar.
- Added Jack o' Lanterns to [[Builder]]'s shop NPC.
- Added /bank command as an [[Emerald]] Collection unlock.
- [[Block Zapper]] cooldown is now stored in the profile and maintained over multiple servers.
  - Removed fatigue from [[Block Zapper]] and [[Builder's Ruler]].
- Changed the suggested amount of [[Metaphysical Serum]] from 4 to 3 in the Bazaar when instabuying / creating buy order.
- Dungeon items with a 50/50 stat boost will now yield +10 bonus essence when salvaged.
- [[Ice Minion|Ice Minions]] and [[Snow Minion|Snow Minions]] now tell you to unlock Tier 12 at [[Einary]] in [[Jerry's Workshop]].
- [[Lucius]]' dialogue now shows you how many purchases you've made.
- [[Mithril Infusion]] may now be applied to minions directly in the minion menu if you have an infusion in your inventory.
- Mobs will now only randomly spawn in the [[Mythological Ritual]] for players which are combat level 10 or higher.
- Overflow Pet XP is now shown on Max Level pets.
- Ranked Bundles will now be sent to stash if you don't have space in your inventory.
- Reduced the price of the Tuning Templates from 30k to 10k bits. Any player who purchased these addons can get the difference refunded in the Stats Tuning menu (where they bought the templates).
- Reworked the /collection command so it allows searching for more collections (including bosses).
- The achievements associated with killing the [[Broodmother|Brood Mother]], [[Magma Boss]], [[Yeti]], [[Grinch]] and [[Vanquisher]] all now only require a 5% max hp assist instead of the final blow to unlock.
- The [[Builder]] will now immediately open his menu when called through the [[Abiphones|Abiphone]].
- The EXP required for next level of your current equipped pet can now be viewed when hovering over the “Pets” button in the SkyBlock Menu.
- The Explorer achievement no longer requires going inside Shen's Regalia Room.
- The rarity of books found in the experimentation table is now labelled:
### User Interface and Messaging Changes
- Added an empty line of space in Master Skull accessories.
- Added new dialogue when you click on a relic before talking to the Archaeologist.
- Added reminder to claim Ranked Bundles.
- Clicking an item in your inventory while the collections menu is open will now open its relevant collection (including recipe outputs).
- Fixed a mistake with the Acacia Birdhouse description in the Power Stones Guide.
- Fixed some formatting inconsistencies with Quest Items.
- Fixed up bazaar order tooltip to still show the co-op member who created the order even if it's filled.
- Formatted Draconic accessories consistently.
- Improved Jerry Island Delivery claiming message.
- Improved the Fire Sale message.
  - You can now click the Fire Sale message to warp to Elizabeth!
- Removed duplicate text for 'The highest tier for this minion has been reached'.
- The text from interacting with a Dark Monolith now follows the standard item reward message format.
- The Metal Detector now mentions the amount of powder found when used in the Mines of Divan.
- The post-Bingo summary message will no longer display if you did not participate in that Bingo.
- Updated the Reforge Unlock tooltip on skill level up to take Powers into account.
## ► Bug Fixes
### Item Fixes
- Fixed a grammar error in the Fruit Bowl lore.
- Fixed a typo with Pest Repellent.
- Fixed a typo in the name of Enchanted Cocoa Beans.
- Fixed being able to remove enchants from items with built-in enchantments (e.g Fancy Sword).
- Fixed Bottle of Jyrre sometimes gaining Intelligence faster than intended.
- Fixed Dungeon Orb displaying a chat message when holding it and stepping on a Pressure Plate.
- Fixed Enchanted Glowstone NPC sell price being too high (was not adjusted with the previous Enchanted Glowstone recipe change).
- Fixed Fire Talisman not working during the Sadan boss fight.
- Fixed Firework Rockets not having a sell price.
- Fixed Ice Spray being included when selecting the “Insert all Rare Items” option in the mass salvage menu.
- Fixed Lotus Pieces having a wrong description after maxing them.
- Fixed misleading description on the Refined reforge when used with the Pioneer Pickaxe.
- Fixed Nutcracker Armor not stacking Magic Find and Defense from other players correctly.
- Fixed Personal Deletor not deleting seeds dropped from the Rookie Hoe.
- Fixed Pet Cakes taking the first valid item from the inventory instead of the item you’re holding.
- Fixed Recombobulated Frosty the Snow Cannon having the wrong name colour in chat messages.
- Fixed sitting on furniture breaking Crystal Armor.
- Fixed Snow Suit not being picked up by the Salvage by Rarity button in the salvaging menu.
- Fixed Super Compactor 3000 not crafting Whipped Magma Cream when paired with a Magma Cube Minion.
- Fixed Tarantula Helmet's crit chance being unreliable.
- Fixed the Personal Deletor calling itself the Personal Compactor.
- Fixed Tier XIII (13) Perfect Armor not having particles.
- Fixed Wither Cloak consuming mana from fire tick even with the Fire Talisman equipped.
- Fixed Wither Cloak consuming mana when being hit by other players.
### Combat Fixes
- Fixed /eq not being affected by combat tag.
- Fixed Bladesoul's skull explosion FX carrying over post-mortem of the player.
- Fixed Cave Spiders not counting towards Spider Slayer spawning.
- Fixed Fire Eels and Rats counting towards Slayer Progress.
- Fixed incorrect wording in Spider Slayer Boss ability description.
- Fixed specific mob axes not dropping the correct amount of loot if the mob is corrupted (e.g Pig Axe).
- Fixed Toxic Arrow Poison drops from the Taranatula Broodfather sometimes not displaying the correct item amounts in the chat message.
### Mining Fixes
- Fixed being unable to use abilities in the Lost Precursor City.
- Fixed Crystal Nucleus dropping more Flawless Amethyst Gemstones than the rest of Gemstones (4 instead of 1).
- Fixed destroying Mithril blocks with Bob-ombs not dropping Mithril.
- Fixed Fetchur dialogue typo.
- Fixed items obtained from Treasure Hunter chests not directly going into sacks.
- Fixed Jungle Temple preventing the application of Speed and Jump Boost related effects from a God Potion, even after leaving the temple.
- Fixed Mining Events giving HOTM experience at max HOTM level.
- Fixed player-placed blocks respawning on a 5-minute timer in Mines of Divans.
- Fixed the Efficient Miner Perk not working on Mithril blocks that have different block materials.
### Farming Fixes
- Fixed a collection exploit regarding double plants.
- Fixed a typo when Garden Milestones are maxed.
- Fixed being able to bypass the speed cap with a Black Cat and Rancher's Boots.
- Fixed being able to gain Garden Cleanup Progress by placing Redstone Materials on/near Tall Grass.
- Fixed being able to paste Garden plots with garden materials without the required SkyBlock Level to use the bazaar.
- Fixed being unable to harvest potatoes in a certain area in the Barn.
- Fixed the Visitor’s Logbook referring to a single requirement as requirements.
### Foraging Fixes
- Fixed being able to break the entrance frames in The Park.
- Fixed some log piles being unbreakable in the Hub's Forest.
- Fixed the Foraging Recipes Icon showing that you are missing a Recipe when you have them all Unlocked.
### Pet Fixes
- Fixed Autopets triggering pet spawning while in places where pets are disabled.
- Fixed being able to get certain pets into the Dojo.
- Fixed Eerie Pet temporarily losing hologram when upgraded.
- Fixed Montezuma pet always showing 'Click to show' regardless of visibility status.
- Fixed Pet Sorting options not saving when switching servers.
- Fixed the Hologram from the Dolphin Pet "Splash Surprise" Ability preventing clicks.
- Fixed the Oringo’s Unique Pets requirement in Stranded requiring 1 more pet than displayed.
- Fixed Rat pet generating tasty cheese in Gardens.
### Minion Fixes
- Fixed the Bunny minion skin showing the wrong skin for tiers 10, 11 and 12 .
- Fixed Mithril Infusion not showing in crafting preview.
- Fixed Mithril Infusion getting wiped when upgrading a Revenant Minion to XII in the Bartender.
- Fixed Snow Minions not being affected by Mining Crystals.
- Fixed Snow Minions received from Gifts always displaying the chat message saying it's a new Minion.
### Misc. Fixes
- Fixed an issue with dragging/interacting with items over the 6th slot in inventories.
- Fixed being able to bid past AH limit.
- Fixed being able to bypass requirements for unlocking an NPC as an Abiphone contact if they visit your Garden.
- Fixed being able to have negative Soup flight durations or being able to go over the integer limit.
- Fixed being able to place NPC relocate eggs over the void.
- Fixed being able to use Human Cannonball while mounted on a cannon in the Kuudra fight.
- Fixed being able to /coopsalvage the free booster cookie.
- Fixed being unable to complete the "Talk to the Auction Master" objective on Ironman.
- Fixed dying in the Mushroom Desert sending players back to The Barn.
- Fixed eating a Jerry Candy with a God Potion active decreasing the duration.
- Fixed formatted profile age being inaccurate.
- Fixed Free Chest Kuudra Essence reward amount being incorrect at random.
- Fixed God Potions being wiped when warping out of Kuudra's Hollow as a ghost.
- Fixed inactive co-op profiles not being kickable due to data being "not available right now".
- Fixed items in the 2x2 crafting grid being wiped when swapping servers.
- Fixed Items purchased from NPCs not stacking.
- Fixed Kat calling you about a finished pet after you gave her a Kat Flower.
- Fixed not being able to assign Island Ranks to offline players.

# Changelog/2023/December 13
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[December 13] Fann Day One Patch
Updated: December 13, 2023
Hey all!
We are here to inform you about the latest update that was just rolled to SkyBlock!
[[Fann]]'s Training Sessions:
We are changing the scaling of the Fann's Pet Training as follows:
- Free Training
  - 1,000-5,000 -> 20,000-40,000 EXP/day (scales with Taming level)
- Light Training (Taming 20)
  - 10,000 -> 125,000 EXP/day
  - Now costs 200,000 coins/day (1.6 coins per EXP)
- Moderate Training (Taming 30)
  - 40,000 -> 500,000 EXP/day
  - Now costs 1,000,000 coins/day (2 coins per EXP)
- Expert Training (Taming 40)
  - 160,000 -> 750,000 EXP/day
  - Now costs 2,000,000 coins/day (2.67 coins per EXP)
- Ultra Training (Taming 50)
  - 900,000 -> 1,000,000 EXP/day
  - Now costs 2,750,000 coins/day (2.75 coins per EXP)
- Turbo! Training (Taming 60)
  - 1,275,000 -> 1,500,000 EXP/day
  - Cost remains at 3,000,000 coins & 500 bits per day (2 coins per EXP)
Why have these changes been made?
- The new EXP values will help you reach your intended level a little faster than you were able to before.
- For the cheaper plans, it will still take ages to level your pet to 100. If that’s your goal, you’re much better off using the more expensive options. The cheaper plans are not intended to be used to level your pet to 100. You could do it, but don’t expect to see your pet maxed out any time soon.
- So what are the cheaper plans for? Cheaper plans are more efficient in terms of coin to EXP cost. By putting a pet in the Light plan for a day, I’m getting 1 EXP per 1.6 coins, compared to 1 EXP per 2 coins in the Moderate plan.
- The prices are generally set based on Carrot Candy prices. Great Carrot Candy has a market price of around 200k coins, grants 100,000 Pet EXP, and everyone’s favorite “Pet Candy (1/10)” description. The Light plan costs 200k coins, gives 125,000 Pet EXP (25% more than the [[Great Carrot Candy]]), and doesn’t add “Pet Candy (1/10)” to your pet, with the opportunity cost of you having to wait a day to get your pet back. If you want some cheap, passive pet EXP for your pet, the lower-tier training plans are an option.
Other Changes
- You will now be refunded the cost difference equivalent of the plan’s cost per EXP when using [[Ultimate Carrot Candy]] through [[Caducous Feeder]] on your pet during a training session.
  - In order to use a [[Caducous Feeder]] during a session, go find your pet in the playground upstairs during their training and right-click them!
- The Confirm Training button now more accurately displays where your pet will be when using the Day Training duration.
- Added an option to train for 1 Day.
If you have a bug to report to us, please make a report HERE! Thanks to everyone who has reported bugs!
Please note: We are always balancing features based on player feedback.

# Changelog/2023/December 15
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[December 15] SkyBlock Patch Notes
Updated: December 15, 2023
Hey all!
We are here to inform you about some small changes that we have rolled or are currently rolling.
- Reverted the /equipment and /eq commands being affected by the Combat Tag timer.
- Reduced the price of giving a [[Rift Ferret Pet]] to [[George]] from 500k to 50k.
- [[Magic Find]] no longer affects [[Loot Sharing|Loot Share]] drops that the mob owner does not roll Magic Find for.
  - For example, a [[Great White Shark Tooth]] has a 10% chance to drop from a [[Great White Shark]]. Magic Find will only affect drops with less than a 5% drop rate. Before this update, Magic Find would buff the chance to get a loot-shared Great White Shark Tooth (2% chance to drop after Loot Share) when the mob spawner's loot roll would not affected by Magic Find (10%). This will no longer happen.
  - Another example, the Epic [[Baby Yeti Pet]] has a 3% chance to drop from a [[Yeti]]. Because this loot drop is affected by Magic Find for the mob spawner (3%), Magic Find will also affect the Loot Share chance of 0.6%.
- Fixed Magic Find displaying the killer's Magic Find in chat instead of your own Magic Find.
  - Contrary to popular belief, the system always took your own Magic Find.
  - This Magic Find value is affected by your individual [[Bestiary]] mob levels for the Magic Find bonus.
If you have a bug to report to us, please make a report HERE! Thanks to everyone who has reported bugs!
Please note: Some of these changes are slowly rolling out and may take up to 12 hours to be fully deployed.

# Changelog/2023/February 14
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
0.18
SkyBlock Patch 0.18 - The Garden
Updated: February 14, 2023
Hello,
Today we are excited to release a new SkyBlock farming island called The Garden! The Garden is an extension of your Private Island, unlocked after reaching SkyBlock Level 5, where you can create and edit your own personal farms.
The Garden allows you to easily create and edit farming plots, and due to its magical nature, your crops will grow much quicker on it!
Learn more about the Garden below!
► Sam the Stranger
The Garden is accessible after speaking to the Stranger that appears on your Private Island at Skyblock Level 5. Once they appear, you are sent a chat message with their coordinates.
Head to the coordinates to find Sam, who will compliment your Private Island and introduce you to The Garden!
► The Barn
The Barn, located in the middle of the island, is the main place of operation. At the Barn, you can unlock and configure plots, upgrade crops, serve visitors, and complete milestones to increase your Garden Level. The higher your Garden Level, the higher tier crops you will gain access to.
Plus, you can also change the look of your Barn with a Barn Skin, allowing you to personalize and make your Garden truly unique!
Barn Skins
Do you feel like standing out from the crowd or showing off? We are also introducing a new type of flashy cosmetic in this update called Barn Skins!
Choose a skin that matches your personality (or one that makes your friends jealous), as there are many to choose from!
► The Desk
The Desk is used for the day-to-day operations of your Garden. It is where you can see your Garden Level, manage your Plots, perform Crop Upgrades, access the SkyMart, change your Barn Skin, or view your Garden Milestones.
You can also use the /desk command to access your Desk from anywhere!
► Plots
The Garden comes with 24 different plots measuring 96x96 in size. Each plot is fully customizable and can be configured to your liking. Whether you wish to have a farm full of Wheat or grow every crop imaginable, it’s entirely up to you!
Presets
For those who prefer a more effortless approach, the Barn also offers a selection of pre-made farm presets that you can use right away. And for the ultimate convenience, you can create your own custom presets and easily reuse them across multiple plots to streamline your farming process.
A plot preset can be pasted into your Garden either by using materials from your inventory or sacks or by purchasing resources from the Bazaar directly at the Desk.
You can also build, save and load your own presets; however, if you are using your own custom preset, any blocks aside from crops that are destroyed will not drop items!
► Composter
The Composter is an essential part of your Barn. You can use your Composter to create a new item called Compost, which can be used to expand The Garden by unlocking new Plots. With each plot you unlock, you have the opportunity to expand your farming operations and grow even more crops. So don't hesitate, get started with the Composter and start unlocking those plots today!
Compost is created by inserting your harvested crops into the Composter along with a form of fuel to power it. In order to start creating Compost, the Composter must have enough Organic Matter and Fuel stored inside.
Once enough Fuel and Organic Matter are in the Composter; it starts generating Compost over time!
► SkyMart
SkyMart is your one-stop shop for everything you need in The Garden. Use it to browse the wide variety of products on offer – from Biofuel, the Builder’s Ruler, to new farming equipment, enchantments, or Barn Skins!
Some of the items in SkyMart cost Copper, a brand-new currency that is only available in The Garden. You gain Copper by accepting offers from your visitors.
► Garden Levels
The Garden comes with its own leveling system, named Garden Levels. These levels dictate the type of crops, amount of plots, crop upgrades, and visitors that you have access to.
You can level up your Garden Level by accepting offers from Visitors and unlocking new Garden Milestones.
► Visitors
Visitors are familiar NPCs from across SkyBlock, each with individual and specific requests. They will visit your Garden from time to time and present you with an offer, allowing you the choice to accept or decline their proposal.
Each visitor has different requirements, so make sure you are prepared for anything!
Visitor's Logbook
You can consult the Visitor's Logbook to keep track of your interactions with these visitors. This handy tool provides you with all the information you need to know about each visitor. Keep in mind that some Visitors will have to be unlocked before they make an appearance in your Garden!
► Crop Upgrades
Crop Upgrades come in 9 stages, where each stage grants +5 Farming Fortune for that specific crop while harvesting. Each upgrade requires a specific Garden Level and some Copper, and the previous upgrade must be unlocked before advancing to the next.
► Garden Milestones
Crop Milestones
As you harvest crops, you progress towards Crop Milestones. Each milestone yields different rewards, such as Garden Experience, Farming Experience, and SkyBlock Experience. The more milestones you unlock, the harder it is to advance to the next level.
Visitor Milestones
Unique Visitors
This milestone is progressed by accepting offers from visitors that are novel to your Garden. The more unique offers you complete, the more milestones you will unlock and the more rewards you will unlock.
Total Offers Accepted
Increase this milestone by accepting offers from all the visitors to your Garden. Complete more offers to unlock more milestones and gain more rewards!
► New Farming Armor
In addition, new sets of Farming Armor have been added to the game, offering various bonuses such as increased Farming Fortune and a chance to harvest rare drops from crops. You can find the first Farming Armor of the series in the later stages of the Melon Collection.
Here's one of these new Armor Sets, the rest you'll have to find yourself!
► New Community Shop Upgrade
We have also introduced a new Community Shop upgrade called Garden Farming Fortune which increases your Farming Fortune stat while on The Garden. This will increase your overall crop yield!
► Additional Changes
- Improved Basket of Seeds to include Sugar Cane, Cocoa Beans, and Cactus
- Changed "Drill Fuel" to "Machine Fuel"
- Added Hoe of Greatest Tilling to Anita's Shop
- Farming Fortune will now also work with a combination of Smelting Touch
- Added Farming Level Requirements for most Farming-related gear
- Added Farmer's Grace Ability to Rancher Boots
- Changed the usage of Prismapump
- Added a new Farming Armor set in the Rabbit Collection
- Added Box of Seeds, requiring 160 enchanted seeds
- Added various farming materials to the Bazaar
- Changed the description of the Harvesting Enchantment
- Added Enchanted Cactus Green and Enchanted Hay Bales to the Enchanted Agronomy Sack
- Added Garden-specific tasks inside Skyblock Levels
- Fixed the Suus quest not dropping his toy when killing the correct enemy
► Future Plans
SkyBlock Chapters
Chapters should be back on Alpha within the next few weeks, in preparation for its release.
Wizard Portal
We made a lot of progress and you can expect this update to appear on Alpha shortly after the next update.
Garden Update
More will be added to the garden at some point in the future, but not much else can be said at the moment as we are too early in the development.
Foraging Update
Like previously stated, we will unveil our plans in the last design thread, which we plan to release in the next few weeks.
As always, take these previews with a grain of salt - a lot of it is subject to change.
If you encounter any bugs, please submit a bug report!
>> SUBMIT BUG REPORT <<
We hope that you enjoy this new update, and we can't wait to see all the creative farming designs that players will come up with. Stay tuned for future updates as we plan to add new features and improvements to the Garden in the coming months!
Thank you for reading!

# Changelog/2023/February 2/0
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
0.17.3
SkyBlock Patch 0.17.3 - Guide Menu & SkyBlock Menu Updates
Updated: February 2, 2023
Hello,
Today we are releasing:
- The SkyBlock Guide Menu
- SkyBlock Menu Changes
## ► Guide Menu
Introducing the Guide Menu - a new feature in the game that helps players stay on track with their progress! With this menu, players can now easily view a list of specific tasks to complete at their current stage of the game. The Guide Menu helps keep you on the right path.
Please note that we are still balancing the Guide Menu based on player feedback and values in it are subject to change!
The Guide Menu will split into 7 different Game Stages: Starter, Amateur, Intermediate, Skilled, Expert, Professional, and Master. You progress through the Guide by completing tasks in your current stage.
If you're ever confused about something in the guide, you can click it to get more information from the Official Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki.
The Guide Menu can be accessed in the SkyBlock Leveling icon from the SkyBlock Menu!
## ► SkyBlock Menu Updates
The Updated SkyBlock Menu
We've made a few changes to the SkyBlock menu to have easier use and to be less confusing for newer players.
Why have we made these changes?
The SkyBlock Menu has remained the same ever since the game has released. As new icons were needed, they were slowly added to the main menu. Over time, this menu became more crowded and crowded. We have decided to make these changes not only to allow the main menu to look nicer but also to help with confusion towards new players. There are several icons that only show up at certain times or icons that need to be unlocked that the new player does not know about. We know a few of these changes will take some time to get used to but we believe it will be better in the long run. We first released these changes almost 3 weeks ago on the Alpha Network to allow mod developers time to properly adjust to these changes.
## What exactly has changed:
- Moved the Trades menu inside the Recipe Book menu
  - You can now instantly sell your items to the Booster Cookie menu if you have the Cookie Buff
  - Moved the Leveling icon to the place the Trades Menu was before
  - Added /trades as a quick shortcut into the Trades Menu
- Moved all 5 Bags into a new Bags menu that can be accessed from the main menu
  - If you have the Cookie Buff, you can now use the following commands:
    - /accessorybag (aliases are /accessories, /accessory, /abag, /ab, /accs)
    - /fishingbag (aliases are /fishbag, /fbag, /fb, /fishingb)
    - /potionbag (aliases are /potions, /potbag, /pots, /pbag, /potionsbag, /pb)
    - /quiver (aliases are /quiv, /arrowsbag, /arrowbag, /arrows)
    - The Sack of Sacks can still be accessed using /sacks without needing the Cookie Buff
- Moved Active Effects to Your Profile menu
- New Booster Cookie Menu
  - You can now buy Booster Cookies directly from this menu!
  - Cookie Clicker v0.02
    - Cookie Clicker now stays across lobbies!
    - There is a Cookie Clicker ranking to view your position.
  - All players have access to this menu from the main menu
- The Crystal Hollows - Entrance Fast Travel is now automatically unlocked when first visiting the Crystal Hollows
- If you do not have Pets, Wardrobe, Personal Bank, or any bags unlocked it will now show locked and what is required to unlock instead of hiding from the menu
- The Quick Warp to Jerry's Workshop has been moved to the Fast Travel menu
- The Calendar and Events menu has been reduced to two lines for readability
- The Upcoming Events and Personal Bank icons will now count down in the main menu in realtime
The New Booster Cookie menu
## Currently known issues:
1. Spooky festival SkyBlock experience is not currently being displayed properly. Any previously gained experience as well as future experience gained will still be stored correctly, however for the time being you will notice that it is missing. This will be fixed as soon as possible!
2. Heart of the Mountain sometimes grants "+0 SkyBlock XP" when mining
3. A few of the Collection suggestions in the Guide Menu are not currently possible
We are working to fix all of these issues as soon as possible.
That's it for today; thanks for reading!

# Changelog/2023/February 21
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
PATCH
Regarding Garden Height Limit Changes
Updated: February 21, 2023
Hey Everyone!
We will be making some important changes to the Garden regarding the buildable height limit.
These changes are not live yet - we will be updating this sentence when it is with a patch notes thread link.
## The Build Height Limit
The buildable height limit will be going from 50 to 10, starting at the bedrock layer.
One major thing that is becoming very obvious now that the Garden update is live is that it takes an incredible amount of space on our databases. As players keep unlocking more plots, the space taken by each player becomes exponential. To prevent issues in the near future, we are forced to make this change to limit how much space each player takes to ensure we don't start hitting saving issues and crashing problems.
While this sounds like a big nerf, we hope you'll understand why we're doing this. Originally, the Garden was designed as a way for a player to have farms in Minecraft that are not only useable and profitable but also useable without a Minecraft Farming degree. Layered farms are end-game farming - efficient but not intuitive and very ugly. New players shouldn't feel disadvantaged for making a pretty-looking farm versus a player with a cube of dirt.
As such, originally, the entirety of the Garden content was balanced around players not doing multi-layered farms, and we were hoping the enhancements from the garden would be enough to discourage players from doing them. During alpha testing, we didn't notice any players doing any kind of layered farming - we're sure there was, but we didn't see any ourselves while looking around. However, once the Garden went live, it became obvious that most players went right back to layered plots.
To recap, ultimately, this change is to ensure servers don't crash and burn and to make farming more approachable to new players and ultimately more aesthetically pleasing to the eye. It's a lot nicer when someone with a pretty farm gets the same output as anyone else, really.
While we're aware this is, from a first glance, a nerf, we'll be monitoring player progress and determining if we do need to change anything to the Garden's progress.
## How is this going to happen?
To keep this short:
- Blocks can no longer be placed above the new limit
- Crops above the limit can be harvested still but will no longer grow
- Preset Saving will ignore blocks above the limit
- Preset Pasting will clean up blocks above the limit
That way, when this patch is live, if you need to recover building materials above the limit, you can do so before pasting any presets, but if you just want to clean up the extra blocks ASAP, just paste a preset to clear it up.
## Crop growing speed
One of the reasons why multiple layers have been necessary for players is because of gathering loops - if you gather your crops, ideally, by the time you are done at the end of your plot, the beginning of your plot should be ready to be gathered again.
For that reason, having the build height limit shouldn't cause any changes in how quickly someone can gather their crops while ensuring there aren't any overkill destroying databases or making players' eyes bleed by having floating blocks everywhere. What will change really is the need to use more plots to complete the loop.
We are, however, aware of some issues with some crops - specifically nether warts - where they need to regrow faster for this to be feasible. We'll be looking into speeding up their growth rate with the height limit patch to ensure they are not negatively affected by it. We're aware that mushrooms runs into the same issue - however, changing their growth rate will require another patch later as we figure out how to implement it properly.
Finally, Garden levels will be changed to increase crop growth rate on your plots. Ultimately, these changes will ensure that the height limit nerf doesn't really feel like a nerf anymore.
## Unlocking Plots
One big concern with this change is unlocking plots - since there is technically less space on each plot, it is now more important to unlock all plots, and there have been growing concerns that the last few plots seem too hard, or even impossible to unlock at all without spending an insane amount of money. While we won't be reducing the cost of plots, we'll look at options to allow players to improve their compost gain through composter upgrades soon.
## Farming Armor
Not much to say, but we do want to mention that we'll be increasing the amount of farming fortune on farming armor across the board.
We apologize for making this change after release; we hope you will understand why they are necessary.
This is a long thread isn't it - here's a TLDR in case you have places to be.
- Height limit above bedrock lowered from 50 to 10
- Nether Warts growing speed increased to help gathering loops
- Garden Levels will provide an increase in crop growing speed
- A future update will allow players to improve compost production
- Farming Armor (most pieces) will receive an increase in farming fortune

[January 10th] Some Bug Fixes
Updated: January 10, 2023
Hey all!
Today we have rolled a collection of bug fixes and tweaks to the game:
- Fixed Wither Cloak shield remaining when you die in Dungeons or Kuudra
- Fixed Wither Cloak ability not having the correctly reduced cooldown as Mage in Dungeons
- Fixed Wither Cloak cancelling immediately if you deactivate and activate the ability before the first activation would have expired
