- Fixed Bonzo Mask and upgraded Bonzo Mask having separate cooldowns allowing for double the ability usage
- Fixed Kuudra sometimes submerging downwards during Phase 4
- Fixed lower tier Kuudra Core accessories counting towards MP/stats when you have a higher tier equipped
- Fixed the message when you hit Kuudra with an Energized Chunk displaying the incorrect amount of time added
- Fixed Slayer bosses despawning on the End Island when a Dragon is killed
- Fixed Protector Dragon armor perk increasing stats other than Defence
- Fixed a bug which sometimes allowed a player to double kill a mob with the Dark Claymore
- Fixed Kuudra Dropship TNT, Bonzo's explosive Balloons and the Power Dragon's TNT Bomb not dealing true knockback
- Fixed an exploit with dropping Training Weights on join
- Fixed some Jerry's Workshop mobs not being blacklisted from the Totem of Corruption effect
- Fixed North Stars counter not always appear in the scoreboard when on Jerry's Workshop
- Tweaked Lava Bounce code to make it more reliable in Dungeons and Kuudra
- Tweaked the Line Up Terminal puzzle in the Floor 7 Dungeon boss fight to be less laggy and more responsive
- Tweaked the Phoenix Pet Rekindle Perk to work more similarly to the Spirit Mask ability and to put you back to half health on activation
- Tweaked doors in Dungeons to open slightly faster
- Tweaked Fresh Tools Kuudra Perk to scale from 2->4->6->8->10% to 5->7.5->10->12.5->15%
- Tweaked mobs spawned by minions to no longer have collisions, damage indicators and health holograms
- Tweaked the December shop requirement to be clearer
If you have a bug to report to us, please make a report HERE! Thanks to everyone who has reported bugs!

# Changelog/2023/January 11
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
0.17.2
SkyBlock Patch 0.17.2 - Leveling Part 2
Updated: January 11, 2023
Hello,
Today we are releasing the second part of the Leveling update!
- 23 New Ways to Level Up
- Redesigned and Cleaner Levels Menus
- Museum Value Updates
- Levels XP Balancing
- Kuudra Hunt Fixes
## ► New Ways to Level Up
There are 23 new ways to earn SkyBlock XP!
- Mining Ores during the Mining Fiesta
- Killing Sharks during the Fishing Festival
- Placing in a bracket during the Spooky Festival
- Purchasing perks from the 6 Essence Shops:
  - Dragon, Undead, Wither, Ice, Spider, and Crimson
- The Kuudra Boss Collection
- Killing Mythological Creatures
- Defeating Kuudra Infernal Tier
- Completing the Crystal Nucleus
- Leveling your Peak of the Mountain
- Special Rock Pet Milestones
- Special Dolphin Pet Milestones
- Purchasing Upgrades from Anita's Shop
- Upgrading your Accessory Bag
- Eating Reaper Peppers
- Unlocking new Power Stones
- Getting all your Abiphone contacts
- Purchasing free upgrades from the Community Shop
- Upgrading your Personal Bank
Overall, there is now 3,887 new XP in the game amounting to another 38 levels you can now achieve!
## ► New Leveling Menus
The Ways to Level Up menu has been split into different categories to make finding what you're looking for easier!
You can now see your SkyBlock Level directly from the SkyBlock Menu!
## ► Levels Balancing
Through it's time on Alpha, we have balanced the amount of XP that certain tasks give, along with the ways to complete them.
- The Dojo
  - You now earn XP based on the highest belt you've achieved
  - XP earned: 350->425
- Harp Songs
  - You will now earn XP for partially completing songs as well!
  - XP earned: 210->236
- Bank Upgrades
  - XP earned: 165->200
## ► Museum Changes
- Item Values in the Museum are now determined based on the items value with all of its modifiers!
  - When determining a value, the server will use values from the bazaar to determine your total item value.
- There are more rewards in the Museum: 230->288
  - The rewards will scale as new items are added to the Museum.
Each item in the Museum now has a Clean Value and a Total Value!
## ► Kuudra Hunt Changes
Now that the Kuudra follower hunt has been solved, we have made a few changes to the steps to make it more accessible to players!
- All players no longer have to be in the air for Drakuu to recognize their efforts.
- You no longer need as much collection to speak to the Kuudra Loremaster!
- You can now use any drill you please to obtain the Kuudra Relic!
## ► Other Balancing
- Slugfish Bobber
  - Reduced Catch Time: 30->20 seconds
That's it for today; thanks for reading!

# Changelog/2023/January 5
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[January 5th] Knockback Resistance changes
Updated: January 5, 2023
Hi all,
Today we have rolled a small change to how the knockback immunity effect works in SkyBlock. This changes:
1. Some knockback immunity items not always cancelling knockback,
2. Knockback immunity overriding other knockback reduction/immunity effects.
This aims to fix situations where, for example, if you unequipped a Slime Hat in Dungeons as a Tank, you would lose your knockback resistance, or if you were hit multiple times at once, you would take knockback through your resistance.
This change affects the following:
- Slime Hat
- Dwarf Turtle Shelmet
- Unflippable Turtle Pet Perk
- Dungeons Tank KB Resistance
This change also fixes Slime knockback not being cancelled by these items/effects.
We are aware of issues related to lava bouncing in Dungeons and are currently looking into a fix. We will also be monitoring what effect this change has on the game and will make follow up adjustments if we feel necessary.
If you have a bug to report to us, please make a report HERE! Thanks to everyone who has reported bugs!

# Changelog/2023/July 12
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
0.19.1
SkyBlock Patch 0.19.1 - Item Linking, Leveling Update, Stash QoL, & Bug Fixes
Updated: July 12, 2023
Hey Guys!
SkyBlock has been updated to version 0.19.1. New features in this patch are Item Linking and several Leveling updates, as well as many other changes and bug fixes.
## ► Item Linking
You can now show off your items in the chat! All you need is the Cookie Buff, and you're good to go.
To link in chat, you just run /show!
You can also use the following keywords to link a specific item in chat: hand, helmet, chestplate, leggings, boots, necklace, cloak, belt, gloves, bracelet, pet. You can use one of these keywords by running /show <keyword>
When clicking on an item link in chat, you will see the following menu:
Inside this menu, you can see the following:
- Profile Viewer
  - Allows you to view the player's information
- Trade Request
  - Sends a trade request detailing the specific item you are interested in
- Worth Breakdown
  - Will show the worth of the item + a breakdown of all modifiers and their individual worth
- Wiki Link
  - Sends a link to the wiki to view more information about that item
- Similar Auctions
  - Will show the cheapest auction available for the item + the closest auction to the item shown
- Extra Data
  - Will show extra information about the item, such as the NPC sell price, dungeon info, etc.
- Item Modifiers
  - Shows all available modifiers that can and have been applied to the item
## ► Leveling Update
We've introduced some new updates to the Leveling system to better reward players for leveling up and make it easier to understand!
- New Leveling & Reward Menus
- New Level Prefix Colors
- Emblems
- The Book of Progression
New Level Prefix Colors
- We have introduced new colors to the level prefix system and rearranged the existing colors to be more cohesive and provide better future-proofing.
## ► Emblems
Emblems are a new way to show off certain accomplishments you have earned across SkyBlock. There are a grand total of 80 emblems you can unlock currently, and more will be added in the future!
- You select an emblem inside the SkyBlock Leveling menu.
- Emblems are unlocked through Skills, Dungeons, Slaying, your SkyBlock Level, and Misc. Achievements.
- Your Selected Emblem will display next to your name, in the tab list, and in chat.
- When hovering over your emblem in chat, players will see extra information based on the emblem you've selected.
- Players can also see your emblems in your profile viewer.
## ► Book of Progression
The Book of Progression is a new accessory unlocked at SkyBlock Level 25 that upgrades as you level up! When you first unlock the book, it will be Common, and as you level up your SkyBlock Level, it will eventually upgrade to Mythic!
The book also has passive bonuses that you will unlock. You can select one to infuse to the book to help you along the way. The percentages on each of these passives scale as your SkyBlock Level increases:
- Stacked Healing
  - When you are being healed, heal +0-5% of the amount.
- Defensive Strongman - unlocked at Level 50
  - When attacked, take 0-5% less damage.
- Extra Infliction - unlocked at Level 75
  - When dealing damage, deal +0-5% more damage.
## ► Stash QoL
You can now view your item or material stash in a menu by using /viewstash!
- Stash menus allow you to visualize your stash and take individual items
- Inside the material stash, you can insta-sell items to the Bazaar or send items directly to your sacks
## ► Other Changes
Trades
- Added a new highest visual coin tier.
- Added a coin trade limit of 50 million coins per day.
  - This daily limit increases to 1b coins if you are SkyBlock level 50 or higher.
  - This daily limit increases to 10b coins if you are SkyBlock level 100 or higher.
UI and Commands
- Added /auctionsearch command (/ahsearch, /ahs).
- Can now use suffixes "d", "h", and "m" to specify time in auctions (i.e. 6d17h5m)
Composter
- Added list of all crops and fuel in inventory and sacks when hovering over menu icons.
- Added two separate buttons for taking items from inventory and from sacks for each crops and fuel.
- Added support for taking fuel from sacks.
Other other Changes
- Can now put Sacks in Ender Chests and Backpacks.
- Buffed the Roll' em perks of Melon and Pumpkin Dicers. The upgrades of these dicers have a chance to drop more items with their Roll' Em ability.
## ► Bug Fixes
The Rift
- Fixed capitalization error of 'barry' in Rift Guide.
Enchants
- Fixed Reflection being applied to players' profiles even after removing enchant.
- Fixed Thorns not removing the Reflection enchant on chestplates.
- Replenish enchant now works while riding a pet.
- Added Delicate enchantment to the Hex
- Fixed the coin gain notification displaying a lower amount of coins than you actually gained when using the Scavenger enchantment or Scavenger Talisman.
Pets and Equipment
- Fixed Reaper Armor saying, "This armor piece is undead" twice.
- Fixed Reaper Armor proccing the zombie perk twice three times.
- Fixed Fairy Armor giving extra speed.
- Fixed Fairy Armor not playing a sound when Fairy Souls are nearby.
- Fixed being able to use Bigfoot’s Lasso as a normal Lead
- Fixed Wither Cloak consuming mana when taking friendly hits.
- Fixed Magma Lord Gauntle's perk not increasing if a Mythic Flying Fish is equipped
- Fixed Mushroom armor not giving permanent Night Vision effect
- Added a cooldown to the Weather Stick.
- Fixed Shark Scale armor not awarding the Absorb It All achievement.
- Fixed the Crimson armor ability working without meeting the requirements.
- Fixed Hex allowing Silex to be applied to Stonk Pickaxes.
- Enabled Phoenix pet to give flight on garden.
- Fixed Slayer Bosses and Minibosses not counting towards Grandma Wolf’s pet kill combo
- Fixed Drills and Hurricane Bow keeping their sell price lore when exiting a shop.
- Fixed Pickonimbus 2000 not losing durability on private islands and Gardens.
- Fixed Saving Grace and Remnant of the Eye activating simultaneously.
- Fixed some Cake Soul capture dates not being shown in EST.
- Added tier numbers to the names of Campfire accessories so they can be differentiated in the SkyBlock Guide.
- Fixed shapeless recipes taking away too many ingredients in some cases.
- Fixed Right-clicking the Spiked Atrocity saying "No recipes!" in chat.
Quests, Achievements and NPCs
- Fixed Lonely Philosopher NPC incorrect voice pitch.
- Also fixed a couple other NPCs that had incorrect interaction sounds.
- Fixed being able to mount on Horses from Trapper’s Quest
- Fixed Touch Choice Achievement being granted under the wrong conditions

- Fixed being able to upgrade Mycelium or Red Sand minions without the required reputation.
- Fixed being unable to finish Shaggy’s quest if all Relics were found previously.
- Fixed the Jungle Temple door not closing after using an Ascension Rope.
- Fixed Windmill Operator shop name.
- Fixed Carpenter NPC requiring VIP rank on the Stranded mode.
- Fixed Treasure Hunter's dialogue overriding the option selection.
- Fixed Metal Detector working outside of the Mines of Divan.
- Fixed Berberis Fuel Injector not working in Chicken Minions
- Renamed the 2 Explorer quests to Hub Explorer and Nether Explorer so they can be differentiated in the Quests menu.
- Fixed being able to claim next year's New Year's Cake if you keep the menu open after the Baker disappears.
- Fixed color issue in the Voodoo Doll lore.
- Fixed the Personal Deletor not deleting double catch items from fishing.
Private Islands and the Garden
- Fixed an incorrect result when combining a Spider’s Den scroll with a Portalizer.
- Fixed being able to use Biome Sticks and different items as fuel.
- Fixed Enchanted Bone Meal consumption even if nothing was grown
- Fixed Hoppers being able to pick up Cobwebs dropped from the Tarantula Broodfather jump effect.
- Limited the Day/Night Savers to only 1 on private islands.
- Fixed Showcase Block not showing some item names and lore correctly.
- Fixed Slime Training Dummy health being too low.
- Fixed Plumbers Sponge not working on portals.
- Fixed the next page button sometimes not working when changing sorting options in the Visitor's Log Book.
- Changed the Visitor's Log Book to show unnicked names.
- Fixed being able to teleport dragon eggs on the Garden, causing them to be moved from plots that have block drops disabled.
- Fixed the parkour times leaderboard on private islands displaying the most recent times instead of best times.
- Had a serious chat with Pete regarding his garden visits, and he should now award Farming experience.
Bazaar, Auction House and Shen's
- Fixed the Auction House Tier filter not working properly for potions.
- Fixed not being able to use the Shen Auction displays in some cases.

Other Miscellaneous Fixes
- Fixed being able to write notes when creating a dungeon party for muted players.
- Fixed being unable to craft Coal Blocks using charcoal.
- Fixed Community Shop profile upgrade history not displaying the right player names
- Fixed not being able to use a Bat fireworks if you had a Spooky Sack.

- Fixed Gemstone Mixture recipe not showing fully in the Forge menu.
- Fixed Runecrafting XP not being awarded correctly from combining runes.
- Fixed collection not being awarded properly upon gaining rewards from some bosses and loot chests.
- Fixed The SkyBlock Guide showing incorrect Vampire Slayer level tiers.
- Fixed SkyBlock XP not updating after upgrading pets.
- Fixed being unable to invite ex-members to co-op.
- Fixed being unable to invite ex-members to co-op when lifetime members have been maxed out.
- Fixed the Slayer rewards preview menu not being capitalized.
- Extended the Mountain region so it covers the area near the Museum.
- Fixed some Nether Wart patches on the Crimson Isle not being breakable.
- Fixed the Forge confirm menu not displaying all items required for the Gemstone Mixture.
- Fixed numbers sometimes being slightly wrong when using letter suffixes.
- Fixed several grammar errors and typos.

# Changelog/2023/June 20
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
0.19
SkyBlock Patch 0.19 - The Rift
Updated: June 20, 2023
Hey all!
The Wizard Portal is finally repaired! It only took 3-5 business days. Rumors are that, upon completion, Barry the Wizard (the ex-mayor) took the risk for all of us and stepped into the portal.
What he discovered might shock you!
If you haven't seen it yet, check out the trailer for this update:
[1]
### ► New Island: The Rift
The Rift is an alternate dimension of SkyBlock based on the Hub. You can access it by talking to the Wizard in the (real) SkyBlock Hub in his tower at (45,122,73) once you're at least Combat level XII.
It costs a few experience bottles (or bits) to join, but you can also join for free every 4 hours.
The island is a separate dimension, meaning your items, pets, bags... they don't transfer into the Rift. But whatever you acquire inside the Rift, you get to keep it when you return!
Wizard's Menu to join the Rift
Once you join the island, you will only have a limited amount of time available inside before you are sent back to the "real" world. Armor, pets, accessories, and various unlocks will allow you to gain more time and access further zones in the island whenever you re-join it.
The Rift island
### ► Questing Like Never Before
With The Rift, we've tried something a little different than usual with SkyBlock.
The Rift isn't a totally infinite adventure; it has an end goal of maxing out the Rift Necklace. The island has a series of quests to find and secure "Timecharms," which upgrade the necklace.
To ease your way through the quests, make sure to talk to the Wizard INSIDE the Rift to unlock the Rift Guide:
Rift Guide unlocked from the Wizard's quest inside the Rift
### ► New Collections & Slayer
Don't worry; once you complete the mainline quest, there's more to do!
There are 6 new mini-collections in the Rift and a brand new slayer boss.
New carrot-making method?
The Rift operates with its own currency instead of coins, called Motes. You get motes from killing mobs, completing quests, and selling items to the Motes Grubber.
Whoever thinks money thinks Shen, and of course, Shen is the richest person in any dimension; therefore, you can spend your Motes in many shops on the island as well as Shen's Auctions:
Shen Auction inside the Rift
There is also the Vampire Slayer boss, the Riftstalker Bloodfiend. It goes up to Tier V on release, which is uh... very challenging.
Vampire slayer boss
All this means that even once you "finished" the Rift, there's most likely a reason for you to come back once in a while!
### ► Conclusion
That's it for this post! You can join the server to find out what other mysteries are hiding in The Rift! It's a self-contained adventure, available for players of any game stage, full of jokes and never-seen-before mechanics.
We can't wait to hear what you think about it!

Updated: June 23, 2023
Hello, here are the changes to the Rift island in the last few days since its release!
Bugfixes
- Implemented better reset of leech supreme abilities (cleaning up holes in the ground that appeared sometimes)
- Fixed getting teleported inside the plaza by the Déja Vu Alley despite not having unlocked it yet
- Fixed being able to get multiple agaricus caps from 1 mushroom by spam clicking

- Fixed the yellow breadcrumb particles being displayed in every island

- Now letting Rift Ferret level up as a regular combat pet
- Fixed Weird Tuba / Weirder Tuba walk speed buff not working inside the rift

Slayer-Related Fixes / Tuning
- Fixed Blood Ichor spawning way too high
- Fixed Killer Spring not spawning in the underground area
- Fixed assisters still being in the attack list of the slayer boss once they die, making them fill up the guards of the Oubliette by repeatingly dying to mania
- Adjusted RNG meter for vampire stuff to be easier to grind

# Changelog/2023/June 26/2
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
ANNOUNCEMENT
Resolution to Rift Access (6/26/23)
Updated: June 26, 2023
Hey everyone!
We would like to apologize for closing off the Rift for the past few hours.
This morning, we discovered that an exploit allowed players to transfer items in and out of the Rift without any restrictions, allowing players to bring in powerful equipment. This obviously allowed players to overpower the Rift's content tremendously.
To fix this issue, we have had to figure out quite a few things, but we've managed to roll an update that should fix both the exploit and anyone who used the exploit, whether they did it intentionally or not.
This means that any items you have transferred into the Rift will be removed from your inventory and sent to a new NPC at the Wizard's Tower called Udium. Any Rift items that were taken outside of the Rift will simply be deleted. This should not cause any issues as there will always be a way to recover lost items by going back to related NPCs in the Rift.
If an item was brought out of the rift using the exploit, enchanted/reforged/other, then brought back in the rift using the exploit, the item will be deleted aswell.
Do note that another issue cropped up which may have removed some rift-transferable talismans from accessory bags. We are working on a solution to this problem and will let you all know more about it in the next few days.
With this, the Rift has been reopened and can be explored again. We apologize for the inconvenience.

# Changelog/2023/June 7
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[June 7] SkyBlock Patch Notes
Updated: June 7, 2023
Hey all!
We are here to inform you about the latest update that was just rolled to SkyBlock!
Bug Fixes
- Fixed pets preventing you from placing blocks, even when invisible.
- Fixed Cleave doing 0 damage to nearby entities.
- Fixed an issue in custom block breaking zones that caused the mining speed not to update correctly, resulting in being unable to break a block when you should be able to.
- Fixed an issue in custom mining zones which prevented you from breaking blocks (mostly above the player), very close to the maximum reach distance.
- Fixed profile menu not being able to display large amounts of Bank Coins.
- Fixed incorrectly worded error message when applying the 10 Hot Potato Book to items.
- Fixed not being able to put leashes on Mushroom Cows on Farming Island.
- Fixed being able to teleport to spawn by going out of bounds and not cancelling the active parkour (e.g. in The End).
- Fixed a bug that caused Frost Walker enchantment to be active when no boots or boots without the enchantment were worn under very specific circumstances.
- Fixed (Dungeon's) Archer's Explosive Shot cooldown expiry message sending twice.
- Fixed (Dungeon's) Archer's Explosive Shot cooldown not being reduced by your Catacombs level.
- Fixed not being able to interact with Fence Gates when holding specific items.
- Fixed Quick Crafting suggestions not correctly sorting some recipes that produce varying amounts of the same item.
Quality of Life
- Added a new icon to the upcoming events menu during the Spooky Festival that can be used to access the Fear Mongerer shop and Spooky Festival rewards from anywhere in SkyBlock
- You can now ready up by clicking your already selected class in Dungeons.
- When trying to queue for Dungeons without a party, then navigating to the party finder, it will automatically filter the parties by the floor you tried to queue to.
- /ec now has an optional page argument. For example: /ec 3 opens the third Ender Chest page.
- Added a "Right click to open" hint to Sack lores.
If you have a bug to report to us, please make a report HERE! Thanks to everyone who has reported bugs!

# Changelog/2023/June 8
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
PATCH
SkyBlock - 4th Anniversary!
Updated: June 8, 2023
Hello,
It's remarkable to think that it's been four years since SkyBlock first went live. As we mark this important milestone, we've put together some special events to celebrate our collective journey.
The celebration kicks off on Monday, the 12th, and will last for exactly 7 days, with a series of activities and updates:
## ► Party Time!
During the event, Simon will be back in the Hub, bearing gifts for every player - a randomly selected Party Hat. While Crab hats won't be making a return, the new Party Hats are sure to turn heads with their Sloth Emoji designs:
​
Like their predecessors, these new Party Hats are Hatcessories - they not only serve as unique cosmetic pieces but also function as accessories. Please note they are in the same "accessory group" as the Crab Hats, so owning both will not provide additional Magical Power.
## ► Skill EXP Boost
We're giving everyone a chance to level up faster - for the entirety of the event, a 20% Skill EXP boost will be in effect across the game.
## ► The Return of the Pig Shop
The Pig Shop is returning with its special brand of commerce. Shiny Orbs can once again be used on the Pigs in the Hub for their usual rewards.
Please note the Pig Shop has seen a few changes. Due to in-game economic fluctuations, some items have seen a price increase.
But, in better news, we've added a new item: the Dante Ring .
Recently found in a hidden warehouse, those accessories are for sale, though they don't hold any explicit functionality...
Once again, they are in the same accessory group as the Dante Talisman, so having both does not give extra magical power.
The Dante Talisman will never return, as it is a collector item. But this provides an opportunity for those who missed the Talisman to claim its equivalent magic power.
## ► 4 Years of SkyBlock Video
Join us Sunday, June 11 at 2PM EST / 11AM PST / 7PM BST (on SkyBlock's birthday!) to watch a special video to commemorate the history of the game. It even features some of your favorite creators!
You can find the premiere here:
## ► Looking Back
Let's take a trip down memory lane and take a look at what has been released in the past 365 days!
- Recent Bug Fixes (June 22, 2022)
- Bug Fixes (July 27, 2022)
- Thunder Revolution Update (August 1, 2022)
- Chili Pepper Update (August 5, 2022)
- 0.14 - Shen's Auction, The Hex, RNG Meter (August 18, 2022)
- Maddox is Mad (August 27, 2022)
- 0.14.1 - Moby's Fishing Speed Update (September 6, 2022)
- Test of Control Dojo Challenge (September 8, 2022)
- 0.15 - Leveling (September 28, 2022)
- 0.16 - Kuudra Revamp & End Island QoL (October 19, 2022)
- 0.16.1 - Spider's Den QoL, Finnegan, and more! (November 9, 2022)
- 0.17 - Holiday Update (December 1, 2022)
- 0.17.1 - Kuudra Infernal Tier & Party Finder (December 8, 2022)
- Patch Notes (December 13, 2022)
- Winter Follow Up (December 14, 2022)
- 0.17.2 - Leveling Part 2 (January 11, 2023)
- 0.17.3 - Guide Menu (February 2, 2023)
- Autopet Updates (February 3, 2023)
- 0.18 - The Garden (February 14, 2023)
- Final Dungeon Journals (February 16, 2023)
- 0.18.1 - Achievements (March 8, 2023)
- 0.18.2 - Garden & Stranded Updates (March 28, 2023)
- 0.18.3 - Garden Presets & Bazaar/Sack Additions (April 18, 2023)
- 0.18.4 - Sack Additions & Bug Fixes (May 11, 2023)
- 0.18.5 - Abiphone Contacts & Dungeon Balancing (May 31, 2023)
The Hypixel team has worked their hardest to make SkyBlock the best it can be, and we look forward to everything that will be released in the next year!
Enjoy these screenshots of the original SkyBlock testing hub that was used before the game even released! ​
With that, we conclude the rundown of our 4th-anniversary event. We hope you enjoy all the activities we've lined up, and here's to another exciting year in SkyBlock!

# Changelog/2023/March 28
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
0.18.2
SkyBlock Update 0.18.2 - Garden and Stranded Update
Updated: March 28, 2023
Hey everyone! SkyBlock has been updated to version 0.18.2! This version comes with major updates to the Garden and Stranded, including composter upgrades, NPC sell price changes, Garden Visitor adjustments, and Stranded Lava Fishing.
## Composter Upgrades
You can now upgrade your Composter using crops from your Garden! This new feature includes five upgrades, with each having 25 levels. As you progress through each level, you'll significantly increase the amount of compost gained as well as gain additional SkyBlock XP.
The five upgrades are as follows:
## Additional Garden Changes
- Significantly increased the NPC sell price of all crops, including rare crops.
- Visitors can take items held in sacks when their offer is accepted. Items in the player's inventory are prioritized over sacks.
- Visitors have been chastised for sometimes asking for too many items. Some of these requests should have been shrunk.
- Increased Copper rewards by an additional 30%
- Added Seymour's Armor set to the Museum.
- Increased the private island and Garden teleport pad limit to 56.
- Added a chat message when a visitor joins your Garden.
- Seeds are now affected by Farming Fortune.
- The wheat crop upgrade now gives Farming Fortune to seeds.
- Custom plot presets can now have special icons added to them.
- Nether wart now gives Farming XP instead of Alchemy XP.
- The next Farming Contest will always display in tab.
- Crop Milestones will now appear in the tab list for your current crop.
- Strength will now appear in tab when you have a Mooshroom Cow pet equipped.
- All farming tools can now be sold at the Auction House.
- Added Skill Numeral support for all Garden Milestones.
- Farming tools can now only break crops and stems (farming axes can also break logs)
- Each unlocked Garden plot gives +3 Farming Fortune while on your Garden and +5 SkyBlock XP.
- Seeds, Cropies, Squash, Fermentos, and Condensed Fermentos can now be added to the Composter.
- Color codes now work on signs in the Garden.
- Using /warp garden while in your Garden will bring you back to your garden spawn point
- Jacob and Anita now have their own space instead of invading Sam's.
  - Note: You may not see their new location until a different barn skin is applied to your garden plot.
- Added 3 new Garden accessories.
- Added 2 new Garden enchants.
- Added 5 new Garden reforge stones.
- Added 2 new pet items.
A few of the items added ​
## Stranded Changes
Lava Fishing Changes
- Enabled Lava Fishing on Stranded
- Odger can now be placed on Stranded Islands.
- Lava Sea Creatures now have an extra chance to become corrupted (0.1% chance per fishing level)
- Plhlegblast can now spawn on Stranded Islands.
- Some special lava fishes will have different methods of obtaining than normal game modes. Experiment to figure out how to obtain them!
  - Volcanic Stonefish, Sulphur Skitter, Skeleton Fish, Moldfin, Soul Fish, Karate Fish, Gusher
Other Changes
- Ender Leggings drop rate nerfed from 3% to 0.3% to match the rest of the armor set
- Zealots now have a chance to drop the Enderman pet
- Golden Ghouls now have a chance to drop the Ghoul pet
- Removed Chili Pepper, Sulphur (ore), and Mycelium from the Builder NPC spawn requirements
- Runic Mobs now spawn on stranded islands
- Dungeon Completion Requirements are no longer required on Stranded Island items
- Marina's Fishing Wisdom perk now also works on Stranded Profiles
- Oasis Sheep and Rabbits can now be fished. Experiment to figure out how to obtain them!
- Fixed Zealot Spawning
- Added the following trades to Villagers as level 1 trades:
  - 1 to 1 Emerald > Mycelium
  - 1 to 1 Emerald > Sulphur
## Other Changes
- Can now use /warp home and /warp garden to warp to your island spawn from within the island.
- Improved Gwendolyn's dialog with new players to teach them about the Fast Travel menu's Crystal Hollow option
- All quest items in Romero and Juliet's quest are now co-op soulbound
- All Tyashoi Alchemist items are now co-op soulbound and can be sold to vendors for a minimal amount of coins.
- The Seal of the Family, Crooked Artifact, and the Shady Ring are now soulbound.
  - These items can now also be re-purchased from Rusty for the following price:
  - Seal of the Family: 10 Million Coins
  - Crooked Artifact: 2 Million Coins
  - Shady Ring: 500,000 Coins
- Reworded the Zombie Pet's perk Chomp to say Heal rather than Gain. Functionality has not changed.
- Updated some discrepancies in the Sea Creature Guide (/scg) for Crystal Hollow sea creatures to ensure the correct information is shown in the Requirements section.
- Odger should turn magmafish into silver magmafish as needed when fileting Trophy Fish.
- In Dungeons: The Tank ability "Seismic Wave" should now also be able to explode crypts.
- Re-enabled fishing rod damage on Private Islands (and Stranded Islands)
- Added two more tiers to the Sack of Sacks upgrade to the Clownfish collection and three tiers of it to the Community Shop.
## Bug Fixes
- Fixed an issue where Alchemist would disappear when the chunk was unloaded.
- Fix Smelting Touch not counting drops towards crop milestones.
- Fixed teleport pads not removing correctly when pasting plots.
- Fixed Seraphine not being unlocked when talking to her in the Election Room.
- Added a confirmation menu when pasting a plot.
- Added Jake's Plushie to the SkyBlock Guide.
- Made some of the Farming Tools' descriptions clearer.
- Fixed paste progress in the plots menu.
- Fixed an exploit with very high cacti
- Snow Shovels now work on the Garden
- Fixed Cocoa Beans not placed properly on the top height limit block
- Fixed Terry (visitor) not unlocking properly
- Fixed the Logbook so that it lists Beth's requirements after receiving Jake's Plushie
- Fixed Cleanup appearing on locked plots scoreboards
- Fixed Box of Seeds from Minion not giving XP
- Fixed Garden Scythe to allow it to break leaves
- Fixed Tia the Fairy's dialogue mentioning the Fairy Soul after you've already claimed it
- Fixed the Rookie Hoe's ability not doing anything
- Fixed the Pickobulus breaking teleport pads
- Fixed the Pickobulus breaking doors in gardens
- Fixed Glowstone on the Garden, giving Vanquished Glowstone Gauntlet Milestones
- Fixed Jukeboxes not working correctly on the Garden
- Fixed a dupe detection issue with the Fishing Blessing causing bad detections
## Crop Growth Speed
We are still planning on increasing crop growth speed, as seen during Alpha testing, but we need a bit more time to iron out the issues to avoid performance issues. This update will come out at a later date.

- Fixed Parrot pet not repeating emotes correctly.

- Added a way to preview Fire Sales through Elizabeth on Stranded.
- Fixed rarity in Ring of Love chat message when it has been recombobulated.
- Fixed being able to shear Sheep summoned by Conjuring in Dungeons.
- Added Red Sand and Mycelium trades to the Trades menu in Stranded.
If you have a bug to report to us, please make a report HERE! Thanks to everyone who has reported bugs!
Please note: We are still balancing and patching this release. This list is not exhaustive and is subject to change.

# Changelog/2023/May 22
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
ANNOUNCEMENT
Regarding Teleport and Jump pad removal in the Garden
Updated: May 22, 2023
Hey Everyone, I'm here again to address some important changes to SkyBlock gameplay, specifically the use of Teleport and Jump Pads in the Garden.
Effective after the release of this thread, Jump Pads and Teleport Pads will stop working in the Garden. To avoid further issues, we suggest players remove them from their plots.
The reason for this change is because we believe these tools makes the gameplay of Farming in SkyBlock too simple. Removing the use of these tools will ensure that some player interaction is required to a certain degree, proving that players are actually playing the game. This will also ensure that it is more easy for us to distinguish between actual players versus bots/scripts.
In tandem with the above change, we're also removing Farming fortune from private islands. This should ensure the Garden remains the key area for private farming.
Finally, due to the intended convenience of teleport pads and jumping pads being removed, we are adding a new command to allow quick movement between plots ( /tptoplot [plotname], /plotteleport [name] ).
As a somewhat related side note - we are currently monitoring the current state of SkyBlock's economy (we always are), and are aware of potential issues with inflation at this moment. These issues will be addressed in the near future.

# Changelog/2023/May 31
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
0.18.5
SkyBlock Patch 0.18.5 - Abiphone Contacts, Dungeons Balancing, & Bug Fixes
Updated: May 31, 2023
Hey Guys!
SkyBlock has been updated to version 0.18.5 - this patch addresses quite a good bit of bug fixes and miscellaneous other changes, including important balancing. This patch also addresses a bit of the inflation problems the game has been experiencing lately.
## ► Balancing and other additions
Abiphone
- Increased Abiphone Contract Trio Limit to 12
- Added Odger, Lumber Merchant and Kuudra Gatekeeper as Abiphone Contacts
- Modified several Abiphone Contacts difficulties
Menus and Inventories
- Added an upgrade to increase players max pets in the Pet Menu
- Added a Confirmation menu when swapping Pet Items
- Added a Confirmation menu when deleting souls in Summoning Ring and other Necromancy Items
- Improved lore descriptions of several Romero and Juliette quests items
- Added a setting to toggle consuming baits from your bag when fishing.
- You can no longer open the reaper scythe souls menu while fighting the Voidgloom Seraph.
- Trevor now informs you how much of his cooldown is left in his dialogue.
Balance Changes
- Allow Pocket Sack-in-a-Sack to be used up to 3 times on the same Sack
- Allow Healing VIII potion to bypass potion limit on Dungeons
- Slightly increased Clay Ball fishing rate on the Fisherman's Hut
- Increased each of the katana's Enderman damage bonus by 50%.
- Sleeping Eyes are no longer auctionable.
Economy-related Changes
These changes are explained later in this thread
- NPC Sell Prices have been changed for the following items:
  - Carrot: 4 -> 3 Coins
  - Nether Wart: 5 -> 4 Coins
  - Potato: 4 -> 3 Coins
  - Sugar Cane: 5 -> 4 Coins
- Heavily increased the droprates of Recombobulator 3000s on most floors
- Increased the drop rate and price of Necron's Handle and it's constituent scrolls (Wither Shield, Shadow Warp, Implosion)
- Increased the price of Dark Claymore
- Bin Auction Listing Fees have been changed for certain price brackets:
  - 1% for items <10,000,000 coins (unchanged)
  - 2% for items <100,000,000 coins
  - 2.5% for items >100,000,000 coins
Other changes and additions
- Added Wooden Tools to the Lumber Merchant
- Added introduction dialogues to Master Tactician Funk and Christopher
- Changed idle dialogue from Carpenter and Fisherman
- Added Trophy Fishing Sacks
  - Bronze Trophy Fishing Sack can hold Bronze Trophy Fish
  - Silver Trophy Fishing Sack can hold Silver Trophy Fish
- Added Hollow Armor, Hollow Wand, all missing Mithril and Gemstone Drills and multiple other Garden tools to the Museum
## ► Bug Fixes
Pet Fixes
- Fixed certain pets getting deleted if attempting to upgrade to an unobtainable tier.
- Fixed transferring soulbound Grandma Wolf pet from Bingo profiles
- Fixed Rock pet giving too much Defense if clicked multiple times
- Fixed unequipping the Bat Pet removing the Night Vision Talisman effect
- Fixed the Rat Pet Extreme Speed Perk
- Fixed how Supernatural and Omen perks from Black Cat pet interact with Chimera enchantment
Garden and Farming
- Fixed Garden quest soft-lock if a player leaves when the tutorial compost is counting down.
- Fixed Basket Of Seeds planting melons and pumpkins on non-soil blocks.
- Fixed crops not growing until rejoining if originally placed on dry soil that later became moist.
- Fixed mushrooms deleting blocks in the world in their ever growing conquest.
- Fixed mushrooms occasionally growing on top of blocks that they shouldn't.
- Fixed players below Jacob's Farming Contest requirements still being able to participate in the contest.
- Fixed melons and pumpkins still growing if changed presets.
- "You haven't unlocked this area yet!" - Added a cooldown to this Sam's Scythe message to prevent spamming.
- Fixed previous sugar cane grieving the plot when changing presets.
- Fixed Jacob and Anita being right next to Sam on a new Garden world.
- In Andrew's Garden commission, clarified that he's talking about in-game time.
Other Fixes
- Fixed /effects loading a blank menu.
- Fixed certain menus overriding items when switching pages
- Fixed several grammatical issues.
- Fixed a Soulbound transfer exploit.
- Fixed being able to salvage Museum soulbound items
- Fixed some issues with Crimson Armor dealing ghost damage
- Fixed launching Spirit Leap by shift right-clicking a chest
- Fixed ending an already finished piece with the Personal Harp redisplaying the Intelligence gain message
- Fixed Shadow Fury not activating on Sven slayer mobs
- Fixed putting a Rune into a Personal Deletor deleting all types of Runes
- Fixed multiple people being able to collect the same Maxor crystal at the same time
- Fixed skill menus displaying the wrong maximum level in the next page icon
- Fixed hitting a training dummy putting the player in combat mode
- Fixed previous page arrow being missing on the second page of the Catacombs reward page
- Fixed Divan Drill not counting previous Drill item tiers in the Museum
- Fixed the Mystical achievement not being properly given
- Fixed Flower of Truth not visually re-appearing in the players inventory if placed on the ground or in a pot
- Fixed Gusher Trophy Fish being unobtainable
- Fixed Sludge Juice not being put into Sacks when dropped with the Jungle Pickaxe
- Fixed Builder playing dialogue sound when using the Go Back button
- Fixed Teleporter Pill placing a skull block when on your island
- Fixed small typo in Sack of Sacks menu
- Fixed Master Tao forgetting that he met you
- Improved Crimson Isle performance
## ► A note regarding Inflation, and balancing changes
Over the past several weeks, we have been closely observing the escalating inflation within SkyBlock. The community has been actively engaging us, asking about our action plan and our general stance on the matter.
First, let's note that the inflation seemed to reach its zenith around May 15th or 16th and is now showing signs of stabilization at a significantly lower level. It's crucial that we take our time to investigate such issues. Large groups of people tend to overreact, causing dramatic economic fluctuations.
Despite this, the current trend indicates that the inflation will stabilize at a level substantially higher than it was before the Garden update. We recognize this issue and want to address it.
One question that has come up is why we increased the NPC Sell prices of Garden Crops.
Before the Garden update, Mining was the most lucrative Skill in the game. We think that, ideally, all skills should compete for coin income to a certain degree. However, this competition doesn't mean equality at all progression stages. For instance, Farming, with its lower learning curve, is expected to reach its income generation "peak" earlier than other skills. New players are encouraged to initially invest in farming to generate coins, and then later divert those funds into mining gear, which provides superior returns in the long run.
Did we achieve our objective? Regrettably, no. Our calculations were erroneous, and we overshot the mark. Farming currently outclasses Mining and other income generation methods for too long during the game progression.
Moreover, our decision to dramatically increase the NPC sell prices all at once, rather than gradually, was misguided. This sudden surge not only exceeded our intended goal but also incited an overreaction that led to the prices soaring for several days. We apologize for this oversight.
Following this incident, we identified four main causes behind the recent inflation:
1. The sudden surge in NPC Sell prices of the Garden Crops.
2. Garden content distracting players from coin sinks (generating coins in the economy rather than spending coins)
3. Decrease in specific RNG drops or valuable items due to fewer players doing those activities at the time, thereby lowering the supply.
4. RNG Meter bug fix, causing an increase in prices of certain Dungeon items.
In response to these issues, we are implementing three major balancing changes today.
1/ We are reducing the NPC Sell Prices of the most problematic Garden Crops:
- Carrot: 4 -> 3 Coins
- Nether Wart: 5 -> 4 Coins
- Potato: 4 -> 3 Coins
- Sugar Cane: 5 -> 4 Coins
While we believe that Farming should be the best early game method for generating coins in SkyBlock, its current strength needs to be rebalanced. These specific crop adjustments should bring Farming closer to its intended power level. We will be monitoring coins earned from farming following this to see if more changes are needed.
2/ We are modifying the weights and chest costs of a few Dungeon drops:
Here's the list of changes:
- Increased Necron’s Handle weight from 13 → 15 in Floor 7 and 15 → 18 in M7 (Bedrock Chests)
  - Essentially, this means they will be a bit more common.
- Increased the three scroll’s weights from 14 → 20 in Floor 7 and 14 → 25 in M7
  - This is a pretty substantial change, especially in Master Mode.
- Increased the chest price of Necron’s Handle to 100m instead of 35m.
- Increased the chest price of each Wither scrolls to 50m each, instead of 15m.
- Increased the chest price of Recombobulator 3000 by 1 extra million.
- Increased the chest price of the Dark Claymore to 150m, instead of 50m.
- Floor 1 and Floor 2 Recombobulator 3000 prices are now adjusted to be the same as the other floors.
- Increased the weights of Recombobulators in many floors, so they are easier to obtain.
  - F1: Up recomb weight from 1 → 3 (1/100 to 3/102 [2.9%]) in OBSIDIAN
  - F2: Up recomb weight from 1 → 2 (1/57 to 2/58 [3.3%]) in OBSIDIAN
  - F3: Up recomb weight from 2 → 4 (2/85 to 4/87 [3.5%]) in OBSIDIAN
  - F4: Up recomb weight from 2 → 3 (2/82 to 3/83 [3.7%]) in OBSIDIAN
  - F6: Up recomb weight from 60 → 100 (60/2729 to 100/2769 [4.3%]) in BEDROCK
  - F7: Up recomb weight from 200 → 400 (200/13489 to 400/13689 [3.7%]) in BEDROCK
  - M1: Up recomb weight from 1 → 4 (1/79 to 4/82 [4.8%]) in OBSIDIAN
  - M2: Up recomb weight from 1 → 3 (1/57 to 3/59 [5.1%]) in OBSIDIAN
  - M3: Up recomb weight from 2 → 4 (2/66 to 4/68 [4.5%]) in OBSIDIAN
  - M4: Up recomb weight from 2 → 3 (2/57 to 3/58 [5.2%]) in OBSIDIAN
  - M6: Up recomb weight from 60 → 120 (60/2749 to 120/2809 [4.3%]) in BEDROCK
  - M7: Up recomb weight from 200 → 500 (200/13558 to 500/13858 [3.6%]) in BEDROCK
Our goal is to ensure that as we roll out more updates, the time required to "complete" SkyBlock doesn't extend indefinitely. By making these items more accessible, we're making the game slightly easier to complete, thereby saving valuable time. This is particularly true for Recombobulators, as each new Accessory we add to the game increases the total number of these items you need. Now is a great time to make these more obtainable.
As for the chest price changes, they were overdue. What once seemed like a large sum at the release of Floor 7, 35 million, no longer does. Dropping any of those items will still be a massive profit, and even for ironman players, obtaining 100m now is much easier than it was back then.
3/ We are increasing the BIN Auction Listing Fees:
Until now, BIN auction listing fees have always been 1%, no matter the cost of the item. Whereas the regular auction listing fees have been 5%.
It makes sense that the BIN fees are lower than the auction fees, as they technically already target the final price, however we feel like 1% is too small.
From now on those are the new BIN auction listing fees.
- 1% for items <10,000,000 coins (unchanged)
- 2% for items <100,000,000 coins
- 2.5% for items >100,000,000 coins.
We anticipate that these changes will have a notable impact on the global economy and, overall, will significantly benefit the game.
Rest assured, we will continue to monitor inflation following these changes, as we believe they're a step in the right direction.

# Changelog/2023/November 14
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
0.19.7
SkyBlock Patch Notes 0.19.7 - Garden Pests
Updated: November 14, 2023
Hello!
Welcome to SkyBlock Patch 0.19.7. Following a fruitful period of Alpha testing, today's update includes a whole bunch of creepy crawlies that will be making their way into your Garden. Read on to find out more!
A Note about Today’s Update:
As many of you are aware, this update originally landed on Alpha with the focus of introducing the core Pests system to players. We have a strong idea of our goals with this update: to create a more active gameplay loop for farming. To put it simply - we are releasing this update not only to create engaging gameplay into Farming, but also promote active play - while we do not want to completely stop the practice of playing The Garden while, for example, watching shows or talking with friends, we are hoping this update reduces the strength of botting and macroing within the Garden while improving the experience for everyone else.
Over the last couple days, we received an immense amount of feedback from sources such as Forums, Reddit, Discord and more, which allowed us to make dozens of impactful changes that have massively enhanced this updated compared to what was released to Alpha mere days ago. The Pests update, which we are releasing today, is a massive improvement, and we will continue to keep our eyes peeled for any further feedback as we make additional changes over the coming days.
We would also note that what players have come to refer to as a “Farming Jiggle” will not be enabled on live release.
## ► Pests
Starting today, players who have reached Garden Level V will encounter a variety of pests spawning around their Garden. From Rats to Mosquitos, there are ten types of pest! Different pests have different behaviors, and drop different items!
As you farm, Pests will spawn slowly over time, and if there are 4 pests or more in your Garden at once, you will lose some Farming Fortune!
But pests are not all that bad! The more you defeat them, the bigger the buff you can claim - so make sure you vacuum all your pests! There are also tools you can use to reduce pests spawning, or spawn way more pests, your choice!
### Pesthunter Phillip
Upon players reaching Garden Level V, Pesthunter Philip, who recently moved into the Garden along with his wife, will give players their first vacuum in exchange for helping him catch an elusive pest. Players can vacuum pests and offer them to Philip in exchange for a limited-time buff to their Farming Fortune. The more pests deposited, the more fortune!
### Pesthunter Pamela
Beloved wife of Philip and a long-time lover of Pests, Pamela’s approach is a little more…delicate. Pamela will give players the Sprayonator, which they can use to attract certain types of Pests more frequently in the hopes of obtaining their rare drops.
## ► New SkyMart Items
Get your Coppers ready; several new items have been added to SkyMart! Between a vacuum upgrade line, some barn skins, and pest repellant, there’s something for everyone!
## ► Pest Drops and a New Drop System
Each Pest has its own unique drop table. For example, the Rat drops:
- Enchanted Pumpkin (100%)
- Compost (4%)
- Honey Jar (4%)
- Dung (4%)
- Plant Matter (4%)
- Tasty Cheese (4%)
- Rodent Revolution Vinyl (2%)
- Polished Pumpkin (1%)
- LEGENDARY Rat Pet (0.2%)
Farming Fortune works on crop drops with a 5% drop chance or more, multiplying the amount of crops you receive. For example, the Rat Pest has a 100% chance to drop at least 1 Enchanted Pumpkin, so if you have a total of 1000 Farming and Pumpkin fortune, you will drop 11 Enchanted Pumpkins! How does a rat carry 11 pumpkins? You shouldnt be asking us those kinds of questions.
For Pest drops with a 5% or lower chance, a new formula has been devised that utilizes Farming Fortune in place of Magic Find. Simply put, Farming Fortune increases your chances of getting rare drops. The higher your Farming Fortune stat, the higher your chances of getting rarer drops.
## ► New Garden Stat: Bonus Pest Chance
Bonus Pest Chance is a new stat that gives you a chance to spawn 2, or even 3 pests instead of 1! This is for players who wish to farm pests most efficiently.
## ► Changes to Jacob’s Medals
As part of this update, we have adjusted the brackets within Farming Contests in an attempt to create more winners! Everyone loves winners. We don’t talk about losers.
- The Gold Medal bracket for Jacob’s Contests has been increased from top 5% to 10%.
- The Silver Medal bracket for Jacob’s Contests has been increased from top 25% to 30%.
In addition to these changes, we have added two new brackets for the farming elite to compete for:
DIAMOND: Top 2% of participants.
- Awards 1 Gold Medal, 1 Silver, and 35 Jacob’s Tickets.
PLATINUM: Top 5% of participants.
- Awards 1 Gold Medal, 1 Bronze, and 30 Jacob’s Tickets.
In addition to this, collecting all unique platinum medals will award players with a gray emblem, while completing a diamond set will award a golden one.
As more and more players get more Farming Fortune and gear up, getting Gold Medal has become harder, especially for newer players. Due to this, we are adjusting the existing medal brackets to allow for more players to obtain the necessary progression rewards from Jacob, while adding 2 new brackets to recognize and reward the true top players accordingly.
## ► Reduced Crop Milestones
As things stand, maxing the Garden takes a bit too long compared to other skills, with the majority of this time being spent grinding out individual crop milestones. As a result of this, we have further reduced all crop milestones to make the grind a bit less harsh. Though this is a pretty small change, we think it will go a long way in the long-term.
## ► New Anita Items!
Anita has added some new goodies to her shop!Her new accessory line grants players Farming Fortune for a random crop during each Jacob’s Contest.She also introduces a new Personal Best menu that grants extra Crop Fortune based on your own records in Jacob’s Contests! Beating your personal best now makes you permanently better at farming that crop!
## ► A Mythic Visitor
As part of this update, a new visitor named Maeve may rarely appear in your Garden if you are Garden XII or higher. If you’re able to fulfil her request, Maeve will gift you a Harvest Harbringer potion, which grants 50 Farming Fortune for the next 25 minutes. With this potion, the sky’s the limit for your Jacob’s Contest personal bests!
Producer’s Note: Crops don’t grow in the sky.
## ► New Barn Skins
We have also added two Legendary new Barn skins to the game as part of this update to make sure that you can continue farming in style! You can purchase them from SkyMart for gems now.
## Other Changes
With this update comes several changes that look to improve the overall SkyBlock experience, with a particular emphasis placed upon The Garden.
### Garden Changes
- Improved accuracy of Jacob’s Contest counter to prevent players from inflating numbers by having co-op members farm crops with a full inventory and letting them pick the crops up.
- Text messages output when switching modes with the Fungi Cutter are now clearer.
- Increased the base rarity of the Fungi Cutter from rare to epic.
- When in the Garden, crop-specific Farming Fortune is now shown beneath the general Farming Fortune line in the tab list.
- Surpassing your previous personal best in a Jacob’s Contest will now inform you via a chat message. An extra message will also play once the contest concludes.
- Compost now has a “Right-click to view recipes” feature so that crafting a Compost Bundle is more intuitive.
- Added 10 new Fortune stats - one for each crop. A player’s crop-specific fortune is now added to their base Farming Fortune to determine their final fortune for the crop they’re farming.
  - Turbo Crop Enchantments, Garden Crop Upgrades, Mathematical Hoes, Carrolyn NPC, Cocoa Chopper, and Garden Talisman line all now grant crop-specific fortune.
- Disabled using Beds to setspawn in The Garden.
### ► Bug Fixes
#### Combat Fixes
- Fixed level 10 Emerald Slimes having lower drop chance for Exp Share Core than level 5 Emerald Slimes.
- Fixed Champion enchantment not levelling up multiple levels at once when gaining enough Combat XP for multiple levels.
- Fixed being able to sell Elle's supplies using /trades during Kuudra.
- Fixed the missing holograms in The End's shop and certain parkours.
- Fixed Vampire Slayer's Mania ability damage when standing on the wrong blocks.
- Fixed being able to convert the Werewolf Armor to a Dungeon Item without having its catacomb requirement.
#### Farming Fixes
- Fixed Sam not appearing on private islands under specific conditions.
- Fixed being able to level up Cultivating Enchantment by breaking blocks that are not crops.
- Fixed the Guest Management menu always missing a guest.
#### Mining Fixes
- Fixed a collection exploit using Smelting Touch.
- Fixed errors related to Mining Fiestas and Ender Node drops.
#### Fishing Fixes
- Fixed a way to rod swap with an open inventory.
#### Misc. Fixes
- Fixed part of the Dungeon Hub Portal not warping correctly to the Hub.
- Fixed being able to use /gfs in combat.
- Fixed one of the Slime Launch Pads in the Dwarven Mines not working.
- Fixed a spot in the Crypts displaying as "None" on the scoreboard.
- Fixed Bat pet Candy Lover perk not working correctly.
- Fixed a deadzone in the bounding box of Rift's Village Plaza letting players roam freely without the timecharm.
- Fixed Crab Hat / Sloth Hat bonus stats while worn on head as a hatcessory.
#### User Interface and Messaging Fixes
- Fixed Crafted Minions Menu action text missing an exclamation mark.
- Fixed being able to search for Master Mode Entrance in the Dungeon Party Finder.
- Fixed the Graveyard entrance showing as being in the Village on the scoreboard.
- Changed the Dungeon Reward Chests text to explain that Coins are taken from Purse if you have enough Coins available.
- Fixed line spacing issue in the Vargul Milestones Menu.
- Fixed a formatting issue in Bazaar Cookies Menu.
- Fixed a text discrepency in the anvil.

# Changelog/2023/November 28
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
0.19.8
SkyBlock Patch Notes 0.19.8 - Winter Update and Secret Bingo
Updated: November 28, 2023
Hello!
Welcome to SkyBlock Patch 0.19.8. Today's update contains a new Advent Calendar, new gifting rewards on Jerry’s Workshop, Secret Bingo, several bug fixes, and more!
## ► Jerry’s Workshop
It’s that magical time of year again! The air is crisp and chilly, the streets twinkle with festive lights, people are bustling with holiday saplings, and SkyBlock has a new Jerry’s Workshop patch!
Like each December, we have a look at our Winter content and improve what needs improving. Here’s what we have done this year:
The festivities begin now! Explore the island's winter wonderland throughout the month.
### 2023 Advent Calendar
Similarly to last year's Advent Calendar, players will be able to claim a gift reward every day of December until the 25th.
This year, you can also claim a second and third daily reward by playing SkyBlock each day and gifting other players!
### Meet Generow, the Gift-Master Penguin
Generow, our charming new NPC on Jerry's Workshop, is here to spread the joy of giving.
The more unique players you donate gifts to during the month of December, the more milestones you’ll reach in Generow. The new rewards include:
- Frozen Bait (an improved version of Ice Bait)
- Gift of Learning (Soulbound item letting you choose a skill to gain EXP in)
- A way to obtain Einary’s Abiphone contact
- A unique Memento item
- A new emblem
- Many, many gifts!
Generow will vanish from the face of SkyBlock in early 2024, so don’t forget to claim your rewards!
### New Bottles of Jyrre
Everybody’s favorite item(?) is back!
But this time it’s actually good.
- Old Bottles of Jyrre you may own are now legacy items.
- Once maxed, the new bottle of Jyrre evolves and can grant you a permanent 1 Intelligence, and 2 SkyBlock XP.
- You can only obtain that permanent buff 5 times.
- It takes a total of 300 hours to max it out, and all you have to do is keep it in your inventory.
- The Bottle does not reset anymore if you place it in a chest.
- Oruo increments the timer of the Bottle by 1 full hour each time you complete his puzzle.
- The Old Bottles of Jyrre are now removed from Sherry’s Shop, and you can obtain the new ones from gifts.
### New Boss Fight Reward
The final gift pile of every Battle of Jerry Mountain event will now grant a Volcanic Rock if the pile was intact. You can now use the Volcanic Rock in Einary’s Shop to purchase new items:
- A new Ultimate Enchant: Refrigerate!
- A Magic Top Hat (used to upgrade the Snowman Pet to Mythic)
- Winter Fragments (used to upgrade the Yeti Sword, Glacial Scythe, and Ice Spray Wand.
### Other Winter Changes
- There are now more Gifts being rewarded in each Gift Pile during the main Event.
- The Ice Essence Shop has a new perk, allowing you to increase the Odds to find a Reindrake
- The Reindrake is 50% more likely to be found by default
- The Glacial Cave loot table now includes new items, such as the Frozen Bait and Einary’s Red Hoodie.
- The smallest Gift and EXP rewards from gifts have been removed, to increase their value.
Enjoy these wintery enhancements and have a splendid time in Jerry's Workshop!
## ► Secret Bingo
To celebrate the 2nd anniversary of SkyBlock Bingo, a special Secret Bingo is being held in December! To complete this bingo, you must figure out the goals and complete them. Do you have the smarts to find the solutions? Talk to Bingo in the SkyBlock Hub to get started!
Here’s some top-secret information that could be useful:
- Secret Bingo is considered a special Bingo card! This means:
  - Secret Bingo will last for 14 days.
  - The points awarded for each goal and row/column completion is double the regular amount.
- The names of Personal Goals will be revealed once the Bingo event starts.
  - The names of Community Goals have been revealed in advance for you to strategize. Good luck!!
- Three hints will be released for each Bingo goal over the course of the event.
  - Hint reveals are staggered per hour, they are not all revealed at once.
  - Hovering over a goal will tell you which hints are already revealed and when the next hint reveal will occur for that goal.
## ► Other Changes
### General Changes and Enhancements
- Added an autopet kill entity rule for Mythological Creatures.
- Added an autopet kill entity rule for Sea Creatures.
- Swappable skins (such as Gemstone Divan) now open a menu, instead of having to spend a century cycling through each skin.
- Swappable skins can now have each individual skin previewed while it's in the Community Shop.
- Added the Alchemist as an Abiphone Contact.
- Added /warp trap as an alias for the Trapper's Den.
- Added a warning when picking up a minion with consumable fuel.
- Added a message when unlocking a new Guide Tier in the SkyBlock Guide.
- You can now hover over the header of the salvage text in chat to view a list of the items you salvaged.
- Added Flaming Sword as a 1% drop chance from Redstone Pigman.
- Buffed the amount of Ice Walker spawns.
- Replaced the “Coming Soon” items in collections with Skill XP.
- They will no longer be coming soon.
- Gold Forger now asks for 1024 Golden Carrots instead of 1028.
- Some accessories have been made soulbound instead of co-op soulbound,
- This is introduced prevent co-op members from being able to bypass content because their co-op members trade them a talisman from some questline that they have not done.
- The museum zone has been adjusted to be slightly lower to not interfere with Diana burrow digging.
- Renamed the Ink Sack category in the Bazaar to Ink Sac to match the items within it.
### Item Changes and Fixes
- Added a Jasper gemstone slot to the upgraded (fragged) version of Shadow Assassin Armor to fix an issue where Jasper gemstones are deleted upon upgrading the armor.
- Fixed Sorrow armor not giving the correct duration of invisibility.
- Fixed being able to teleport through locked Dungeon doors with Shadow Fury ability.
- Fixed losing an Archfiend Dice if a player left the server while it was rolling.
- Fixed the Bone Reaver and the Zombie Sword items sharing the same charge cooldowns.
- Fixed Dark Candy not being able to go in the Trick or Treat bag.
- Fixed Master Stars not being applicable on Dungeon equipment.
- Fixed being able to apply enchantments to items with base enchantments like the Rookie Pickaxe.
- Fixed an item swap exploit with Smite and the Bonemerang.
- Lore, Description, and Visual Improvements
- Fixed name of Ender Armor Set in Museum.
- Fixed the formatting of The One enchantment lore.
- Fixed a small issue with roof on default Garden barn.
## ► Bug Fixes
### Combat Fixes
- Fixed Lion pet First Pounce perk not working as intended.
- Fixed the 'You only have X flint arrows left in your Quiver!' message appearing when the player is not using Flint Arrows.
### Mining Fixes
- Fixed an issue where some players couldn't complete the Crystal Nucleus.
- Fixed a weird case where the Jade Crystal would not appear in the Mines of Divan if the player left the server before picking it up.
- Fixed the Crystal Vault hologram disappearing from the Crystal Nucleus when everyone left the zone after server boot.
- Fixed Sky Mall perks not rotating if the player was outside of a Mining Island. Players will get a notification about their Sky Mall outside of Mining Islands.
- Added some extra wording to the Sky Mall message reflecting this change.
- Fixed the color of the Crystal Hollows in the game being mixed, it is now always dark purple.
- Fixed Fetchur accepting any rocket-material item for firework rocket tasks.
### Farming Fixes
- Fixed items being compacted before being replanted, resulting in some blocks not being replanted.
- Fixed Vacuums not being able to work through some blocks.
- Fixed misleading text about pelts.
### Fishing Fixes
- Fixed Expertise enchantment not levelling up the fishing rod that was used to fish the Sea Creature.
### Misc. Fixes
- Fixed being able to mount other player's pets.
- Fixed being able to equip spawned pig pets with a saddle.
- Fixed not being able to take some items back out of furnaces without breaking the furnace.
- Fixed some non-vanilla items being smeltable in furnaces.
- Fixed some non-vanilla items being able to be used as furnace fuel.
- Fixed fire ticks not being reset when the player entered the Matriarch.
- Fixed the Pickobulus pick ability being able to break End Portal Frames.
- Fixed the Bee Pet's absorption still being active when entering a Dojo Challenge.
- Fixed Primal Fears being able to spawn in some Dojo challenges.
- Fixed the Experimentation Table not rewarding Fame when the player used Bits to renew it.
- Fixed being able to put a lead on Iron Golems and then take them on a magical journey.
- Fixed guests being able to pick up candy from the Spooky Tree on player's private islands.
- Fixed Reaper Peppers creating null head when consumed while looking at the ground.
- Fixed Hemovibe displaying the wrong error when placing in a Personal Compactor.
- Fixed left-click activating bed spawns and suspicious soup.
- Fixed the SkyBlock Guide displaying roman numerals for dungeon levels when they’re disabled.
- Fixed the cookie menu showing the incorrect unlock level for the Bazaar.
- Fixed Centauri not maxing dungeon levels.
- Fixed a missing water block on the Rift.
- Fixed an inescapable area in the Hub.
### User Interface and Messaging Fixes
- Fixed auction time incorrectly being set to 1 minute when entering an invalid duration.
- Fixed the Bazaar manage orders description showing the incorrect value.
- Fixed Commissions and Slayers listing bits in their rewards even when the player didn't have a cookie buff active.

# Changelog/2023/November 30
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
This post contained non-SkyBlock information, which has been omitted for clarity.
ANNOUNCEMENT
Happy Holidays from Hypixel!
Updated: November 30, 2023
Boy is it cold out! Our lobbies have only just received their first frost, but the holidays are just around the corner! This holiday season we're bringing in some Holiday Bingo tasks, a bunch of new maps, new cosmetics, and more! Here's a full list!
- Holiday Sale - Up to 30% off!
- Holiday Bingo Cards
- Event Shop Updates
- Holiday Lobbies with Present Hunt
  - Main Lobby
  - Bed Wars
  - SkyWars
  - Housing
  - Prototype
- Main Lobby Ice Fishing Returns
- Advent Calendars starting December 1st!
- New Bed Wars and SkyWars Maps
- New SkyBlock Holiday Rank Bundles
Read on for all the details!
## ► Holiday Sale
Our Holiday Sale is now active! It will offer up to a 30% discount on everything in the store in-game, while the web store will offer up to a 25% discount.
Check out the web store here!
## ► Holiday Bingo
Bingo continues this Holiday season with new festive objectives! These winter-ified tasks will only be available during the Holiday Event, so bundle up and hop into our various minigames! We've got some brand-new rewards for each card; the Easy card gets the Epic Baby White Rabbit Pet, the Medium card has the Legendary Snow Click Effect, and the Hard card has the Legendary Packed Ice Pet for when you feel like taking part of the winter-season home with you!
### ► New Cosmetics
As with every event, we've stocked up the Event Shop with tons of new cosmetics! We've got a lot of different options this time around, so make sure you get that Silver!
North Star Backpack Skin, Snowball Spirit Pet Skin, & Gift Backpack Skins
New Sprays
New Furniture
### ► SkyBlock Bundles
Our Rank Bundles for the Holidays have been refreshed with our brand-new Sprays and Furniture! Claim them before the event ends!
Thanks for reading and we hope everyone has an amazing holiday season!
