# Changelog/2023/October 24
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
0.19.6
SkyBlock Patch Notes 0.19.6 - The Great Spook, Essence Shops, & More
Updated: October 24, 2023
Hello!
Welcome to SkyBlock Patch 0.19.6. This update contains permanent Spooky content in the form of The Great Spook, several additions and changes to Essence Shops, and much more!
## ► Great Spook 2023
This year, the arcane master known as the Tyashoi Alchemist re-emerges from the veil of mystery to aid players in banishing The Great Spook!
Will you succeed or completely fail? Time will tell!
Like the previous year, you have access to the 2021 and 2022 hunts and their items.
However the 6 new hunts will be revealed day by day, starting from today.
Click on the items in his shop to reveal the clues to find them, good luck!
In a shocking twist, The Great Spook will unleash its terror on the 6th day, once all new hunts are completed. Turns out, the Tyashoi Alchemist is actually terrible at his job; he may have actually accelerated the arrival of The Great Spook!
New Mechanics: Primal Fears and Dark Candies
During the Great Spook Event, players will occasionally encounter Primal Fears while defeating mobs. These entities are simple to defeat but beware—they can manifest as YOUR primal fear. From Math Teachers to Commitment Phobia, each Primal Fear has a unique trick for defeating it. Milestones and Rewards Defeating Primal Fears will drop Dark Candies, and you'll also find the Tombstone of Vargul the Unearthed at the back of Hub Island. Donating enough Dark Candies to Vargul will unlock various new items. Extra Spooky Events Scheduled Like the previous years, we added 6 extra Spooky Events in the SkyBlock Calendar, between the 28th and the 31st of October. Look inside the SkyBlock Calendar in your SkyBlock Menu if you don’t want to miss them! Good luck, and don't get spooked!
## ► Essence Shops
As part of this update, the final two essence shops will be making their long-awaited appearance. In addition, several changes have been made that will make other essences easier to get and also add a few new perks to existing shops. Read on to learn more!
### Gold Essence Shop
A direct descendant of King Midas himself, Marigold has finally arrived at the Royal Mines. Players who help her catch a pesky thief will be rewarded with access to the Gold Essence shop. Here, players may trade gold essence for a variety of perks that will help them in a number of ways, such as by granting bonus mining speed or increasing the chances of worms and scatha spawning when mining.
### Diamond Essence Shop
Additionally, Gemma has set up shop in the Crystal Nucleus! Players who prove their worth by completing a Crystal Nucleus run will be rewarded with access to the Diamond Essence Shop. Here, players may trade in diamond essence to gain useful perks, such as bonus breaking power on diamond pickaxes, increased chances of fishing up diamond trophy fish, and a higher chance of spawning diamond goblins. Players may also purchase an item that can be applied in an anvil to upgrade any piece of Tier XII Perfect Armor to Tier XIII.
### Diamond Goblins
Diamond Goblins are an exceedingly rare type of Goblin that have a chance to spawn in place of Golden Goblins. When killed, they will always drop diamond essence, mithril powder, and gemstone powder. Some lucky players may find themselves dropping an item that can be used to upgrade a particular pet to mythic.
### Other Changes to Essence Shops
We have also made several changes related to essence shops that give players new ways to gain essence and new perks to spend essence on.
- New Perk: Unbridled Rage added to Dragon Essence shop. Increases Berserk class XP gain.
- New Perk: Toxophilite added to Spider Essence shop. Increases Archer class XP gain.
  - When taking into account the perks of the two new essence shops, every dungeons class now has access to a perk that allows them to gain class XP faster.
- Gold & Diamond Essence can now drop from Treasure Chests in the Crystal Hollows.
- Golden Goblins may now spawn in the Crystal Hollows.
- Goblin Eggs of all kinds can now be thrown and have varying chances of spawning a Golden Goblin:
  - 2% chance from Goblin Egg
  - 2.5% chance from Green Goblin Egg
  - 3.33% chance from Yellow Goblin Egg
  - 5% chance from Red Goblin Egg
  - 10% chance from Blue Goblin Egg
- Golden Goblins now have a 0.1% chance of spawning when mining Gemstones, Mithril, or Titanium in the Crystal Hollows.
- Golden Goblins now have a 0.01% chance of spawning when mining Hard Stone.
- Golden Goblins no longer can spawn from cobblestone and vanilla ores.
- Gold & Diamond Dungeon Heads are no longer salvageable.
- A salvage function has now been added to the Spider Tamer NPC.
- Dungeon secret loot now includes Ice Essence, Spider Essence, Diamond Essence, and Gold Essence.
- A guide menu has been added to the Crystal Nucleus that tells players where to find each Gemstone Crystal.
## ► Other Changes
### New Equipment Reforges
We are introducing basic reforges for equipment!. Simply bring your equipment pieces to a Blacksmith or anvil near you and pay some coins to roll a random basic reforge for your equipment.
New Equipment Reforge Stats (in order of rarity)
### General Changes
- Increased Sack capacities for Small and Medium tier sacks.
  - Small Sacks 640 → 2,240
  - Medium Sacks 2,240 → 6,720
- Whale Bait is now a lot more effective in fishing up rare Lava Sea Creatures, now only boosting the chances of fishing up Lava Flame, Fire Eel, Taurus, Thunder, and Lord Jawbus.
  - Lava Sea Creature weights have all been quadrupled to reach this effect.
- Using at least 7x Recombobulated Summoning Eyes will now guarantee a Superior Dragon.
- Your Crimson Essence is now shown in the scoreboard in the Forgotten Skull zone.
- Flower Minions are now categorized as Foraging minions as they grant Foraging experience.
- Maddox will now open his menu directly when called with the abiphone/batphone instead of having to click in chat.
- Slayer bosses now show their tier in their nametag.
- Changed Ender Monocle Combat Requirement from 20 to 15.
- Added “Sort by Pet Exp” as a sorting parameter within the pets menu.
- Added /warp winter, /warp jerry and /warp workshop commands to warp to Jerry’s Workshop (alias of /savethejerrys).
- Clicking on a minion in your inventory while looking at the Crafted Generators menu will now open its recipes page.
- Gulliver's Red Gift can now be earned once per profile rather than once per account.
- Added COMMON Kuudra pet to Rusty after unlocking Kuudra Boss Collection I (removed the confusing way to re-obtain from doing Kuudra).
### Item Changes and Fixes
- Upgraded the Fire Veil Wand's particle effect, while making it support /pq and having 5x less particles.
- Fixed Jinxed and regular Voodoo Dolls not sharing their ability cooldown.
- Piggy Bank now no longer breaks if you have at least two pieces of armor enchanted with Bank V.
- InfinityBoom TNT’s rarity has been changed from RARE to EPIC, making it easier to distinguish from regular superboom.
- Updated Lava Talisman lore to reflect the fact that it doesn't work on heat damage caused by standing in Lava in the Magma Fields of the Crystal Hollows.
- Updated the "Present Stack" furniture item texture.
- Added Concentrated Stone to the Enchanted Mining Sack.
- Added a sell price of 1,000 coins to the Prehistoric Egg.
- Added a 1s internal cooldown to using the Wishing Compass to avoid double-clicking.
- Added a 5s cooldown to the Rogue Sword.
- Duplicate Rift Timecharms may now be sold for 99 motes IF they've been secured in the Gallery.
  - This allows players to get rid of extra Rift Timecharms they might have accidentally crafted.
- Fixed Fel Sword and Raider Axe kills incrementing on bow kills.
- Purchase confirmation check of 1,000,000 coins will now use the base amount instead of the value modified by the Seal of the Family talisman line.
### Lore, Description and Visual Improvements
- Improved the message when fishing Essence on Jerry's Workshop.
- Improved the Luck of the Cave HOTM perk description to list the "rare occurrences" it impacts.
- Moved McGrubber's Burger tasks in the leveling guide.
- Moved Garden armor sets to a nicer spot in the Museum.
- Changed the date formatting shown on captured Cake Souls.
- Fixed inaccurate Crimson Essence description claiming that Crimson Essence can dungeonize armor.
- Fixed the Rift Necklace item name color when reforged.
- Added stained glass to Jerry's updates and deliveries menus.
- Added an indication that Dwarven Mines can be accessed at Mining level 12 in the level up message.
## ► Bug Fixes
#### Combat Fixes
- Fixed Zealot Bruiser Hideout being unlocked from a random location in the End Island Caves.
- Fixed hits from extended Swing Range sometimes not applying damage correctly.
  - This caused unintended behavior, e.g. against Typhoeus and Quazii where hits “don’t deal damage”.
#### Mining Fixes
- Fixed being able to reset your Sky Mall perk when resetting your Heart of the Mountain.
- Fixed being able to give Rhys the same item multiple times by switching instances during the dwarven mines quest.
- Fixed a Mithril Block in the Dwarven Mines which couldn’t be mined.
Dungeon Fixes
- Fixed Ragnarok's damage boost (Berserker class ability) working on all damage instead of melee only.
- Fixed a way to drop multiple Spirit Bows at the same time during Thorn's boss fight.
  - It's a common strategy to abuse this bug to get Spirit Bows faster. While it is not intended for players to get bows in such quick succession, we have taken this opportunity to tweak the boss fight to make it playable without the bug. M4 now has the same spawning mechanics of 1 bear per spawn as F4, requiring slightly more mob kills to spawn each bear. This change is open to further adjustments to spawn the bears faster overall.
#### Misc. Fixes
- Fixed being able to gain additional pet score from having more than 1 Wisp Pet.
- Fixed being able to upgrade Arthur's wheat minion in the hub.
- Fixed the Hub Portal Trade not showing up in the Trades Menu until switching servers.
- Fixed dying in the Void Sepulture giving the Remnant of the Eye message when you don't have the item in your inventory.
- Fixed some locations being marked as "None" on the scoreboard.
- Fixed Shadow Assassin and Adaptive Armor bonus requiring the equipment to be worn.
- Fixed Magical Power not being calculated correctly in specific cases.
- Fixed being able to do damage to Stranded villagers using Flaming Fist while visiting an island.
- Fixed players being unable to use their private islands due to their spawn points being out of bounds.
- Fixed the unobtainable item "Aspect of the Draconic" showing up in the Essence and Gemstone guides.
- Fixed donating the Bone Reaver not crediting the Spirit Sword in the museum.
- Fixed accessing the Hex through the Booster Cookie menu on Stranded.
- Fixed the armor set count display when mixing upgraded and non-upgraded pieces of a an armor set.
- Fixed Jotraeline Greatforge not being aware of the Abiphone Flip being the latest released Abiphone in her contact quest.
- Fixed an inconsistency with viewing details in the slayer menu.
- Fixed clicking Montezuma in The Rift's SkyBlock Menu.
- Fixed the stats breakdown menu sometimes completely rewriting the lore of your items/accessories.
- Fixed Creepers exploding breaking blocks on private islands.
- Fixed Melon Minions not giving the correct amount of collection from melon blocks.

# Changelog/2023/October 3
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
This post contained non-SkyBlock information, which has been omitted for clarity.
ANNOUNCEMENT
Spooktacular Halloween 2023
Updated: October 3, 2023
Trick or treat? Today starts the Hypixel Halloween Event! We have some spooky new content, including the return of Bingo, new Event Shop cosmetics, and more!
- Halloween Sale - up to 25% off on the in-game store and 20% off the web store!
- Halloween Bingo Cards
- Event Shop Updates
- Murder Mystery Darkfall Revamp
- Spooky Main Lobby Reskin
  - Candy Hunt Returns
  - New Turbo Bat Racers Courses
- Game Lobby Reskins
- New Bed Wars and SkyWars Maps
- New SkyBlock Halloween Rank Bundles
Continue reading for a breakdown of all the new content!
## ► Halloween Sale
Our Halloween Sale is back!! Our Halloween Sale will offer up to 25% discount on everything in the store in-game, while the web store will offer up to 20% discount.
Check out the web store here!
## ► Event Shop Updates
### ► New Cosmetics
As usual, we've stocked up the shop with a boatload of spooky cosmetics. Be sure to earn Halloween Event Levels to get that Silver!
Dynamite Endermite Pet Skin Hero Bat Person Helmet Skin
New Sprays
New Furniture
## ► SkyBlock Bundles
Our Rank Bundles for Halloween have been refreshed with our brand-new Sprays and Furniture! Claim them before the event ends!
## ► The end of an Era: The removal of Random Loot
Along with this update, we are beginning our phased removal of Mystery Boxes and Loot Chests, which we briefly touched on with the summer update last year. As said in that previous thread, we no longer believe randomized loot to be something that favors players anymore, and we are slowly heading to a more direct approach. We currently have a plan that includes three phases, with the first phase being rolled out with this update.
### Phase 1
With the first phase releasing today, we aim to begin the phasing out of Mystery Boxes, primarily seasonal boxes and gifts. As such, we are making the following changes:
- Seasonal Mystery Boxes are no longer available to purchase from the store.
  - All cosmetics previously part of seasonal Mystery Boxes will now be in the Event Shop, starting with the Halloween cosmetics for this event. These can be purchased using Event Silver, obtained by participating in the seasonal event.
- Mystery Box Gift Bundles are no longer available to purchase from the store.
- Generosity will now also be granted from gifting Ranks.
  - When earning experience via Generosity, it will now automatically be granted instead of being added to the Mystery Vault to be claimed.
### Phase 2
In a later update, we will complete the removal of Mystery Boxes by doing the following:
- Mystery Boxes will stop dropping; instead, players will be rewarded directly with Mystery Dust. All non-seasonal cosmetics will be purchasable via dust directly in the collectibles menu. Mystery Boxes will also be removed from the stores.
  - Cosmetics exclusive to Crafted Mystery Boxes will be in an automatically rotating section, allowing them to be purchased for Mystery Dust at a higher cost than other cosmetics.
- Any existing Mystery Boxes, Experience Bags, and Gift Bundles will be automatically opened, and the rewards will be given to the player.
- Mystery Box Gifting rewards and progress will be merged with the Rank Gifting system, allowing these rewards to still be obtained.
### Phase 3
Finally, in a much later update, we will complete the removal of Loot Chests in Bed Wars, SkyWars, Murder Mystery, and Duels. This will include the following changes:
- Loot Chests for these 4 games will stop dropping, and instead, players will be rewarded directly with the appropriate cosmetic currency for these games.
  - All non-seasonal cosmetics will continue to be purchasable at any time via said cosmetic currency.
  - Seasonal cosmetics will be available both via the Event Shop and the game's cosmetic menu during a seasonal event.
    - When purchasing seasonal cosmetics, players will be given the option to either use the game's currency or Event Silver to complete the purchase.
- We are also exploring the potential change of renaming "Coins" in the games that use it for cosmetics to be more clear that the currency is for cosmetics only, such as adopting "Tokens" from SkyWars into the other games.
  - As an example, Bed Wars Coins have always only been used to unlock cosmetic items, unlike SkyWars coins which were used for progression within the game. By renaming Bed Wars Coins to Bed Wars Tokens, we are making these currency names consistent with SkyWars Tokens, which are also only used to purchase cosmetic items.
While these phases are our current plans and a lot of development has already begun to make these changes, they are also subject to change if needed. We don't currently have any public ETA to share for when these changes will be completed and rolled out, but we'll keep you updated as things progress.
We hope you all have a very spooktacular Halloween this year!

# Changelog/2023/October 30
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[October 30] Co-op Management Re-Enabled
Updated: October 30, 2023
Hello.
The Co-op Management Menu has been disabled for a long time following a wipe situation. We've update the functionality of the menu and the /coopadd command. You can access the menu by right-clicking a co-op profile in the /profiles menu.
There's also the new /coopclear command which has been disabled since its creation, which lets you rescind a pending co-op invitation and clear out that historic member slot.
Furthermore, in the backend there is a new system which should reduce issues when there are not enough private worlds available, for example during the raffle event.
That's it, simple!

# Changelog/2023/October 31

[October 31] Great Spook Event Post-Patch Fixes!
Updated: October 31, 2023
Good morning video gamers,

- Made Primal Fears more common:
  - Primal Fears will spawn after 6 minutes, minus 3 seconds for every 1 Fear that you have.

- Messages that make the Public Speaking Demon happy will no longer be sent to chat.

- Added a special emblem for players that give Vargul 600 Dark Candy!
If you have a bug to report to us, please make a report HERE! Thanks to everyone who has reported bugs!

# Changelog/2023/October 4
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
0.19.5
SkyBlock Patch Notes 0.19.5
Updated: October 4, 2023
Hey guys! Here are the patch notes for SkyBlock Patch 0.19.5. Today's update is a fairly minor patch with many bug fixes and adds many quality of life changes!
## ► Inferno Minion Economy Update
Following our announcement thread, we have made the following changes to the Inferno Minion and Gabagool in order to reduce the coins cost associated with Blaze Slayer content:
### Hypergolic Fuel Drop Rates
Inferno Minions running Hypergolic-tier fuel will have higher drop rates:
- 2.8x more [[Inferno Vertex]]
- 20% more [[Inferno Apex]]
- 15% more [[Chili Pepper|Chilli Peppers]]
### Other Cost-Slashing Measures
- Hypergolic-tier fuels now take half the amount of Heavy Fuels (from 24 to 12, removing the corners of the crafting grid)
- Gabagool distillates used to be a RARER drop than other distillates. It is now a more FREQUENT drop than other distillates.
  - Before, each distillate had 900 weight, except 500 for Gabagool
  - Now, each distillate has a 700 weight, except 1400 for Gabagool
- Inferno Minion action speed has been multiplied by 0.92x, meaning it’s about 9% faster.
Furthermore, following feedback from the thread and SkyBlock Nerds, we have made the following changes:
### Capsaicin Eyedrops
We're giving this item a slight change in its recipe, and changing how it functions to allow it to be available in the Bazaar.
- Now has 1 charge and is stackable
- The recipe has been revamped (now includes… Exportable Carrots)
- Now available in the Bazaar under Inferno Minions
All the existing Capsaicin Eyedrops with charges will still exist in the game but aren’t craftable anymore.
### Wisp Pet
We're hoping this pet sees a bit more use by making it more accessible to players by making it cheaper to upgrade along with a small buff.
- Added +10/15/20/25 base damage to the pet
- Upgraded Icehammer to provide healing over time after breaking a pillar.
- Each upgrade recipe has been updated to be generally much cheaper.
### Other Inferno/Blaze Changes
- Inferno Demonlord now has good chances of dropping some Enchanted Blaze Powder as an EXTRA drop.
- The Coldfused Reforge (from [[Entropy Suppressor|Chili Peppers Collection]]) now adds +15/20/25/35/45/55 Strength and +20/30/40/50/60/75 Crit Damage to the daggers.
- Added Very Crude Gabagool item, which can be compacted using an Super Compactor 3000.
- Cayenne Enchant now also grants 0.1 true defense per digit, per enchant level.
- Amalgated Crimsonite recipe will now output 2 at a time (halved cost!)
  - Amalgated Crimsonite can also be crafted en-masse with the enchanted block/cube variants of the materials!
  - Enchanted Glowstone now costs 160 Enchanted Glowstone instead of 196.
## ► Island Entry Requirement and Crimson Isle Changes
We have lowered the requirements to enter the following zones:
- Crimson Isle entry requirement has been lowered from Combat 24 to Combat 22.
  - All items from the Crimson Isle have had their item requirements lowered from Combat 24 to Combat 22.
- Zealot Bruiser Hideout entry requirement has been lowered from Combat 22 to Combat 20.
While the Crimson Isle is intended to be mainly for endgame combat content, there are many other content on the island that players at earlier stages could still enjoy. In order to make the island more hospitable for newer players, we have nerfed the HP and damage of most mobs in the Crimson Isle. Combat EXP gained from these mobs have also been adjusted accordingly. Hopefully these changes will allow more players to enjoy the Crimson Isle’s expansive content!
## ► General Changes
- The Mythological Ritual will now remember which last tier of Griffin pet you had equipped when generating your burrows, avoiding a common problem where players had to dig through 15-25 COMMON quality burrows if they forgot to equip their Legendary Griffin pet when joining the hub.
  - Added a way to reset your burrows in case it's still happening, or if you want easier burrows after upgrading your Griffin and realizing the mobs are maybe a bit too tough:
- Revamped Shen's menu to avoid unintended high bidding.
- Added sorting to the Abiphone contacts menu.
- Modified the Teleport Pad item to not allow you to teleport to a location if the location was obstructed.
- Changed the Auto-Pet item lore to indicate that it can also be consumed by left-click.
- Changed the names of the Guildford Quest Objectives to show which race settings are required.
- Changed the Guildford Quest in the Quest Log to open a new menu that displays your race progress in all the different races.
- Reforge Stones now has the correct description explaining which items it is applicable on instead of just referring to the item as item.
- Adjusted zone boundaries of the Hexatorum.
## ►Bug Fixes
### Item Fixes
- Fixed Enchanted Red Sand Cube incorrectly being categorized as a Farming Recipe.
- Fixed Simple Carrot Candy incorrectly being labelled as a brewing ingredient.
- Fixed Scoville Belt not granting any defense whatsoever.
- Fixed Blaze Belt and Ghast Cloak having the same ability name.
- Fixed enchants/reforges/etc not transferring from the iron pickaxe when crafting a Lapis Pickaxe.
### Combat Fixes
- Fixed some enemies triggering the "On Kill Zombie" auto pet rule when they are not Zombies.
- Fixed cooldown message always appearing when recovering a Summoning Eye.
- Fixed Thunderlord and Wither Impact sometimes applying on already-dead mobs.
- Fixed all Remnants of the Eye in the inventory being consumed when dying in the Voidling Sepulture.
- Fixed Brood Mother not respawning when using the Aspect of the Dragons ability.
- Fixed Slayer Quest showing boss alive when it's already dead.
### Dungeon Fixes
- Fixed absorption hearts persisting after leaving a Dungeon.
- Fixed Help of the Fairies perk not reviving yourself or other party members.
- Fixed dying with a crystal during the Maxor fight (Dungeons Floor VII) softlocking your party.
- Fixed an issue where Mort would say you need Combat Level 5 to start a Dungeon, when it reality you need Dungeon Level 15.
### Mining Fixes
- Fixed the hoverable tooltip on Mining Commission Milestone items erroring out when clicked.
- Fixed Mithril Crystals not working on several mining minions.
- Jungle Temple chests now spawn loot per player; in other words, one person's loot will not be the same as another's in the same lobby.
- Fixed the Slime Hat and other anti-knockback item effects persisting when not using them.
- Fixed Kalhuiki Tribe Members not acknowledging ability attacks as attacks on them.
- Disabled the Rat Pet in the Jungle Temple.
### Farming Fixes
- Fixed the 'Next Tier' button on Pig Minion XI missing text about where to get the next tier.
- Fixed Bits awarded from fulfilling Garden visitor requests being inconsistent with what you actually get.
- Fixed Cultivating enchantment not appearing in the Hex.
### Alchemy Fixes
- Fixed Pet Luck potions not rewarding Alchemy XP (now giving 550).
### Fishing Fixes
- Fixed Nutcracker fishing mob not giving Fishing Skill XP when dying.
- Fixed Prismarine Shards not going into the inventory when the player was using the Prismarine Rod.
- Fixed Water Hydras and Magma Slugs not crediting Fishing Exp to the player that fished them if they were killed by another player.
- Fixed hooks from the Phantom Fisherman counting towards the Ultimate Bobbin' Time enchantment.
### Pet Fixes
- Fixed Pets having a tooltip when viewing from the Profile Viewer.
- Fixed Bingo Pet perk "Consumer" not increasing Alixer splash potions.
### Misc. Fixes
- Fixed being able to kill villagers on other players' Stranded islands with Grappling Hook.
- Fixed Day/Night Crystals not being auctionable and changed their icons to custom skins.
- Fixed wardrobe sounds when clicking your own inventory.
- Fixed not being able to use the Caducous Feeder on pets with maxed pet candies.
- Fixed Ditto Skin NPC dialogue not working for guests.
- Fixed the "Go Back" button in the Rune Sack menu changing to a "Close" button when taking out runes.
- Fixed being able to obtain a Null Red Sandstone Slab item when using a Block Zapper on it.
- Fixed the Block Zapper only giving back 1 Red Sandstone Slab when Zapping a Red Sandstone Block made from 2 Slabs.
- Fixed sometimes getting the Auto Recombobulator trigger message when no items were dropped.
- Fixed Cake Souls not getting picked up by stash.
- Fixed the "Go Back" button in the storage menu sometimes disappearing.
- Fixed Plumber's Sponge not working on 1x1x1 water blocks.
- Fixed being able to cheese Hard Mode in the Mirrorverse lava parkour.
- Fixed bags playing their respective sounds when they were not able to be opened.
- Fixed Egg Hunt count increasing even when it cannot be placed.
- Fixed some of the Savanna Woodland trees giving Yellow Rocks from the Romero quest.
- Fixed not being able to invite a player to visit your private island while on a public island.
- Fixed a few blocks of the Mirrorverse Zone being visible from above the Mirrorverse.
- Fixed the 'Flip Order' button in the Bazaar not giving feedback when you weren't able to flip the order yet.
- Fixed Faction Rewards giving feedback about getting bits even when the player didn't have bits available.
- Fixed Small and Large Dragon Sack textures being flipped.
- Fixed Ice Rods not being salvageable through the Rare items button in the Salvaging menu.
- Fixed Piscary enchantment showing as obtainable from the Experimentation Table in the Enchantments Guide.
- Fixed Frozen Chicken accessory cost in the Rusty menu being too low relative to other accessories of the same rarity.
- Fixed Cleave enchantment not working on Ghosts in the Dwarven Mines.
- Fixed being able to spawn Golden Goblins in Dungeons.
- Fixed Sleeping Eyes being placeable within Ender Chests and Backpacks.
- Fixed a softlock in the Rift where upgrading the Detective Amog questline weapon can softlock you out of the questline.
- Fixed some items not appearing as a result when searching for them on the Bazaar.
- Fixed Jacobus not properly listing the Accessory Bag Slots from the Redstone Miner.
- Fixed Booster Cookie flight on private islands being retained after Booster Cookie expires.
- Made custom beacons unable to be blown up, as they would drop useless vanilla beacons.
- Fixed many inconsistencies with several buttons in several menus across the game.
- Fixed some spelling, grammar and formatting issues.
That is that!

# Changelog/2023/September 12
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
0.19.4
SkyBlock Patch Notes 0.19.4 - Dungeons, QoL, Bug Fixes
Updated: September 12, 2023
Hello!
Welcome to SkyBlock Patch 0.19.4. Today's patch includes many Dungeons changes and additions, QoL features, bug fixes, and more!
## ► Dungeons
Today’s the day that the Berserk class will rise to glory. That’s right, this is an update to The Catacombs! We’re introducing a bunch of new items and class abilities to make melee Berserk a viable option when plundering The Catacombs. Read more to learn about the changes!
### ► Berserk Changes
- Berserk gains a new ability Weapons Master, effectively making each swing “AOE”.
  - Mobs within this cone will take knockback as if they were damaged by a melee hit.
  - Add a swing range of +0.06 blocks per class level, maximizing at +3 blocks swing range at Berserk 50. This stacks on top of any weapon with swing range.
  - Ferocity has been disabled from proccing when Weapon Master is in effect.
- Berserk’s Lust for Blood ability is rescaled to buff its effect at lower class level and buffs the Dungeon Class Bonus if solo.
  - Let p be amount of damage increase per hit and let m be the damage increase cap.
  - p% = 15% + Berserk Level * 0.6%
  - max% = 250% + floor(Berserk Level / 5) * 70%
- Berserk’s Blood Lust ability will no longer heal for 1.5% of any damage dealt. Instead, heal for 3% of your missing health per melee hit.
  - Procs on Flower of Truth right clicks.
  - Hitting more than one mob at once will not result in additional healing.
- Berserk gains a new passive ability Indomitable, increasing its tankiness to go close range.
  - Gain 5% + (0.1% * Class Level) of Strength as Defense.
- Berserk’s Ragnarok ability now has the following additional effects on top of the current minions:
  - If player’s attack speed is less than 100, set it to 100.
  - If player’s speed is less than 200 + Berserk Level * 4, set their speed to 200 + Berserk Level * 4.
  - Increase their melee damage to all mobs by 1.5x.
- Berserk’s Throwing Axe ability has been buffed as follows:
  - Allow sneak to activate Throwing Axe.
  - Increased the hitbox of Throwing Axe by 0.5 blocks radius.
  - Throwing Axe now pierces through enemies.
#### Other Class Changes
- Archer’s Arrow Damage Bonus has been rescaled to not punish dupe Archer as heavily.
  - Arrow Damage Boost% = 150% + Archer Level * 1.6%
  - +50% Arrow Damage if Dungeon Class Bonus is applied.
- Healer gains a new passive ability: Overheal, allowing it to convert excess healing on a player into an absorption shield.
  - Healing done by a Healer when the player is max health is converted into an absorption shield.
  - The maximum absorption shield is based on the player’s Healer level - 20 + 0.4 per Healer Level.
  - If the healer is the sole healer in the party, calculate max absorption shield as 40 + 0.4 per healer level instead. (maximum 40 + 0.4*50 = 60% overheal absorption shield).
  - Wish now works with Overheal - it fully heals you and gives you the maximum absorption hearts you can get from Overheal + an additional 20%.
- Mage’s Mage Staff’s (beam) dropoff range now starts at 10 instead of 5.
  - It’s indicated that the beam drops off in damage starting from 15 blocks but that’s not the case on live.
- Mage’s Mage Staff’s (beam) hit reg has been increased by 0.5 blocks radius.
- Mage’s Mage Staff’s (beam) cooldown is now affected by attack speed.
- Mage’s Mage Staff’s (beam) range is doubled in M7 P5, allowing players to hit Dragons with Mage Beam easier.
## ► New Stat: Swing Range
Swing Range is now indicated as a stat on your profile. The base Swing Range stat is 3 blocks and can be increased by various longswords. The Swing Range stat on swords are now additive to the base Swing Range. Please note that swing range is a global stat, not just to be used in dungeons.
Swing Range is shown and works accurately to 1.d.p. Swing Range obtained from Dominus stats is also dynamically reflected by the Swing Range stat.
## ► New Items
All the new items from this update can be donated to the Museum. If you can't find certain items (i.e Adaptive Belt and Shadow Assassin Cloak), they need to be donated with their full set.
- [[Bouquet of Lies]] (Upgrade to Flower of Truth)
  - Damage: +220
  - Strength: +300
  - Crit Damage: +50
  - Bouquet of Lies' ability fires a barrage of three roses and ricochets two additional times (compared to the Flower of Truth).
  - Requires F6 Completion
- [[Bone Reaver]] (New longsword crafted with materials obtained from F4)
  - Damage: +235
  - Strength: +90
  - Swing Range: +0.5
  - Right click ability deals damage to enemies in a wide cone.
  - Requires F4 Completion
- [[Felthorn Reaper]] (Upgrade to Bone Reaver)
  - Damage: +295
  - Strength: +135
  - Swing Range: +1
  - Right click ability deals more damage to more enemies in a wider cone.
  - Requires F6 Completion
- [[Balloon Snake]] (New dungeon necklace obtained from F1)
  - Health: +10
  - Intelligence: +10
  - Grants Jump Boost 2 to the player.
  - Requires F1 Completion
- [[Adaptive Belt]] (New dungeon belt obtained from F2)
  - Strength: +5
  - Health: +10
  - Defense: +5
  - Grants additional bonuses based on your selected dungeon class!
    - Berserk: +10 Strength
    - Healer: +10 Health, + 5 Mending
    - Mage: +25 Intelligence
    - Tank: +5 Health, +10 Defense
    - Archer: +2 Crit Chance, +5 Crit Damage
  - Requires F2 Completion
- [[Bone Necklace]] (New Dungeon Necklace obtained from F4)
  - Defense: +35
  - Crit Chance: +3
  - This necklace gains +3 Defense as base stats for every enemy within 10 blocks (up to 30 mobs, 60 as Tank). Range increases to 30 blocks when you play as Tank in dungeons and increase Diversion passive range by 15 blocks.
  - Requires F4 Completion
- [[Shadow Assassin Cloak]] (New dungeon cloak obtained from F5)
  - Strength: +20
  - Speed: +3
  - After you teleport, your next hit will deal 1.1x damage.
  - Requires F5 Completion
- [[Soulweaver Gloves]] (New dungeon gloves obtained from F6)
  - Strength: +10
  - Crit Damage: +10
  - While in The Catacombs, summon a haunted skull that slowly revolves around you every 15 seconds or when you kill a mob with melee damage. When an enemy is hit by a haunted skull, they are stunned for 2 seconds and deal 5% less damage
  - Requires F6 Completion
- [[Fel Skull]] (New dungeon drop from F6 Bedrock Chest)
  - Used to craft [[Felthorn Reaper]].
- Some of the new items can be upgraded by their respective floor fragments.
- Spirit Bear is now a miniboss that appears in blood room starting from F5/M5 onwards. He drops Thorn Fragments that can be used to upgrade items obtained in F4/M4.
- Players now start with a base 10% base item dungeon stat boost, giving newer players a taste of dungeon stat boosts and encourage them to use dungeon items.
#### ► Terminator and Shortbow Changes
You can now hold right-click to fire your Terminator and other shortbows at maximum efficiency! The rate of firing is affected by your attack speed. Your shot cooldown now dynamically updates on your item tooltip based on your attack speed stat. Pretty nifty, eh?
In addition, Terminator's Salvation ability now has a 0.25s cooldown and is activated on left-clicking only.
### Modifications to Existing Items
- [[Dreadlord Sword]] now has a Catacombs requirement of 3 (from 5).
- [[Dragon Shortbow]] can now be dungeonized. (Requires Catacombs 8)
- Increased the health of [[Skeleton Grunt]] pieces by 10 per piece.
- Reduced the mana cost of [[Bonzo's Staff]] from 100 to 90.
- Slightly increased the damage of the higher tier [[Cleaver|Cleavers]] obtained from Ophelia
  - Increase damage of [[Hyper Cleaver]] to 175 dmg / 100 str / 100 cd - (will make it equal to the FoT)
  - Increase damage of [[Giant Cleaver]] to 235 dmg / 120 str / 120 cd
- [[Giant's Sword]] is now a longsword and has a swing range of +1.
- [[Ophelia]] shop item requirements have been significantly nerfed.
  - [[Mender Fedora]], [[Shadow Goggles]], [[Metal Chestplate]], [[Hyper Cleaver]], and [[Super Undead Bow]] now requires a F3 clear instead of Catacombs Level 16.
  - [[Mender Crown]], [[Wither Goggles]], [[Steel Chestplate]], [[Giant Cleaver]], and [[Death Bow]] now require a F6 clear instead of Catacombs Level 28.
- Young, Unstable, Holy, and Old Dragon Armor now have a Catacombs requirement of 10.
- Wise, Protector, and Strong Dragon Armor now have a Catacombs requirement of 12.
- [[Superior Dragon Armor]] now has a Catacombs requirement of 14.
- [[Aspect of the Dragons]] now has a Catacombs requirement of 12.
- [[Revenant Falchion]] now has a Catacombs requirement of 12.
- [[Reaper Falchion]] now has a Catacombs requirement of 18.
### Dungeon Changes
- Dungeon Talismans now give double the MP while in Dungeons.
- Master Mode clears now count for 2 completions in collections.
- There are now warning signs when a Shadow Assassin is about to jump you.
- You can now activate class abilities without having a [[Dungeon Orb]] in your inventory.
  - You can still use a [[Dungeon Orb]] to activate your abilities if you want.
- Withered Dragons in M7 P5 now have 1,000,000,000 HP and 2500 Defense.
- [[Decoy|Decoys]] no longer bounce arrows (Bouncy Arrows and Archer's Bouncy Arrows ability).
- You now have a short period of invulnerability when teleporting to someone using Spirit Leaps
- [[Super Archer]] now wears armor on F5-F7.
- Heavily reduced the mob HP of Entrance, F1 and F2 to make dungeons easier for new players.
- Vitality and Mending no longer scale with dungeons stat boost.
- Durability is no longer a mechanic in SkyBlock.
### Catacombs Loot Table Changes
- [[Adaptive Armor]] is now a lot cheaper from chests, making it a way more palatable option for newer players.
  - Helmet: 500k
  - Chestplate: 2m
  - Leggings: 1m
  - Boots: 250k
- Increased the weight of [[Precursor Eye]] in F6 from 2 -> 3
- Increased the weight of [[Giant's Sword]] in F6 from 3 -> 5
- Increased the weight of [[Precursor Eye]] in M6 from 2 -> 3
- Increased the weight of [[Giant's Sword]] in M6 from 3 -> 6
- Added [[Fel Skull]] to the Bedrock Chest of the F6/M6 chests.
- Added [[Dark Orb]] to the emerald chests and above's loot table of F5/M5.
  - This should fix the issue of [[Dark Orb]] not appearing in the RNG Meter of F5/M5.
- The Gilded reforge now gives an additional 350/400 Intelligence and can be applied on the Midas Staff.
- Removed Ancient Rose from the drop table of Emerald, Obsidian and Bedrock Chests.
### QOL Improvements
- You can now salvage multiple items at once!
  - Want to quickly insert your items into the salvage menu? Click the buttons in the bottom right of the salvage menu to add all Rare/Epic mob drops into the GUI!
- F3 now ends when The Professor explodes, you are able to claim your chests while Necron rambles on.
- F6's final giant's chains were sped up. Immensely.
- F7 P1's floor cracks open faster when Maxor dies.
- F7 P2's Storm's idle time when you enter the boss is reduced by 0.1s per Wither Miner/Guard kill.
- Ice Fill now updates the block you're walking on much faster, making the puzzle a lot more lenient.
- Players can no longer dungeonize their gear if they don't meet the gear's Catacombs requirements.
  - For dungeon items with a catacombs requirement to be used in dungeons, the tooltip should say “Catacombs Requirement: Level XI” instead of “Requires Catacombs XI”.
  - “Catacombs Requirement” tells players that the requirement is only applicable in the Catacombs; you can still use your armor as normal outside of dungeons.
- If you don't meet the requirements for a dungeonized item, it will now show the catacombs requirement if you hover over it.
- Soul Eater now no longer procs on non-critical hits, stopping it from being procc'd by enchants like Thorns.
- Added a cooldown of 1s on deploying Power Orbs (so you don't accidentally double-click).
- Reduced the cooldown of SOS Flare from 20s to 10s.
### Dungeon Bug Fixes
- Fixed [[King Midas]] room completing when you blow up King Midas's crypt without killing him
- Fixed Potion effects and class bonus not being retained when rejoining a dungeon.
- Fixed rewards not being automatically picked up when claiming a chest from a failed run.
- Fixed players being unable to activate certain abilities in doorways.
- Fixed keys being elevated upwards when spawning in iron bars, fences etc.
- Fixed Respawning Skeletons dropping keys and blessings only after they complete their respawning animation.
- Fixed Ender Dragon's End Strike giving an additional 100% additive damage.
- Fixed /getfromsacks (/gfs) being disabled in Dungeons.
- Fixed Soul Whip dealing damage to invulnerable enemies.
- Fixed the blood room portal taking too long to teleport on later versions.
## ► Pets Menu
### Searching & Sorting
- The Pets Menu and AutoPets menu now have a search option.
- The Pets Menu and AutoPets menu now have a sorting filter of Rarity, Alphabetical, and Skill.
### Visibility
Pet Visibility has now been completely updated with 4 features:
- Show all pets
- Hide all pets
- Show only your pet
- Pet nametags: Enabled/Disabled
### AutoPets
- Specific pets can now be selected in the Autopets menu. Rather than the highest pet rarity and level always being chosen, now any leveled pet can be selected and used for autopet rules!
### Item Converting
- Pets can now be converted to items via Right-Clicking on the pet in the menu.
## ► Other Changes
With this patch we’ve decided to bring in a few much needed Broodmother changes, as well as a new NPC in the Deep Caverns.
### Broodmother Changes
- Renamed 'Brood Mother' to 'Broodmother'.
- Brought styling inline with Broodfather.
- Added particles when Broodmother spawns.
- Added better indication of when the Broodmother will spawn on the scoreboard and tab list.
- /warp mound will now take you to the top of the Spider Mound.
- Changed Haymitch’s dialogue to guide the player to the Broodmother.
### Redstone Miner
- Added a new dude to the Pigmen’s Den in the Deep Caverns.
  - He’ll ask for some Redstone and in return teach you about the Accessory Bag!
  - In case you don’t care about the Accessory Bag, or you already know how it works, he’ll also give you some Accessory Bag slots and SkyBlock XP.
- Changed the skin of the Lapis Miner to match his brother, the Redstone Miner.
### Items and Equipment Changes
These changes usually affect all useable items and wearable equipment, as well as pets and the Bazaar/Auction House.
#### Combat and Slayer Items
- Significantly reduced the damage conversion rates of Tactical Insertion and Souls Rebound.
  - Tactical Insertion’s burn deals 10% of all damage instead of 40% within 3s.
  - Souls Rebound’s damage increase is now 10% instead of 20%.
These items had too much damage potential and were pretty much only used to perform exploits such as 1-tapping slayer bosses. We have nerfed the damage potency of these items to prevent similar issues from happening in the future.
### World and Progression Changes
These changes usually affect progression related systems like quests, collections, levels and skills, NPC interactions and worlds.
#### Combat Progression
- Nest Endermites now give Combat XP and do less damage than before.
#### Mining Items
- Added a chest sound and a cute jingle to completing the lockpick minigame in Crystal Hollows.
- Removed the cooldown when resetting Heart of the Mountain.
## ► Other Bug Fixes
### Combat Fixes
- Fixed Griffin Pet buffs remaining after unequipping the pet.
- Fixed being able to spawn in multiple Rollim and Kaus NPCs.
- Changed Dungeoneering skill menu arrow style to match other similar menus.
- Fixed the Ability Cooldown Chat setting not being respected by the Zombie Sword ability.
- Fixed being able to turn a Moody Grappleshot into a Tripwire Hook by clicking a furnace.
- Fixed Magma Cube kills not counting toward Bestiary.
- Croesus will no longer display their menu if you haven’t completed a Dungeon yet.
- Fixed the always-active Kuudra pet perks being inactive/the wrong level if multiple Kuudra pets were in the menu.
- Fixed the Kuudra pet's Trophy Bait perk not working if the pet wasn't active.
- Fixed the Rend enchantment’s cooldown sometimes not applying correctly.
- Fixed abilities working on Sven Packmaster.
- Fixed the Ashfang Gravity Well taking too long to explode.
### Mining Fixes
- Using Smelting Touch on Netherrack now provides Netherrack collection.
- Fixed the Chest Looter commission in the Crystal Hollows resetting when leaving the lobby.
- Fixed Slow Moving Snail Pet perk activating only half the time when breaking blocks.
### Farming Fixes
- Fixed a Garden Milestone exploit.
- Chicken Head fall damage is now displayed without the unnecessary decimal.
- Fixed typo in Elizabeth’s Barn Skin preview dialogue.
- Fixed not being able to open chests while having Enchanted Seeds in hand.
### Misc. Fixes
- Fixed getting 3 clicks as a reward from non-highest difficulties in Experimentation Table.
- Fixed Bingo profiles always getting the Abiphone achievement.
- Fixed Builder's Wand not working with Quartz Pillar Blocks.
- Beach Ball decoration can now be placed.
- Fixed Day Savers turning into vanilla blocks when breaking them.
- Fixed being able to turn a Moody Grappleshot into a Tripwire Hook by clicking a furnace.
- Fixed Personal Deletor not deleting Luxurious Spools dropped by Arachne and items obtained from Crystal Hollows chests.
- Egg Hunt blocks will no longer be removed from an explosion.
- Picking up a minion will now give you XP and collection for the items inside.
- Fixed being unable to mount pets.
- Fixed the Bingo NPC saying you aren't SkyBlock Level 10 when on a Bingo profile.
- Fixed being able to collect extra SkyBlock XP from the Claim All button in the Museum menu.
- Fixed the wiki link not properly sending for the Blazetekk™ Ham Radio
- Fixed Rhys still only awarding 1 copper in the Garden.
- Fixed many coloring and spelling inconsistencies.

# Changelog/2023/September 23
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
ANNOUNCEMENT
Inferno Minion Design & Heads Up
Updated: September 23, 2023
Hello!
We've taken a look at the inferno minion and how expensive it is to run. To remediate it, we're considering the following changes:
Hypergolic drop rates
- 2.8x more vertexes
- 15% more chili peppers
- 20% more apexes
Hypergolic Recipe
- Half the number of Heavy Fuels in the Hypergolic Recipe (from 24 to 12, removing the corners in the grid)
Distillate drops
- Changing the distillate drop rates from the boss from being weighted 900 for all and 500 for gabagool (gabagool was rarer) to a ratio of 700:1400, making gabagool distillate a lot more common
Inferno Minion
- 10% faster action speed
Take note, these are NOT FINAL and we'll take your feedback into consideration, but a big nerfs to the inferno minion / blaze slayer costs is the direction. We're giving a heads up so no one is taken by surprise once the patch drops.
Let us know what you think! :hypixel_smile:

# Changelog/2023/September 3
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
PATCH
SkyBlock - Year 300
Updated: September 3, 2023
Hello!
Today is the day SkyBlock reaches its 300th year, over 1500 real-life days since the game's inception. As has quickly become tradition, we will be celebrating the turn of the century with lots of cake! However, there will also be some special events taking place throughout the year for everyone to get involved in. Read on to find out more!
## ► Century Cakes!
In celebration of such a momentous occasion, Simon himself has appeared in the Hub. As is typical of century celebrations, he will hand out a random Century Cake to each player who speaks to him. Not only can this be any of the 12 previously existing cakes, but some players may also receive one of two new cakes just added to the game! The Undead Century Cake grants those who consume it Vitality, and players who eat the Not-a-lie cake will find themselves given a small True Defense boost.
## ► Raffles!
This year, Simon has brought a friend! Rezzus can be found at the Raffle Stand just in front of the Community Center in the Hub. Players who speak to him will receive a snazzy emblem showcasing which team they've been designated as part of the raffle event. Players who have been assigned a team will receive a raffle ticket for every 20 minutes spent online. Active playing can also reward players with a second ticket, which has a separate 20-minute cooldown.
All tickets a player collects will be automatically entered into the next raffle of each type. Raffles are held from the beginning of Year 300 until the end, and consist of three types:
- The Speed Raffle is drawn every 2 hours, real-life time. All tickets players collected within the last two hours will be entered.
- The Daily Raffle is drawn every 24 hours. All tickets players collected within the last 24 hours will be entered. Due to there being more tickets entered, there will also be more rewards!
- The Big One is drawn once, at the end of Year 300. It gives the most (and best) rewards of any raffle type, and all tickets gathered throughout the entirety of Year 300 will be entered into the draw.
All of this means that each ticket is entered into three different draws, giving you three separate chances at winning a prize! Fret not, however, as even the more unlucky entrants into the daily and big raffles will receive a consolation prize scaling with how many tickets they gathered.
## ► Raffle Milestones
Another way in which those who participate in the event can earn rewards is through Raffle Milestones. As players gather tickets, they will receive rewards upon reaching certain thresholds. There is no raffle here - every player who reaches a milestone will be able to immediately claim the corresponding reward. With emblems, pet skins, and even a talisman up for grabs, nobody has to go home empty-handed!
## ► A Special Memento
Remember when we spoke about teams being assigned earlier? This is where that comes into play. Throughout the event, a tally will be kept of how many raffle tickets each team has collected in total. By the end of the event, the team that has gathered the most tickets will be declared the winner. All players who took part in the event will then receive a special memento item, though only the winning team will be immortalized in the item's description.
And that's it! The Year 300 celebrations are live right now, so get online and have some fun! A massive thanks to the community for being so passionate about the game, and we look forward to celebrating again with you soon!

# Changelog/2024/April 19
Categories: 2024 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[April 19] SkyBlock 0.20.1 Alpha
Updated: April 19, 2024
Howdy howdy!
Alpha is currently rolling with some new changes for the 0.20.1 update.
► Hoppity's Chocolate Factory
Check out this thread for more information on Hoppity's Chocolate Factory!
► Dark Auction Changes
- Added 2 new items to the Dark Auction rotation - the Magic 8 Ball and Sirius' Personal Phone Number.
- Allocated 2x as many servers to the Dark Auction, allowing more players to enter.
  - This is increased to 3x when Scorpius is mayor.
- Adjusted Dark Auction structure and added 1 additional round:
  - OLD: Item, Book, Item, Flower Minion.
  - NEW: Item, Book, Item, Item, Flower Minion.
- Adjusted Darker Auction (Scorpius) structure and added 1 additional round.
  - OLD: Item, Book, Item, Book, Plasma Nucleus/Hegemony Artifact, Flower Minion, Midas Staff.
  - NEW: Item, Book, Item, Book, Item, Plasma Nucleus/Hegemony Artifact, Flower Minion, Midas Staff.
- Doubled the weights of the Ender Artifact, Wither Artifact.
- Quintupled the weight of the Dark Purple Dye.
► Glacite Tunnels Changes
- Added Glacite Golem pet to Glacite Collection IX.
- Mining Helix Emblems now show off the amount of commissions you've completed.
- Umber, Tungsten, and Glacite now require 3 base fuel to mine.
- Portable Campfire now removes 50 Cold instead of 15, also reduced the time between being able to place them.
- Glacite Mutt, Glacite Caver, Glacite Mage, and Glacite Bowman now drop 50 Glacite Powder on kill.
- Changed the Frozen Corpse chat breakdown to now include the type of corpse.
- Changed Mineshaft portals to now stick around for up to 30 seconds.
- Changed Suspicious Scrap to drop a max of 5 times per Mineshaft (each scrap has a lower drop chance than the last), but heavily reduced the chance of a Scrap containing fossils or powder drops while buffing other drops.
- Changed the drops from Frozen Corpses to no longer condense in chat.
- Reaper Peppers now grant +1 Cold Resistance in addition to their previous effect.
- Abiphones are now be usable in the Glacite Tunnels.
- Crystal variants of Mineshafts now spawn 25% of the time instead of 10%.
- Adjusted some forge times.
- Frozen Corpses now give a base amount of Glacite Powder + HOTM EXP when looted, scaling with corpse tier.
- Adjusted Umber, Tungsten, and Glacite collections so that collection items unlock earlier.
- Reduced the Penguin Pet’s max sea creature chance from 30 to 10
- Gave the Mole Pet and Goblin Pet to receive magic find as a base stat.
- Adjusted the stats granted by the Shiny Prism reforge, swapping the mining fortune and intelligence values.
- Condensed the Peak of the Mountain lore.
- Adjusted the Shattered Locket item lore.
- Ascension Rope now works in the Glacite Mineshafts - it will give the bonus rewards as if you used the Exit Portal.
- Edited the recipes of several items.
- Edited Fossil Excavator, Umber & Tungsten Corpse, and Vanguard Corpse loot tables.
  - Fossil Excavator drops less powder.
  - Umber & Tungsten corpses are more likely to contain gemstone crystals.
  - Vanguard Corpses have been buffed across the board.
- Skeleton key now only works on Vanguard corpses.
- Adjusted the location of the Fossil Pets in the SkyBlock Leveling guide.
- Adjusted the Campfire in the Dwarven Base Camp to reduce Cold over time instead of instantly.
  - While we like this change, we are looking to speed up the rate at which the campfire reduces cold.
- Increased the rewards players receive upon leaving a Glacite Mineshaft, scaling with their accomplishments.
- Adjusted Tungsten Keychain to activate every 500 → 100 block breaks, renamed to Tungsten Regulator.
- Adjusted ‘Mineshaft Explorer’ commission to complete when entering any Mineshaft as opposed to only when spawning one.
- Changed the HOTM requirements of several items.
- Changed the Bal pet's 'Made of Lava' perk
  - This change will not be releasing with 0.20.1.
- Added the Relic of Power to the SkyBlock Leveling guide.
- Added rewards to Dr. Stone upon donating all desired fossils to her.
- Added some Glacite Tunnels items to the Museum.
- Added +1 Fossil Dust as a drop when mining Quartz Blocks or slabs inside a Glacite Mineshaft.
- Added Aquamarine Gemstone Slots to the Auger Rod and Giant Rod.
- Added NPC sell price to all fossils.
- Added Autopet rules for when you first start getting cold, and when you first start getting heat.
- Added a Dwarven Emissary to the Dwarven Base Camp.
- Added 2 new Glacite Tunnels commissions.
  - The Gemstone Crystal commission will not be releasing with 0.20.1.
- Added information about how much scrap you've obtained in a Mineshaft to the General Info widget.
- Added an animation to the Campfire in the Dwarven Base Camp.
- Added title text that alerts the player when they spawn a Glacite Mineshaft.
- Increased the amount of Glacite Powder you get from mining Glacite to 5/6/7.
- Removed starter commissions from the Glacite Tunnels.
- Renamed several NPC’s throughout the Glacite Tunnels.
- Players who have reached the Dwarven Base Camp may now warp to the Crystal Hollows without needing to talk to Gwendolyn to renew their free pass.
This is a non-exhaustive list and there may be unlisted changes here or there. Bugfixes are not listed.
04/20 Edit:
- Changes to the Bal Pet will not be released alongside this update. There are future plans in place to take the stats Bal would lose from its proposed nerf and re-add them to the game through other sources (and as flat stats rather than the current % increase, which poses several problems when it comes to designing future content), but these plans will not be part of this update and we thus deem it fair to delay any changes made to the Bal Pet until everything's ready to be tested and released as one. Thanks for the feedback! In addition to this, the Scatha Pet is not being changed as part of this update. The current changes to the pet are an unintended side effect of a bugfix made elsewhere in the game. Following the update, the Scatha Pet will still function the same as it does currently.
As always, if you encounter any issues - small, major, or critical; please report them here and make sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!

# Changelog/2024/April 9/1
Categories: 2024 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[April 9th] Hoppity’s Chocolate Factory
Updated: April 9, 2024
Hello,
Two years ago on April 1st we added a cookie clicker to the Booster Cookie. Last year we improved it, and this year we decided to make it an official part of the game.
Hoppity is a new NPC that will appear in Spring, every in game year. Being a Rabbit and all, of course he wants you to hunt chocolate rabbit eggs. They will spawn periodically during the whole duration of Spring on public islands.
There are 3 different eggs, and every in game day, each egg type will respawn at a different location, allowing you to collect them again. Every player can collect each egg, so if you are the first player to find an egg, you can notify where it is to the other players.
Right clicking an egg will roll for a chocolate rabbit. There are many different rabbits, and each unique rabbit you find will increase the production of Hoppity’s Chocolate Factory, which is the revamped cookie clicker.
You can find the Chocolate Factory inside the Event Menu, or from Hoppity. As your chocolate production increases, you will be able to unlock more and more upgrades and prestiges. Eventually if your production is strong enough, you will even be able to buy a few items from his shop!
While this is (mostly) an idle game, clicking isn’t supposed to be the intended gameplay, therefore clicking will only have a few upgrades and cap out at a very low tier.
We simply hope that those eggs will be an exciting addition to the game and that you will have fun with the chocolate factory.
But please give us your feedback in this thread!
If you encounter any issues - small, major, or critical; please report them here and make sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!
### ➥ Where is Better Mayors?
It’s currently still in development. We put the community project up early as we wanted to give you all as much time as possible to get your memento items. If all goes well, we hope to see y’all some time next week.

# Changelog/2024/August 20
Aliases: Changelog/2024/August 21, Changelog/2024/August 26
Categories: 2024 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[August 20] Some Mining Changes
Updated: August 20, 2024
Hello,
Today we are bringing a small update to Alpha with a couple of changes to mining.
Day 1 Changes
- The Crystal Nucleus RNG Meter will give a small amount of retroactive XP, based upon how many runs you've completed.
- Golden Dragon Pet's 'Shining Scales' perk will now cap out at 100 million gold instead of 1 billion. The maximum stats earned are still the same, you just earn them faster.
- Specific Mining Fortunes, such as Gemstone Fortune, will be hidden from the main SkyBlock Profile in the same way farming's numerous fortune stats are, though you can still view specific fortunes in the stats breakdown.
- /warp forge will be adjusted to make the player face Fred instead of a dangerously positioned lava pool (probably a safety hazard).
- King Yolkar's dialogue will be sped up for players who have completed a Crystal Nucleus run previously.
- Bal will respawn faster and all players who contributed will be allowed to claim the Topaz Crystal (previously only those who received Bestiary progression could claim).These changes may not have been implemented at the time of reading, but are on their way. More to come!
## ► Crystal Hollows Access
- Requires HotM III > IV
Designer's Note: Now that the Mining Skill has 3 distinct areas, we thought it would be appropriate to rebalance progression so that it more evenly spreads across them. This change was not made lightly, countless other changes have been made to account for this.
## ► Gemstone Gauntlet Revamp
- Breaking Power: 9 > 8
- Damage: 200 > +300
- Strength: 0 > +50
- Mining Speed: 1600 > +800
- New Passive: Reduced To Atoms Mobs killed on Mining Islands now drop Gemstones when killed with Gemstone Gauntlets. What Gemstones they drop correlate to the Gemstones that you have on your Gauntlet. Killing these mobs also charges your Gauntlet Meter by 1.
- New Passive: Kinetic Mobs killed on Mining Islands will now reduce the timers on all your Forge Slots by a certain amount of time when killed with Gemstone Gauntlets. The amount of time scales by 0.5s for each Perfect Gemstone you have slotted within your Gauntlet.
- New Ability: Gemstone Snap The Gauntlet Meter stores up to 150 charges (150 mob kills). Once fully charged, you may Shift Right Click with the item to drain your meter, giving you +50 Mining Fortune for 60m. This effect does not stack.
Designer's Note: This is a big one. The Gemstone Gauntlet has long been problematic in mining's progression. Most player's never even interact with Drills, and we don't love that. Drill Parts and Fuel are systems we want the player to interact with more as they progress in mining, and currently that just isn't the case until late in the game. To coincide with these changes, we will also be reducing the cost for many Drills in the game and putting more Gemstone Slots on Gemstone Drills. Drill Parts have also been largely buffed, so Drills should be more appealing to go for more than ever. That all being said, we still wanted the Gemstone Gauntlet to have some greater place in mining as a whole, so we are introducing some new abilities to the item in hopes of maintaining its relevancy in some regards.
## ► Crystal Nucleus RNG Meter
The Crystal Nucleus is finally receiving an RNG Meter. Each run will fill up your meter by 1,000 EXP, and you can set your drop to pretty much any non-Gemstone item. Below is a list of all the available items and their required runs.
- Pickonimbus 2000: 4,000 EXP (4 runs)
- Divan Fragment: 8,000 EXP
- Prehistoric Egg: 8,000 EXP
- Helix: 10,000 EXP
- Ruby Crystal: 10,000 EXP
- Jasper Crystal: 10,000 EXP
- Jaderald: 20,000 EXP
- Gemstone Mixture: 35,000 EXP
- Fortune IV: 35,000 EXP
- Precious Pearl: 50,000 EXP
- Claw Fossil: 50,000 EXP
- Recall Potion: 100,000 EXP
- Quick Claw: 450,000 EXP
- Divan's Alloy: 1,000,000 EXP
In addition, we have done away with the old dynamic drop rate system that the original Divan's Alloy had. This is now changing to a 1 in 17,000 rolls, which is roughly 1 in 1,000 runs (less if you have the Mole Pet and High Roller Diamond Essence Shop perk).
## ► Pure Ores
We have renamed the Dwarven Ores (such as Dwarven Gold) into "Pure Ores". Their functionality remains unchanged.
In addition to this, we have added Pure variants of all the remaining vanilla ores. This includes Coal, Iron, Lapis, Redstone, Emerald, and Diamond. These can now all be found in the Royal Mines zone of the Dwarven Mines. It should be much easier now to collect large amounts of these resources!
## ► 7 New Stats
### ➥ Mining Spread
This stat is your chance to mine an additional non-Gemstone block. Stacking over 100 Mining Spread guarantees you mine an additional block, and starts working towards the next block, similar to Fortune stats. Having 450 Mining Spread means that you will mine 4 additional blocks for every block you mine, and there is a 50% chance for you to mine a 5th.
### ➥ Gemstone Spread
Works just like Mining Spread but is exclusive to Gemstones. This stat is compatible with Pristine, although is very difficult to come by to compensate.
### ➥ 4 New Sub Fortunes
Each of these types applies on top of standard Mining Fortune for affected blocks.
Affected By Ore Fortune
- Coal Ore / Dwarven Coal (new!)
- Iron Ore / Dwarven Iron (new!)
- Gold Ore / Dwarven Gold
- Lapis Ore / Dwarven Lapis (new!)
- Redstone Ore / Dwarven Redstone
- Emerald Ore / Dwarven Emerald
- Diamond Ore / Dwarven Diamond (new!)
Affected By Block Fortune
- Cobblestone / Stone
- End Stone
- Glowstone
- Gravel / Flint
- Hard Stone
- Ice
- Netherrack
- Mycelium
- Obsidian
- Red Sand
- Sand
Affected By Dwarven Metal Fortune
- Mithril
- Titanium
- Umber
- Tungsten
- Glacite
Affected By Gemstone Fortune
- No points for guessing
Designer's Note: Yes, this means blocks like Red Sand and Mycelium are now directly affected by Mining Fortune. We anticipate changes to costs and recipes will happen, but we want to see how things turn out first.
### ➥ Heat Resistance
For every 1 Heat Resistance you have, Heat will build up 1% slower while you are in the Magma Fields. The way Heat builds up has all been revamped.
Current System
+ While in the Magma Fields: +1 Heat/s
+ While in lava: +5 Heat/s
- While not in the Magma Fields: -0.5 Heat/s
- While in water: -1 Heat/s
- Drinking a water bottle: -40 Heat
New System
+ While in the Magma Fields: +0.1 Heat/s
+ While in Khazad-Dum: +0.2 Heat/s (takes priority over Magma Fields)
+ While on fire: +1 Heat/s
+ While in lava: +3 Heat/s (takes priority over fire)
- While not in the Magma Fields: -1 Heat/s
- While in water: -5 Heat/s
- Drinking any potion: -10 Heat
Designer's Note: These changes make it so that on average Heat will build up slower and disperse faster. This is to compensate for additional changes listed further in the thread, including the removal of the Heat Immunity mechanic from the Bal Pet and reintroduction of it onto a fairly underutilized armor set as an option for those who want it. That being said, having large amounts of Heat Resistance will make it take a pretty long while for Heat to build up to lethal levels. This is something we will actively be looking to balance.
## ► Heart of the Mountain Overhaul
Designer's Note: With the release of the Glacite Tunnels update, there are now 10 tiers to the Heart of the Mountain. Now that we have a better idea of what works and what doesn't, we are taking the time to overhaul the entirety of the tree to increase the diversity in builds players can spec into.
### ➥ Powder Costs
- Mithril Powder: HotM I - III perks
- Gemstone Powder: HotM IV - VII perks
- Glacite Powder: HotM VIII - X perks
Designer's Note: This is one of the many changes being made in conjunction with the new Crystal Hollows HotM IV requirement. This puts the Heart of the Mountain directly in line with when you unlock new mining areas to explore. No more waiting until HotM VI to spend your Gemstone Powder!
Note: Perks are listed in order from left to right on the tree.
### ➥ Tier I
- No changes
### ➥ Tier II
- 1st Slot: Mining Speed Boost (Pickaxe Ability)
  - Level I
    - Effect: 200% > 150%
  - Level II
    - Effect: 300% > 200%
  - Level III
    - Effect: 400% > 250%
Designer's Note: Mining Speed Boost was far and away the best Pickaxe Ability for most players. This change, paired with buffs to the other Pickaxes abilities, should create a more level playing field.
- 2nd Slot: Precision Mining
  - Moved from the 7th slot of HotM IV to here
  - Can now be upgraded (2o levels)
    - +5% + level%
    - Same cost scaling as Great Explorer
- 3rd Slot: Mining Fortune
  - +5 > +2 Mining Fortune per level
Designer's Note: We have given back this Mining Fortune in new places. This was a change we made so that we can diversify how Mining Fortune is obtained.
- 4th Slot: Titanium Insanium
  - Moved from the 2nd slot of HotM II to here
  - Functionality unchanged
- 5th Slot: Pickobulus (Pickaxe Ability)
  - Level I
    - Radius: 2 > 2.5 Blocks
    - Cooldown: 120s > 60s
  - Level II
    - Radius: 2 > 2.5 Blocks
    - Cooldown: 110s > 50s
  - Level III
    - Radius: 3 > 2.5 Blocks
    - Cooldown: 100s > 40s
Designer's Note: We really liked the idea of some Pickaxe Abilities being more frequent in usage than others. If this turns out not to be enough for Pickobulus, we will look into further reducing its cooldown.
### ➥ Tier III
- 1st Slot: Luck of the Cave
  - Functionality unchanged
- 2nd Slot: Efficient Miner
  - Moved from the 4th slot of HotM IV to here
  - Rephrased description to now include Mining Spread
  - Functionality unchanged
- 3rd Slot: Quick Forge
  - Moved from the 4th slot of HotM II to here
  - Changed the cost scaling to be identical with Great Explorer
### ➥ Tier IV
- 1st Slot: Sky Mall
  - Rephrased description so that it mentions all Mining Islands rather than just the Dwarven Mines
  - Functionality unchanged (description was just outdated)
- 2nd Slot: Old-School
  - 20 Levels
  - Grants +5 Ore Fortune per level
  - Same cost scaling as Fortunate
- 3rd Slot: Professional
  - Moved from the 3rd slot of HotM VI to here
  - Functionality unchanged
- 4th Slot: Mole
  - Moved from the 4th slot of HotM VI to here
  - Functionality unchanged
- 5th Slot: Gem Lover
  - Moved from the 5th slot of HotM VI to here (now costs Gemstone Powder)
  - Renamed from Fortunate
  - Fixed the cost scaling of this perk
  - Functionality unchanged
- 6th Slot: Seasoned Mineman
  - Moved from the 3rd slot of HotM IV to here
  - Functionality unchanged
- 7th Slot: Front Loaded
  - Moved from the 6th slot of HotM IV to here
  - Now grants the following buffs for the first 2,500 Gemstones you mine each day:
    - x3 Gemstone Powder
    - +150 Gemstone Fortune
    - +250 Mining Speed
### ➥ Tier V
- 1st Slot: Daily Grind
  - Moved from the 4th slot of HotM VIII to here
  - No longer upgraded, instead gives 500 Powder multiplied by your HotM level when triggered
- 2nd Slot: Core of the Mountain
  - Renamed from Peak of the Mountain
  - Removed the +1 Forge Slot from Core of the Mountain II
  - Moved the +1 Pickaxe Ability Level from Core of the Mountain I to II
Designer's Note: As you will see soon, we are tying your available Forge Slots to your HotM level (up to VII). This change was made in conjunction with that, which means you will be able to have a maximum of 7 Forge Slots available.
- 3rd Slot: Daily Powder
  - Moved from the 3rd slot of HotM III to here
  - No longer upgraded, instead gives 500 Powder multiplied by your HotM level when triggered
### ➥ Tier VI
- 1st Slot: Anomalous Desire (Pickaxe Ability)
  - Increases the chances of certain events happening while active
    - Golden Goblins
    - Fallen Stars
    - Powder Ghasts
    - Worms
    - Glacite Mineshafts
  - Chances: +30%/+40%/+50%
  - Cooldowns: 120s/110s/100s
- 2nd Slot: Blockhead
  - 20 Levels
  - Grants +5 Block Fortune per level
  - Same cost scaling as Fortunate
- 3rd Slot: Subterranean Fisher
  - 40 Levels
  - Grants 5 + (0.5 * Level) Fishing Speed in the Crystal Hollows and Glacite Tunnels
  - Grants 1 + (0.1 * Level) Sea Creature Chance in the Crystal Hollows and Glacite Tunnels
  - Same cost scaling as Lonesome Miner
- 4th Slot: Keep It Cool
  - 50 Levels
  - Grants +0.4 Heat Resistance per level
  - Same cost scaling as Warm-Hearted
- 5th Slot: Lonesome Miner
  - Moved from the 2nd slot of HotM VI to here
  - Functionality unchanged
- 6th Slot: Great Explorer
  - Functionality unchanged
- 7th Slot: Maniac Miner (Pickaxe Ability)
  - Grants +1 Breaking Power and a stacking amount of Mining Fortune per block broken for the duration of the ability. Each block broken consumes 20 Mana.
  - Mining Fortune: +5/+10/+15
  - Duration: 10s/15s/20s
### ➥ Tier VII
- 1st Slot: Speedy Mineman
  - Renamed from Mining Speed II
  - Functionality unchanged
- 2nd Slot: Powder Buff
  - Functionality unchanged
- 3rd Slot: Fortunate Mineman
  - Renamed from Mining Fortune II
  - +5 > +3 Mining Fortune per level
### ➥ Tier VIII
- 1st Slot: Miner's Blessing
  - Grants +30 Magic Find while on Mining Islands
- 2nd Slot: No Stone Unturned
  - Moved from the 6th slot of HotM VIII to here
  - Functionality unchanged
- 3rd Slot: Strong Arm
  - Moved from the 5th slot of HotM VIII to here
  - Now effects all Dwarven Metals, not just Umber and Tungsten
- 4th Slot: Steady Hand
  - 100 levels
  - Grants a 1% per level chance in Glacite Mineshafts to get Gemstone Spread when mining a block
  - At levels 1-50: +1 Gemstone Spread
  - At levels 51-100: +2 Gemstone Spread
  - Same cost scaling as Efficient Miner
- 5th Slot: Surveyor
  - Moved from the 1st slot of HotM IX to here
  - Functionality unchanged
- 6th Slot: Cold-Hearted
  - Moved from the 2nd slot of HotM VIII to here
  - Renamed from Warm-Hearted
  - +0.2 > +0.4 Cold Resistance per level
- 7th Slot: Mineshaft Mayhem
  - Functionality unchanged
### ➥ Tier IX
- 1st Slot: Metalhead
  - 20 levels
  - Grants +5 Dwarven Metal Fortune per level
  - Same cost scaling as Fortunate
- 2nd Slot: Rags to Riches
  - Moved from the 6th slot of HotM X to here
  - +2 > +4 Mining Fortune per level
- 3rd Slot: Eager Adventurer
  - +2 > +4 Mining Speed per level
### ➥ Tier X
- 1st Slot: Gemstone Infusion (Pickaxe Ability)
  - Functionality unchanged
- 2nd Slot: Crystalline
  - 50 Levels
  - Increases your chances to find a Gemstone Mineshaft with a Crystal in it
  - +0.5% per level
  - Cost Scaling: (nextlevel+1) ^ 3.3
- 3rd Slot: Gifts from the Departed
  - Moved from the 2nd slot of HotM X to here
  - Functionality unchanged
- 4th Slot: Mining Master
  - 10 Levels
  - Grants +0.1 Pristine per level
  - Cost Scaling: (nextlevel+7) ^ 5
- 5th Slot: Dead Man's Chest
  - Moved from the 4th slot of HotM X to here
  - Functionality unchanged
- 6th Slot: Vanguard Seeker
  - 50 levels
  - Increases your chances of discovering a Glacite Mineshaft with a Vanguard Corpse within it
  - +1% per level
  - Same cost scaling as Warm-Hearted
- 7th Slot: Sheer Force (Pickaxe Ability)
  - Grants +100 Mining Spread for a duration
  - Duration: 20s/25s/30s
### ➥ Other
- Reset every player's Heart of the Mountain trees
- Added a warning for when you mine with unspent HotM tokens
- Disabled HotM perks now appear as Redstone Dust
- You can no longer switch to a different Pickaxe Ability while you have one currently active or on cooldown
## ► The Forge
### ➥ More Forge Slots
- Added a Forge Slot to HotM V, VI, and VII
- Removed the Forge Slot from Core of the Mountain II
  - The +1 Pickaxe Ability level from Core of the Mountain I is moving to II to compensate
Designer's Note: With these changes players will be able to get 7 Forge Slots. We understand forge times can be a pain point, but we did not want to reduce timers across the board as it lessens the purpose of even having the Forge in the first place, so we are instead opting to add more slots.
### ➥ Forge Categorization
We have re-sorted all forgeable items into the following categories:
- Refining (refined mithril, refined titanium, etc.)
- Forging (mithril plates, gemstone mixtures, etc.)
- Tools (drills, chisels, etc.)
- Gear (armor, accessories, etc.)
- Reforge Stones
- Drill Parts (goblin omelettes, fuel tanks, etc.)
- Perfect Gemstones
- Pets (pets and pet items)
- Other (anything we didn't want to create an entirely new category for, etc.)
We have also added a search button in case you can't find where something is!
### ➥ Forge Recipes
Are all your Forge Slots still somehow full even though we just added 2 more? No worries! (well not exactly...) Now you can access all of your unlocked Forge Recipes from the main menu.
### ➥ Recipe Changes
- Mithril Drill SX-326
  - 5 > 3 Golden Plates
- Topaz Drill KGR-12
  - 5 > 8 Magma Cores
- Jasper Drill X
  - 0 > 16 Magma Cores
- Mithril-Plated Drill Engine
  - 3 > 1 Mithril Plates
- Titanium-Plated Drill Engine
  - 5 > 0 Plasma
  - 4 > 0 Mithril Plates
  - 0 > 1 Mithril-Plated Drill Engine
  - 10 > 2 Drill Engines
  - 5 > 8 Refined Titanium
- Ruby-Polished Drill Engine
  - 1 Mithril-Plated Drill Engine > 1 Titanium-Plated Drill Engine
  - 0 > 5 Drill Engines
  - 10 Fine Ruby Gemstones > 1 Perfect Ruby Gemstone
- Sapphire-Polished Drill Engine
  - 1 Titanium-Plated Drill Engine > 1 Ruby-Polished Drill Engine
  - 25 FTX 3070s > 8 Precursor Apparatuses
  - 20 Fine Sapphire Gemstones > 3 Perfect Sapphire Gemstones
- Amber-Polished Drill Engine
  - 1 > 0 Ruby-Polished Drill Engines
  - 1 Flawless Amber Gemstone > 5 Perfect Amber Gemstones
  - 50 Robotron Reflectors > 16 Precursor Apparatuses
  - 0 > 5 Drill Engines
  - 0 > 64 Plasma
- Perfectly-Cut Fuel Tank
  - 0 > 64 Plasma
- Goblin Omelette
  - 99 > 96 Goblin Eggs
- Pesto Goblin Omelette
  - 99 > 96 Green Goblin Eggs
  - 1 Fine Jade Gemstone > 1 Flawless Jade Gemstone
  - 0 > 1 Goblin Omelette
- Sunny Side Goblin Omelette
  - 99 > 96 Yellow Goblin Eggs
  - 1 Fine Topaz Gemstone > 1 Flawless Topaz Gemstone
  - 0 > 1 Goblin Omelette
- Spicy Goblin Omelette
  - 99 > 96 Red Goblin Eggs
  - 0 > 1 Goblin Omelette
- Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette
  - 99 > 96 Blue Goblin Eggs

  - 0 > 1 Goblin Omelette
### ➥ Other
- Moved the Mithril Pickaxe out of The Forge and into Bubu's Shop
- Moved the Refined Mithril Pickaxe out of The Forge and into Bubu's Shop
Designer's Note: It's nice to have all the Pickaxes laid out for newer players in Bubu's Shop. This has a knock-on effect of letting NBT carry over to more upgrades now.
## ► Balancing
### ➥ Cost Changes
