### ➥ Mining Reforge Stone Overhaul
Designer's Note: This is one of the places we are giving Mining Fortune back. Reforge Stones for Mining Tools are being entirely reworked, but also buffed significantly. The goal of this is to make sure each of them have a place at some point in the game.
Amber Material
Designer's Note: Amber Material is intended to be the BIS Reforge Stone for Mining Speed. It will give you the best odds at surpassing a tick threshold compared to all other Reforge Stones. The cost? No Mining Fortune whatsoever. As an added bonus, you will mine up Golden & Diamond Goblins significantly more frequently thanks to the new Ambered Bonus. Our hope is that this will synergize well enough with Luck of the Cave and Sky Mall to make Goblin mining feel more worth doing.
- COMMON: +25 Mining Speed / +10% Ambered Bonus
- UNCOMMON: +35 Mining Speed / +15% Ambered Bonus
- RARE: +50 Mining Speed / +20% Ambered Bonus
- EPIC: +75 Mining Speed / +25% Ambered Bonus
- LEGENDARY: +100 Mining Speed / +30% Ambered Bonus
- MYTHIC: +150 Mining Speed / +40% Ambered Bonus
- DIVINE: +200 Mining Speed / +50% Ambered Bonus
Ambered Bonus: Discover Golden/Diamond Goblins +???% more often while mining.
Andesite Whetstone (renamed from Refined Amber)
Designer's Note: Andesite Whetstone has stayed largely the same here, it is still the BIS Reforge Stone for Mining Wisdom. The item has been renamed due to the fact that it was created before Amber existed as a resource. Now that Amber AND the Amber Material reforge stone exist, this item was due for a rebrand. Hopefully we never have to add an Andesite Collection. Or a Whetstone Collection.
- COMMON: +1 Mining Wisdom / 0.1% Refined Bonus
- UNCOMMON: +2 Mining Wisdom / 0.13% Refined Bonus
- RARE: +3 Mining Wisdom / 0.16% Refined Bonus
- EPIC: +4 Mining Wisdom / 0.19% Refined Bonus
- LEGENDARY: +6 Mining Wisdom / 0.22% Refined Bonus
- MYTHIC: +8 Mining Wisdom / 0.26% Refined Bonus
- DIVINE: +10 Mining Wisdom / 0.3% Refined Bonus
Refined Bonus: Grants a ???% chance to drop the enchanted item when mining a block.
Black Diamond
Designer's Note: Black Diamond is still maintaining its spot as an early game Reforge Stone. The Defense it used to give has now been consolidated into a more powerful reforge bonus, while the base stats are now a nice split between Mining Speed and Fortune.
- COMMON: +5 Mining Speed / +2 Mining Fortune / +25 Fruitful Bonus
- UNCOMMON: +10 Mining Speed / +4 Mining Fortune / +30 Fruitful Bonus
- RARE: +15 Mining Speed / +6 Mining Fortune / +35 Fruitful Bonus
- EPIC: +25 Mining Speed / +8 Mining Fortune / +40 Fruitful Bonus
- LEGENDARY: +40 Mining Speed / +10 Mining Fortune / +50 Fruitful Bonus
- MYTHIC: +55 Mining Speed / +12 Mining Fortune / +60 Fruitful Bonus
- DIVINE: +70 Mining Speed / +15 Mining Fortune / +70 Fruitful Bonus
Fruitful Bonus: Grants +??? Defense while on Mining Islands.
Diamonite
Designer's Note: Diamonite has had its focus shifted to Blocks affected by Mining Fortune, now being the BIS at that. The Fleet Bonus is interesting in that it grants Mining Spread on Blocks affected by Mining Fortune as well, which will pair nicely with all the new Dwarven Ore veins in the Royal Mines.
- COMMON: +10 Mining Speed / +25 Block Fortune / +50 Fleet Bonus
- UNCOMMON: +20 Mining Speed / +30 Block Fortune / +60 Fleet Bonus
- RARE: +30 Mining Speed / +35 Block Fortune / +70 Fleet Bonus
- EPIC: +40 Mining Speed / +45 Block Fortune / +85 Fleet Bonus
- LEGENDARY: +50 Mining Speed / +55 Block Fortune / +100 Fleet Bonus
- MYTHIC: +75 Mining Speed / +65 Block Fortune / +125 Fleet Bonus
- DIVINE: +100 Mining Speed / +75 Block Fortune / +150 Fleet Bonus
Fleet Bonus: Grants +??? Mining Spread on Blocks.
Dwarven Geode (renamed from Rock Gemstone)
Designer's Note: Dwarven Geode is now a nice mix between Mining Speed and Fortune, although its reforge bonus can prove quite powerful in the later stages of mining. Like Refined Amber, this item was introduced before the introduction of the Crystal Hollows. "Rock" is not a type of Gemstone.
- COMMON: +10 Mining Speed / +5 Mining Fortune / +0.4% Auspicious Bonus
- UNCOMMON: +20 Mining Speed / +10 Mining Fortune / +0.5% Auspicious Bonus
- RARE: +30 Mining Speed / +15 Mining Fortune / +0.6% Auspicious Bonus
- EPIC: +40 Mining Speed / +20 Mining Fortune / +0.7% Auspicious Bonus
- LEGENDARY: +50 Mining Speed / +25 Mining Fortune / +0.8% Auspicious Bonus
- MYTHIC: +75 Mining Speed / +35 Mining Fortune / +0.9% Auspicious Bonus
- DIVINE: +100 Mining Speed / +50 Mining Fortune / +1% Auspicious Bonus
Auspicious Bonus: Grants +???% Mining Fortune.
Frigid Husk
Designer's Note: Frigid Husk is intended to be the best Reforge Stone for Mining Fortune, but only when you are accumulating Cold. This is to further encourage mining in the Glacite Tunnels and Mineshafts, but it also has an active risk assessment element to the reforge bonus. Ironically, at high Cold you will be needing lots of Mining Speed, which this Reforge Stone provides none of!
- COMMON: +3 Mining Fortune / +0.5 Glacial Bonus
- UNCOMMON: +6 Mining Fortune / +0.7 Glacial Bonus
- RARE: +9 Mining Fortune / +0.9 Glacial Bonus
- EPIC: +13 Mining Fortune / +1.1 Glacial Bonus
- LEGENDARY: +16 Mining Fortune / +1.4 Glacial Bonus
- MYTHIC: +20 Mining Fortune / +1.7 Glacial Bonus
- DIVINE: +25 Mining Fortune / +2 Glacial Bonus
Glacial Bonus: Grants +??? Mining Fortune per 1 Cold you have.
Lapis Crystal
Designer's Note: Lapis Crystal is another super early game Reforge Stone, being in the same category as Black Diamonds. This reforge goes all in on Mining Speed as opposed to the Black Diamond, which has some Mining Fortune. The reforge bonus has also been buffed so that early game players can earn more EXP while mining, meaning faster enchants.
- COMMON: +10 Mining Speed / +25% Magnetic Bonus
- UNCOMMON: +20 Mining Speed / +35% Magnetic Bonus
- RARE: +30 Mining Speed / +45% Magnetic Bonus
- EPIC: +40 Mining Speed / +55% Magnetic Bonus
- LEGENDARY: +50 Mining Speed / +65% Magnetic Bonus
- MYTHIC: +75 Mining Speed / +80% Magnetic Bonus
- DIVINE: +100 Mining Speed / +100% Magnetic Bonus
Magnetic Bonus: Earn more +???% EXP when mining.
Petrified Starfall
Designer's Note: Petrified Starfall has been changed to become a more general purpose Reforge Stone for mining. The caveat is that it now becomes the best reforge in the Dwarven Mines (by a significant margin) while you are near Fallen Stars, granting more Mithril Powder and Mining Fortune.
- COMMON: +5 Mining Speed / +5 Mining Fortune / +8%/25 Stellar Bonus
- UNCOMMON: +10 Mining Speed / +10 Mining Fortune / +9%/30 Stellar Bonus
- RARE: +15 Mining Speed / +15 Mining Fortune / +10%/35 Stellar Bonus
- EPIC: +25 Mining Speed / +20 Mining Fortune / +12%/45 Stellar Bonus
- LEGENDARY: +40 Mining Speed / +25 Mining Fortune / +14%/55 Stellar Bonus
- MYTHIC: +55 Mining Speed / +35 Mining Fortune / +17%/65 Stellar Bonus
- DIVINE: +70 Mining Speed / +50 Mining Fortune / +20%/75 Stellar Bonus
Stellar Bonus: Earn +???% Mithril Powder and ??? Mining Fortune near Fallen Stars.
Pure Mithril
Designer's Note: Pure Mithril is in the same space as Petrified Starfall, at its best while in the Dwarven Mines. The difference here is that Pure Mithril is better for those who like to mine more passively. While you won't be getting the bonus Mithril Powder and some or quite as much Mining Fortune, you won't have to move every time a new Fallen Star lands.
- COMMON: +3 Mining Fortune / +10 Mithraic Bonus
- UNCOMMON: +6 Mining Fortune / +15 Mithraic Bonus
- RARE: +9 Mining Fortune / +20 Mithraic Bonus
- EPIC: +13 Mining Fortune / +25 Mithraic Bonus
- LEGENDARY: +16 Mining Fortune / +30 Mithraic Bonus
- MYTHIC: +20 Mining Fortune / +40 Mithraic Bonus
- DIVINE: +25 Mining Fortune / +50 Mithraic Bonus
Mithraic Bonus: Grants +??? Mining Fortune on Mithril and Titanium.
Scorched Topaz (renamed from Hot Stuff)
Designer's Note: Scorched Topaz has been reworked into a more competitive option for mining in the Magma Fields, much like a counterpart to the Frigid Husk, but for Heat instead. The item was renamed because frankly the old name was just not great, among other reasons.
- COMMON: +5 Mining Speed / +3 Mining Fortune / +0.3/+1 Heated Bonus
- UNCOMMON: +10 Mining Speed / +6 Mining Fortune / +0.4/+1.4 Heated Bonus
- RARE: +15 Mining Speed / +9 Mining Fortune / +0.5/+1.8 Heated Bonus
- EPIC: +25 Mining Speed / +13 Mining Fortune / +0.6/+2.2 Heated Bonus
- LEGENDARY: +40 Mining Speed / +16 Mining Fortune / +0.7/+2.8 Heated Bonus
- MYTHIC: +55 Mining Speed / +20 Mining Fortune / +0.8/+3.4 Heated Bonus
- DIVINE: +70 Mining Speed / +25 Mining Fortune / +1/+4 Heated Bonus
Heated Bonus: Grants +??? Mining Fortune and +??? Mining Speed per 1 Heat you have.

## ► 16 New Items
### ➥ Precursor Apparatus
- Can be crafted using all 6 robot parts dropped from Automatons (alphabetical order!)
- Give this item to Professor Robot to instantly complete the Sapphire Crystal Quest
- Individual robot parts are no longer used in Forge Recipes, this item is used instead (see below)
  - Gemstone Fuel Tank: 4
  - Ruby-Polished Drill Engine: 4
  - Sapphire-Polished Drill Engine: 8
  - Amber-Polished Drill Engine: 16

- Recipe unlocked at Cobblestone IX

  - Miner's Outfit Armor (now Prospecting Armor) has moved to Iron Ingot II
- Grants +25 Mining Speed and permanent Haste III
### ➥ Royal Compass
- Obtained after completing Commission Milestone I
  - Can be purchased for 50k coins from Rusty
- Points towards the nearest Emissary (or King if he is closer)
### ➥ Dwarven Treasure
- Obtained as a reward for participating in Dwarven Events
- Equipment Reforge Stone that grants Mining Speed and Fortune
- Reforge Bonus grants additional Mithril Powder from Dwarven Events
### ➥ Flowstate Ultimate Enchantment
- Obtained as a reward for participating in Dwarven Events
- Ultimate Enchantment for Mining Tools
- Grants progressively more Mining Speed as you mine blocks consecutively
### ➥ Titanium Minecart
- Obtained as a reward for participating in Dwarven Events
- Pet Item that grants +33 Mining Fortune on Titanium
### ➥ Titanium Tesseract
- Forged using x1 Refined Titanium and x16 Enchanted Lapis Lazuli
- Armor Reforge Stone that grants Mining Wisdom and Speed
- Reforge bonus grants +10 Mithril Powder every time your mine Titanium
### ➥ Polished Topaz Rod
- Forged using x2 Flawless Topaz Gemstones and x3 Bejeweled Handles
- Mid game Lava Rod that reduces your Heat when fishing up Lava Sea Creatures
### ➥ Rusty Titanium Pickaxe
- Purchased from Bubu for 50k coins
- Early game Pickaxe that grants a lot of Mining Fortune when mining Titanium
- Upgrades into the Titanium Pickaxe
### ➥ Blazen Sphere
- 1% drop rate from Lava Blazes
- Equipment Reforge Stone that grants Mining Fortune and Heat Resistance
- Reforge Bonus grants bonus chance to dig up Worms the higher your Heat
### ➥ Starfall Seasoning
- Forged using x64 Starfall and x16 Treasurite
- Early game Drill Upgrade Module that grants Mining Speed and Fortune, doubled while near Fallen Stars
### ➥ Pocket Iceberg
- Forged using x5 Glacite Jewels
- Mining Tool Reforge Stone that grants Breaking Power
- Reforge Bonus grants Mining Fortune if you are mining a block with the same Breaking Power as your tool
### ➥ 4 Dwarven O's
- There is one for each sub-type of Mining Fortune (Ore, Block, Dwarven Metal, Gemstone)
  - Ore Oats: Earned from Dwarven Events
  - Block Bran: Drops from Scathas
  - Gemstone Grahams: Drops from Crystal Nucleus
  - Metallic Minis: Drops from Tungsten and Umber corpses
- Consuming it grants +30 of that sub-type of Mining Fortune for 60m
- The first 5 times you consume one you get a permanent +2 of that sub-type of Mining Fortune
## ► Brynmor
A new NPC, Brynmor has shown up at the tavern in the Dwarven Village! He is selling 2 new consumables and 1 revamped consumable:
- Powder Pumpkin
  - Renamed from Mining Pumpkin
  - Costs 50,000 coins
  - Grants +5% Mithril Powder for 60m
  - What happened to Malmar?
- Filet O' Fortune
  - Costs 100,000 coins
  - Grants +20 Mining Fortune for 60m
- Chilled Pristine Potato
  - Costs 250,000 coins
  - Grants +0.1 Pristine (while in Glacite Mineshafts)
## ► Dwarven Event Improvements
All players who "participate" in an active Dwarven Event are now given the following rewards:
- 1 Item
- 20,000 Mining XP
- 200 HOTM XP
- 2,000 Mithril Powder
- 20 Bits (if you have a Booster Cookie active)
The item you get can be one of the following:
- x1 Flowstate I Book
- x1 Dwarven O's Ore Oats
- x1 Dwarven Treasure
- x1 Rare Mining Skill EXP Boost
- x1 Titanium Minecart
If the lobby achieves a lobby bonus in that event, all participators get an additional item (2 total). Note that the Raffle doesn't have a "lobby bonus", instead you get the additional item if you win.
Some of the participation requirements for events have changed:
- Goblin Raid: 25 > 50 Kills
- Raffle: N/A > 10 Tickets
## ► Other
### ➥ Bingo Changes
Due to significant changes to early game progression and requirements for mining, we have gone ahead and pre-emptively made changes to the Bingo Pet to counteract this.
- New COMMON Perk: Mountain Climber
  - Increases HOTM XP gain by 100% - 250%
- Since buffed HOTM XP now has its own standalone perk, Fast Learner no longer affects HOTM XP gain
We will be monitoring upcoming Bingo events to see if further changes are necessary.
### ➥ Crystal Nucleus Changes
You now need to re-complete the Crystal Nucleus in order to pick up Crystals again. Certain Crystals now also require re-completing their objectives in order for you to collect them again.
Designer's Note: This change was made as an anti-abuse measure for forging Perfect Gemstones. We are looking at potentially making other changes to hasten how quickly runs can be completed to compensate for this.
### ➥ Refactored Custom Blocks
With this update we have optimized how custom blocks function, and as a result we have had to make some changes to Sulphur Ore in the Crimson Isles.
- You now need to be within 4 blocks when catching Sulphur Skitters
- Mobs are no longer corrupted while near Sulphur Ore
### ➥ Railway Station Changes
We have removed the Gates to the Mines Railway Station and replaced it with a new station in the lower part of the Royal Mines (where many of the new Dwarven Ores are). In the past this was a fairly unknown place, and since it now has added relevance we decided to put a station here. The Gates to the Mines station was hardly ever used and was fairly close to the Dwarven Village station anyways, so we decided to swap it out.
### ➥ Rhys' Quest Changes
Rhys will now ask you for 15 total of any enchanted mining resource, instead of 10 enchanted resources for each of the 3 different types (3o total). Effectively this means you can use your leftover enchanted resources from going through the Deep Caverns without having to over-commit to just a few of them.
### ➥ Requirement Changes
- Jaderald: Mining XX > XXX
- Pickonimbus 2000: N/A > IV
- Mithril Drill SX-226: HOTM III > II
- Mithril Drill SX-326: HOTM IV > III
- Ruby Drill TX-15: HOTM III > IV

- Perfectly-Cut Fuel Tank: HOTM V > VIII

- Ruby-Polished Drill Engine: HOTM V > VII
- Sapphire-Polished Drill Engine: HOTM VI > VIII
- Amber-Polished Drill Engine: HOTM VII > IX
- Goblin Omelette: HOTM IV > III
- Pesto Goblin Omelette: HOTM IV > V
- Sunny Side Goblin Omelette: HOTM IV > V
- Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette: HOTM VI > V

  - Green Goblin Egg: +5,000 Fuel

  - Red Goblin Egg: +15,000 Fuel

  - All Fossils: +50,000 Fuel

- The Mithril and Refined Mithril Pickaxes now use Diamond Pickaxes as their icon

- Swapped the positions of Enchanted Mithril and the Spelunker Potion in the Mithril Collection

- Decreased the number of kills needed to max the Treasure Hoarder bestiary (Bracket III > IV)

- You can now open the Drill Mechanic's menu without a Drill in your inventory
- The Corpse Looter commission now only requires looting 2 corpses instead of 3

- Reworked the item lores of Drill Parts
- Centered all the icons in the Lift Operator menu
- Added a HOTM IV requirement to accessing Lumina's shop
- Polarvoid Book now gives +5 Mining Fortune instead of +1 Breaking Power when it's first applied
- Zog's Shop now sells an Uncommon Mining Exp Boost instead of a Rare Mining Exp Boost
### ➥ Backend NMS Blocks & Entities Refactor
Separately to our changes to "custom blocks" in SkyBlock, some backend internals of our server software have been rewritten to accommodate more blocks and entities from newer versions of Minecraft. This is also being tested during this update's alpha cycle. If you notice anything weird with block placement/breaking, block hitboxes, entity behaviour, entity hitboxes, etc. please report those as we look to address them before Foraging.
## ► Bug Fixes
- Power Scrolls can now be applied to mining tools with Pickaxe Abilities on them
- Power Scrolls can now be applied to weapons with Necron's Blade Scrolls applied (Hyperion)
- Updated Sky Mall description so that it now mentions "all Mining Islands" rather than just the Dwarven Mines
  - It already worked this way, the description was just outdated
- Fixed Fortunate using Mithril Powder instead of Gemstone Powder
- Fixed the Pickonimbus 2000 not counting as an EPIC item in the salvage menu
- Fixed outdated text in a Glacite Tunnels commission description
- Fixed outdated text in an error message for Vanguard Corpses
- Fixed outdated text in an error message for the Lift Operator
- Fixed Ascension Rope item lore not mentioning it could be used to get out of Glacite Mineshafts
- Fixed the Sapphire Power Scroll not displaying the fact that it has a cooldown
## ► Alpha Item
Talk to Centauri to learn how you can earn an exclusive item on the main server.
Note: Some things in the changelog may be outdated or entirely missing.

# Changelog/2024/February 28
Categories: 2024 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[February 28th] Alpha Testing - New Dragon Content, Tab List Widgets, and more!
Updated: February 28, 2024
Hello!
It's time for some more testing of SkyBlock content on the Alpha Network. Today's additions include the following:
- Dragon Rush and Primal Dragons
- Tablist Widgets
- General's Medallion Accessory
- Pet Buffs and Reworks
- ... and more!
Read on for more details about them!
## ► Dragon Rush and Primal Dragons
The End Island was introduced very early in SkyBlock with a central dragon fight in the Dragon's Nest. With this update, we're introducing a fun new twist that allows you to fight multiple dragons at the same time and a new dragon type: The Primal Dragon! These new events can only occur by placing Awakened Summoning Eyes on the altar, a new item crafted with 8x Summoning Eyes and 2x Null Ovoids. Try them out on the Alpha Network!
## ► Tablist Widgets
You've seen this one before! Thanks to your suggestions, we've greatly improved a lot of things regarding Tablist Widgets. Looking forward for some more feedback about them when you've checked them out this alpha session.
Hypixel Team said: On most islands, the tablist will be replaced by a customizable tablist which contains so-called widgets. You can change your settings using the SkyBlock settings menu or running the /widgets command.
## ► General's Medallion
General's Medallion is a new accessory that you can obtain from dungeons. Look for Zodd in a new dungeon room to purchase one from him!
General's Medallion boosts your dungeon item stats. The amount this accessory boosts your stats by scales off of your secret count. As we currently do not track your secret count per profile, we've done some magic to split your secret count among all eligible profiles.
EDIT: Secret Count migration will not work on Alpha as your achievement data is not migrated over. This is a known issue.
## ► Pet Buffs and Reworks
Nothing much to see here, just a dozen or so changes to pet stats, perks, and even perk reworks. Open the spoiler below to see the full list of changes.
Code:
## Ghoul Pet

### Base Stats

- The Vitality from “Amplified Healing” should become a pet stat instead. The “Undead Slayer” perk (see below) should replace “Amplified Healing”.
- Add Mending to the pet’s base stats equal to the amount of vitality it has.

### Undead Slayer (Replacement of “Amplified Healing”)

- Gain 1.005x ➜ 1.5x Combat XP against Zombies.

### Reaper Soul (Rework)

- Reaper Soul reduces the summoning cost of mobs by 0.3% ➜ 30% and increases their damage output by 0.2% ➜ 20%.
- Reword the part about health of summoned mobs to “Increases the health of all summoned mobs by 1 ➜ 100%”.

### Army of the Dead (Replacement of “Zombie Arm”)

- Increase the amount of souls you can store and summon by 2. Also increases the chance of getting a mob’s soul by 0 ➜ 20%.

## Giraffe Pet

### Base Stats

- Add +0.01 ➜ +1 Swing Range to the pet’s Base Stats.

### Good Heart (Rework)

- Gain +x Health Regen.
    - Common: 0.015% ➜ 1.5%
    - Uncommon, Rare: 0.02% ➜ 2%
    - Epic, Legendary: 2 ➜ 200

### Long Neck (Rework)

- Increases your melee damage by 50% ➜ 100% if you are more than 3 blocks away from the target.

### Higher Ground (Rework)

- Increase your Crit Damage and Strength by 0.0015% ➜ 0.15% for every 0.1 Swing Range over 3. Capped at 6 Swing Range.
    - There are many ways you can gain swing range and figure out how to best utilize the Giraffe Pet. You can easily reach the 6 Swing Range cap while in dungeons as Berserker, or cobble together an interesting combination of items in the overworld to reach the 6 Swing Range cap. Hopefully this opens up more options to players and enjoy theorycrafting what works best!

## Golem Pet

### Base Stats

- Add +0.01 ➜ 1 Swing Range to the pet’s base stats.

### Last Stand (Rework)

- While at less than 20% HP, reduce incoming and outgoing damage by 20%. Additionally, gain a temporary shield equal to 10% of the player's maximum health. (Lasts 5 seconds, 60s cooldown).

### Toss (Rework)

- Toss deals 5x damage per hit. Cooldown reduced to 5s.

## Hound Pet

### Base Stats

- Change the Attack Speed stat to +0 ➜ +25 Attack Speed.

### Pack Slayer (Replacement of “Fury Claws”

- Gain 1.005x ➜ 1.5x Combat XP against Wolves.

### Finder (Rework)

- Change the scaling of the perk to 0.3% ➜ 30% chance increase to find Armor.
    - This is done as the current 10% is kinda overshadowed by literally anything that gives Magic Find.

## Megalodon Pet

### Base Stats

- Add +5 ➜ +10 Ferocity to the pet’s base stats.
- Add +0 ➜ 40 Fishing Speed to the pet’s base stats.
- Add 0 ➜ 10 Sea Creature Chance to the pet’s base stats.

### Blood Scent (Rework)

- Deal up to +50% ➜ +150% Damage based on the enemy's missing health.

### Enhanced Scales (Rework)

- Doubles the pet's base stats during the Fishing Festival.

### Feeding Frenzy (Rework)

- Increases your chance to catch Sharks during the Fishing Festival by 20%.

## Pigman Pet

### Giant Slayer (Rework)

- Deal +50% extra damage to monsters level 50 and up. Deal +75% extra damage to monsters level 100 and up.
    - This used to be a +25% **additive** modifier (that is very bad compared to every other pet) that procced almost never).

## Skeleton Pet

### Bone Arrows (Rework)

- Increase arrow damage by +0 ➜ +75% (at Legendary). This effect is doubled while in Dungeons.
    - This damage is an **additive** modifier.
    - Rework this for other rarities because they give less arrow damage.

### Skeletal Defense (Bug Fix)

- The arrow your skeleton shoots will no longer bounce.

## Snowman Pet

### Blizzard (Rework)

- Enemies within 8 ➜ 16 blocks are slowed by 25% and deal 0.15% ➜ 15% less damage.

### Snow Cannon (Rework)

- Shoot a snowball towards an enemy when you attack.
    - The snowman does NOT attack by itself anymore, it now only triggers when you attack.
- The snowball damage is equal to 10% ➜ 20% of the last melee damage hit you dealt.
- Cooldown: 1 Second

## Spider Pet

### Base Stats

- Change the Strength stat to +0 ➜ +50.

### One With The Spider (Rework)

- Add x Strength to every piece of Arachnid weapon, armor, and equipment that you have equipped. (applied to base stats).
    - +1 ➜ 3 Strength at Common, 0.02 per level.
    - +2 ➜ 6 Strength at Uncommon, 0.04 per level.
    - +3 ➜ 9 Strength at Rare, 0.06 per level.
    - +4 ➜ 12 Strength at Epic, 0.08 per level.
    - +5 ➜ 15 Strength at Legendary. 0.1 per level
- Holding an Arachnid weapon counts as having it equipped.
- Spider-related items, such as Tarantula Armor, Arachne Equipment etc. have been assigned the Arachnid tag for easier reference.

## Turtle Pet

### Base Stats

- Change the Health stat to +50 ➜ +75.
- Change the Defense stat to +100 ➜ +150.
- Add True Defense 0 ➜ +15 to the pet’s base stats.

### Turtle Tactics (Rework)

- Gain +3.27 % ➜ +30% Defense. Standing still will grant another +10% Defense.

### Genius Amniote (Rework)

- Grants +1% ➜ +2.5% Defense to up to 4 players within 50 blocks of you.
    - This effect does **NOT** stack with multiple turtle pets!

### Turtle Shell (Rework)

- When under 40% maximum HP, take 0.25 ➜ 25% less damage. Gain +10 ➜ +20 Vitality for 15 seconds after taking 10 hits.
    - Hits are instances of damage.
    - 10 instances of damage will grant you the Turtle Shell vitality bonus. If you already have the bonus, it will refresh the duration of the vitality buff.

## Wither Skeleton Pet

### Wither Blood (Rework)

- Deal +125% more damage to wither mobs.
    - Contrary to what it seems, the change from a 25% multiplicative to a 125% additive modifier is actually a lot stronger for most players. For players a lot further into progression where this change is a slight nerf, you have other pets at your disposal that deal more damage than Wither Skeleton in general anyway.

## Zombie Pet

### Bite Shield (Rework of “Chomp”)

- Reduce damage taken from Zombies by x% ➜ y%.
    - Common: 5% ➜15%
    Uncommon, Rare: 10% ➜ 20%
    Epic, Legendary: 15% ➜ 25%

### Rotten Blade (Rework)

- 25% Damage to Zombies ➜ Deal 150% more damage to Zombies.

### Living Dead (Rework)

- 0.25 ➜ 25% Stats on Undead Armor (instead of 0.2 ➜ 20%).

### Unlock Requirement

- The current way to unlock the Zombie Pet craft is Zombie Collection 9. Let’s move it down to Zombie Collection 6, and change the crafting recipe to 16*8 Enchanted Rotten Flesh.
    - This makes the pet more accessible to it’s intended new-player audience!
If you encounter any issues - small, major or critical - please report them here and make sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!
If you have any feedback for us, make sure to drop it in the Alpha Network forums section!

[January 29th] Alpha Testing - Tablist Widgets
Updated: January 29, 2024
Hi all,
Alpha is open for the testing the Tablist Widgets update in SkyBlock. Please help us find bugs and improve its stability before it's released at a later time!
On most islands, the tablist will be replaced by a customizable tablist which contains so-called widgets. You can change your settings using the SkyBlock settings menu or running the /widgets command.
While in development, we played around with different ways to customize, different features and different menus. Being limited by Minecraft menus, we know that some parts of the menus will take some time to get used to, but we think we found a good compromise between complexity and ease-of-use.

# Changelog/2024/January 31
Categories: 2024 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[January 31st] Alpha Testing - God Potion and Mixin Changes
Updated: January 31, 2024
Hello,
Today we're testing a change to how we handle God Potions and improved the functionality of Mixins. We're testing these changes on Alpha early to help look out for profile-related issues.
Here's the details regarding the God Potion rework:
- You can now stack God Potion and Mixin timers! Drinking multiple God Potions will result in the God Potion timer being added on to your existing time instead of refreshing your God Potion duration.
- Mixins now each have a separate timer to God Potions. When you don't have a God Potion active, Mixin timers are paused.
- You can directly consume a Mixin by right clicking it as long as you have a God Potion and Cookie Buff active.
  - The mixin duration has the same duration modifiers as the God Potion.
  - The tooltip on your Mixin dynamically changes depending on whether you have a God Potion or a Cookie Buff active.
- The active effects menu now has a filter. Check it out with the /effects command.
If you encounter any issues - small, major or critical - please report them here and make sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!
If you have any feedback for us, make sure to drop it in the Alpha Network forums section!

# Changelog/2024/July 31
Categories: 2024 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[July 31] The Artist's Abode
Updated: July 31, 2024
Hello, fellow artisans!
The Artist's Abode community project has been ongoing for a while now, and will soon be complete! Vincent has been eagerly awaiting the moment when he is finally able to move into his new home on the main network, but first he will need to make a quick stop by the Alpha server, just to make sure everything's working as intended.
August 1st/2nd Changes
The following changes may not be live on Alpha at the time of you reading this, but are planned before release:
Dye Adjustments
- Changed Celadon Dye from 1/100k to 1/20k from Bacte
- Changed Jade Dye from 1/20M to 1/2M from Crystal Nucleus
  - Keep in mind this dye rolls multiple times per crystal nucleus completion action.
- Changed Nyanza Dye from 1/1M to 1/250k from Mining Comms
- Changed Tentacle Dye to the following:
  - Kuudra Basic Tier → 1/250k
  - Kuudra Hot Tier → 1/200k
  - Kuudra Burning Tier → 1/150k
  - Kuudra Fiery Tier → 1/100k
  - Kuudra Infernal Tier → 1/50k
- Changed Frostbitten Dye to the following:
  - Regular Corpses -> 1/1M
  - Tungsten/Umber Corpses -> 1/250k
  - Vanguard Corpses -> 1/25k
    - Keep in mind this dye rolls multiple times per corpse loot action.
- Changed Fossil Dye from 1/5M Fossil Excavator to 1/1M
  - Keep in mind this dye rolls multiple times per fossil excavation.
- Changed Carmine Dye to the following:
  - The most common mobs go from 1/10M to 1/5M
  - The middle section of mobs goes from 1/5M to 1 / 2.5M
  - The rarest section of mobs (jawb,plhleg,thunder) goes from 1/500k to 1/250k
- Removed Livid Dye from Floor V loot - now only drops from Master Mode Floor V
- Changed Secret Dye from 1/5M to 1/1M
- Changed Nadeshiko Dye to the following:
  - Supreme Superpairs Experiment -> From 1/50k chance to appear as a pair
  - Transcendent Superpairs Experiment -> 1/25k chance to appear as a pair
  - Metaphysical Superpairs Experiment -> 1/10k chance to appear as a pair
- Changed Pelt Dye to the following:
  - 1/1M Common Trapper Animal
  - 1/750K Uncommon Trapper Animal
  - 1/500k Rare Trapper Animal
  - 1/250k Epic Trapper Animal
  - 1/50k Legendary Trapper Animal
- Changed Sangria Dye to the following:
  - 1/150k from T1 Riftstalker
  - 1/100k from T2 Riftstalker
  - 1/75k from T3 Riftstalker
  - 1/50k from T4 Riftstalker
  - 1/25k from T5 Riftstalker
- Changed Copper Dye to the following:
  - 1/100k from UNCOMMON
  - 1/50k from RARE
  - 1/25k from LEGENDARY
  - 1/5k from MYTHIC
  - 1/500 from SPECIAL
Other
- Increased base rarity of Bucket of Dye accessory from Uncommon to Rare
- Changed the formatting of some of Vincent's dialogue
- Clarified in the Dye Compendium that only the Revenant Horror slayer has a Tier V - we apologize for the unintended hype but no other Tier V bosses are currently in development, this was merely a misinput
- Clarified in the Dye Compendium that Periwinkle's best odds cap out at 1/5M
- Moved Flares from the middle bracket of viable Cyclamen mobs to the rarest one.
More to come!
## ► The Artist's Abode
Found on the outskirts of the Village near what's left of the Colosseum, the Artist's Abode is home to Vincent. A master artisan, Vincent prefers to paint with dyes, and somehow always manages to find exactly what he needs to paint his latest masterpiece.
### ➥ Included in this update:
- Vincent's Masterpiece
  - Talk to Vincent to see what he's currently painting! Every SkyBlock Year, Vincent will choose 3 dyes at random to use in his latest work. Two of these dyes will have their drop rates increased by 2x throughout the year, while the other will have its rates increased by a whopping 3x!
- Dye Balancing
  - As part of this update, we have taken a look at all dyes currently dropped from playing Hypixel SkyBlock, and have made a number of changes to either drop rates, drop sources, or both. We will give you some detailed insight into our rationale when making these changes as part of the Artist's Abode update thread, but the TL;DR is that we would like dyes to drop from expansive content with progression systems tied to them (think dungeons, crystal nucleus, etc.) as opposed to niche sources (mushroom cows, sneaky creepers, etc.) that essentially create a grind out of nothing.
- 14 New Dyes!
  - Now that we've adjusted dye drop rates a bit, we're happy to add some new dyes that add a bit more vibrancy to the current dye palette. These new dyes look to add a tad more vibrancy to the existing dye palette and drop from sources of intentionally repeatable content such as slayers, pests, the crystal nucleus, and more!
- Dye Compendium
  - Viewable within Vincent's Masterpiece menu, the Dye Compendium contains an exhaustive list of every dye currently in the game, its drop source, and its drop rate! This is particularly useful for players wanting to hunt dyes that have a varying drop rate (for example, the Matcha Dye, which drops from the Revenant Horror, is far more likely to drop from a T5 than it is from a T1).
- Bucket of Dye Accessory
  - The Bucket of Dye is a unique new accessory that not only makes dye-hunting easier, but also comes in many colors! Depending upon which color dye you donate to Vincent, the color of the accessory you receive will change!
Please note that we will be monitoring community feedback to this Alpha and will balance dye drop rates accordingly. Additionally, following the update's eventual release, we will monitor our dye-related metrics and, if any dyes are still far too rare or even start to become too common, we will more than likely make further changes. Our goal, however, is for players to have a very small chance of dropping a dye when playing the core game as opposed to having to grind niche content that is often intended for early-game players and thus doesn't give any meaningful rewards to more advanced players if they aren't lucky enough to drop a dye.
### ➥ Other Changes
- Bomb Defuse puzzle has been removed from The Catacombs.
- Some Gemstone Slot Additions
  - A Peridot Gemstone Slot have been added to the Pumpkin and Melon Dicers.
  - Dark Claymore's Gemstone Slots are now Combat Gemstone Slots instead of locked to Jasper only.
- When all the abiphone relays are fixed, Fred will no longer complain about bad signal.
- The Nucleus Loot Bundle reward summary message has been improved.
- Added /setmaxspeed command to change Rancher's Boots speed without having to take them off.
- Added button in Bazaar to claim all coins from all sell orders.
- Fixed an issue where votes for hidden elections could still be viewed in Dungeons.
- Removed NPC sell price from Dyes.
- Adjusted the hex color of the Midnight Dye.
- Fixed an issue causing the Midnight Dye not to drop.
- Fixed an issue causing players with 100% Sea Creature Chance to not roll for Dyes.
- Adjusted how Dyes are displayed in the Bestiary from percentage to raw odds.
- Added a lobby-wide message that is sent to all players whenever someone drops a Dye.

# Changelog/2024/June 13
Categories: 2024 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[June 13] Better Mayors Changes 3
Updated: June 13, 2024
Hello!
We're back with another series of changes and bug fixes for Better Mayors.
Quick heads up that we will be disabling the Alpha Checklist within an hour of posting this thread, meaning that the Campaign Poster memento will no longer be obtainable once the Alpha Network updates with changes. The Alpha Network will remain up as we continue to troubleshoot some issues.
Changes
- The Ravenous Rhino now only visits your Garden after you've unlocked 50 unique visitors.
- Bait is now no longer consumed while playing Catch a Fish.
- Pest Vest has been added to the Museum.
- Magic Find from the Starred Daedalus Axe now correctly displays that magic find upon killing Mythological creatures.
- Disabled the Grapping Hook in Catch a Fish.
- Revamped the Fruit Digging Carnival Minigame.
  - You can now dig 15 times instead of 10.
  - There are now bombs and rum on the field.
  - Your shovel has the "Dowse" ability that helps you get a higher score.
  - The digging animation has been sped up significantly.
  - Introduced two new fruits.
  - Introductory dialogue has been rewritten/enhanced for a bunch of the Carnival Minigames.
- Added an individual sea creature cap of 5 in the Crimson Isle and 20 in the Crystal Hollows.
  - This change is to prevent players from hogging the sea creature cap on islands for their own barn fishing enjoyment.
    - The issue is the most prominent on the Crimson Isle so that island has the most stringent cap.
    - Implemented a smaller cap on the Crystal Hollows for worm fishing.
  - The overall sea creature cap remains at 60 sea creatures.
  - The HP and Damage of various Lava Sea Creatures have been significantly reduced so they can be killed much easier.
  - Please give feedback on this change!
- Divan's Powder Coating can now be applied to the Divan's Pendant to grant 10 Mining Fortune.
- Dwarven Handwarmers's Mining Speed has been increased from 15 to 45.
- Crown of Avarice now requires 2 Avaricious Chalices to craft instead of 4.
Bug Fixes
- Fixed some grammar issues in dialogue.
- Fixed the Party Chests using incorrect stairs to holster the chest.
- Fixed some formatting issues with leaderboard holograms.
- Fixed the Baker visitor having "null" as a requirement at the Visitor Logbook.
- Fixed the Zombie Shootout summary being inaccurate.
- Fixed an issue with Mineral Armor and it's upgrade having the bonus not given correctly.

# Changelog/2024/June 3
Categories: 2024 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[June 3] Better Mayors
Updated: June 3, 2024
Hello voters!
The long awaited Better Mayors update has finally arrived on Alpha! Continue reading if you want a full breakdown on all the changes and additions, or you can just read the TLDR below
TLDR:
- The runner up Candidate of an election becomes a "Minister" (one of their perks activates as if they get 2nd place)
- (Almost) Every mayor is getting a new 4th perk
- Diaz has completely changed her perks
- A new Carnival event for Foxy
- Much more!
Quick Note: We changed some internal menu logic with this update. If you notice any weird behavior, please report it here!
## ► Minister System
The governing body of SkyBlock (no relation to elected Mayors) have decreed that elections now also determine a new position: Ministers! Ministers are the runner up Candidates in an election. Every Mayor will have a perk chosen as a Minister Perk at the beginning of every election. If they become the Minister, this perk becomes active! If Aatrox becomes the Minister, all Slayer Quests are half price! Additional Notes:
- Special Mayors cannot have Ministers
- Ministers will not show up in the election after they are elected
- Unlike Mayors, Ministers will keep their perks when they do show up again and can still roll for additional perk
## ► Fourth Perks
(Almost) every Mayor is getting a new never-before-seen Fourth Perk! These new perks should help some of the less popular Mayors get elected more often.
Please refer to this handy table for how Candidates receive new perks:
|Current # of Perks|New # of Perks|Chance of Happening|
|1|2|50% (up from 45%)|
|2|3|50% (up from 45%)|
|3|4|8%|
We currently have no plans to introduce new never-before seen Fourth Perks to Special Mayors.
### ➥ Where are Aatrox and Paul's new never-before seen Fourth Perks?
You didn't hear this from me but apparently Aatrox and Paul failed to hand in their new campaign plans in enough time. Aatrox claimed he couldn't read Clerk Seraphine's memo (even though she sent out multiple, well in advance), and Paul had no Abiphone Cell Service while running M4 (which sounds like the least of his problems).
Once again, you didn't hear this from me, but I heard they had some eventful plans that will take longer to develop.
### ➥ Hiding Election Votes for Ongoing Elections
We've gathered that a lot of players would prefer if every Candidate's votes were not displayed during elections, so we are going to try something out. Once this update leaves the Alpha Network, we plan on alternating between elections where voting is private and elections where voting is public.
We have a hunch that you guys will end up preferring for the votes to be public so that you can strategize and "vote" for a Minister, but we'll see how everything plays out and will be monitoring for feedback.
## ► Mayor Perks
Exclusively for this Alpha playtest, we will be frequently rotating between different Mayors so that we can test as many different things as possible. The playtest will start with Foxy as Mayor, who has the most significant changes.
Marina
### ► Marina
We think the Fishing Festival is exciting enough as is, so Marina will just be getting a new perk.
#### New Perk: Double Trouble
For every 1% of Sea Creature Chance, gain 0.1% chance to double hook sea creatures (+0.1 Double Hook Chance).
- Yes, that means Double Hook Chance is officially becoming a stat.
- The Giant Fishing Rod and Double Hook Attribute have been updated to reflect this.
Diana
### ► Diana
The Mythological Ritual has plenty of content, so Diana is also only getting one new perk.
#### New Perk: Sharing is Caring
You can have up to 3 EXP shared pets active at once. Your EXP Share rate is increased by 10%.
#### ➥ EXP Sharing Rework
Diana's perk may have caught you off guard, that's because we have reworked how EXP Share works in SkyBlock! Now, rather than using the Exp Share item, you can simply use a menu to chose what Pet you want to EXP Share with. Your 'EXP Share rate' also goes up by 0.2% per Taming Level, up to a maximum of 12% at Taming LX. By default, you will only have one "active" EXP Share Slot. When Diana is in office with her 'Sharing is Caring' perk, two more EXP Share Slots will also become enabled. Those slots will no longer provide EXP when Diana leaves office, but you can still add/remove pets from those slots - they're not stuck in there
#### ➥ EXP Share Indicators
The three slots within the EXP Share menu have colorful indicators to tell you their status. Active Slots are Green: Slots that currently have a Pet in them that is getting EXP Share are considered active. Empty Slots are Yellow: Slots that are currently enabled but have no Pets in them are considered empty. Inactive Slots are Red: Slots that are currently disabled (like the second/third slots without Diana) are considered inactive. Max level Pets within otherwise active slots turn inactive, to show that they are no longer getting EXP Share.
#### ➥ What happened to the EXP Share item?
Instead of enabling the system as a whole, the EXP Share item now simply boosts the effectiveness of Pets receiving EXP Share by 15%. This item stacked with the 12% Exp Share rate from Taming LX means that you can reach a total of 27%, which is higher than the original items 25% (outside of Diana's 10% boost).
#### ➥ Favoriting Pets
We've taken the time to add some minor QoL to the Pets menu. With this feature, simply Shift + Left-Click to favorite a Pet. Favorited Pets show up at the front of your Pets menu so that you can access them easily. A favorited Level 100 Common Tiger Pet with 8,000,000 Overflow XP Designer’s Note: The current behavior with sorting on your favorite pets is that the sort still applies, but all favorited pets will appear before non favorited pets. This functionality can totally be adjusted if it’s not desirable for favorites to be affected by sorting. Please give feedback! We hope these changes are going to make EXP Sharing a lot more accessible to players, but you'll still need the original EXP Share item to get the best EXP Share rates!
Finnegan
### ► Finnegan
Finnegan is a little bit underwhelming, so we went a little deeper with his new perk!
#### New Perk: Blooming Business
Garden Visitors will give out Fine Flour and appear more often. Additional visitors may visit your Garden. Higher rarity Visitors are more likely to show up & will provide 10% more Copper.
#### ➥ Fine Flour
While Blooming Business is active, visitors will reward you with Fine Flour for completing their requests in addition to their regular rewards. The saga continues... The amount of Fine Flour a visitor gives out is dependent on their rarity: UNCOMMON - 1 Fine Flour RARE - 3 Fine Flour LEGENDARY - 5 Fine Flour MYTHIC - 10 Fine Flour
#### ➥ Four New Visitors
Also while Blooming Business is active, there is a chance that some new visitors may appear on your Garden. These include the Baker, the Vinyl Collector, the Pest Wrangler, and the Ravenous Rhino. We won't spoil everything here, but the Ravenous Rhino is a new MYTHIC visitor that is an additional source for Dedication IV books.
#### ➥ New Items
We have added some new items to Talbot's Shop while Pelt-pocalypse is active.
- Douce Pluie de Stinky Cheese Potion
  - Costs 40 Pelts
Douce Pluie de Stinky Cheese I will not be added to the God Potion
- Brown Bandana
  - Costs 200 Pelts
- Pest Vest
  - Costs 250 Pelts
We hope these additions give players a little more reason to gather Pelts.
Cole
### ► Cole
The Mining Fiesta's signature Refined Minerals have ran their course... so we're introducing Glossy Gemstones, a new material exclusive to it, along with a handful of other items. We're also giving Cole a fourth perk like the other Mayors.
#### New Perk: Molten Forge
Decreases the time it takes to forge by 25%.
#### Updated Perk: Mining Fiesta
Schedules five Mining Fiesta events that last for 7 days each. Earn 2x drops and extra unique loot, including Refined Minerals and Glossy Gemstones. Grants +75 ☯ Mining Wisdom during the event. (Only active on public islands.) Designer’s Note: This old description hasn't been updated to this new one on Alpha.
#### ➥ New Items
Refined Minerals aren't used for a whole lot, so we are introducing a whole array of new items and forge recipes that utilize them. Glossy Gemstones are also used in many of these!
- Mithril Equipment
  - Forge Requirement: HOTM 2
- Titanium Equipment
  - Forge Requirement: HOTM 3
- Gemstone Equipment
  - Forge Requirement: HOTM 5
- Divan’s Powder Coating
  - Forge Requirement: HOTM 7
- Gleaming Crystal Reforge Stone
  - Forge Requirement: HOTM 4
- Glossy Mineral Armor (upgrade to Mineral Armor)
  - Crafting Recipe (no requirement)
- Glossy Mineral Talisman (upgrade to Mineral Talisman)
  - Crafting Recipe (no requirement)
Foxy
### ► Foxy
Foxy was one of our most underwhelming Mayors, so we have significantly reworked her! She will be introducing a whole new event to SkyBlock (our 2nd this year!) along with new Party Chests and Party Gifts.
#### Updated Perk: Sweet Benevolence
Increases your chances of dropping Candy and Gifts by +30% during the Spooky Festival and Season of Jerry respectively.
#### New Perk: A Time for Giving
Party Chests and Party Gifts can be obtained while this event is active.
#### New Event Perk: Chivalrous Carnival
Schedules a Carnival in the hub, active throughout the entire year. Take on fun activities to earn Tokens and exchange them for perks and special items!
#### ➥ Sweet Benevolence
This perk is the result of merging Foxy's old Sweet Tooth and Benevolence perks.
#### ➥ Party Chests and Party Gifts
Foxy's A Time for Giving perk introduces two new items: Party Chests and Party Gifts. These items are only obtainable while the perk is active, and can drop some new items! Party Chests Party Chests are earned throughout the Spooky Festival, including the hour before and after the event. Party Chests outright replace Trick or Treat Chests, giving some bonus loot: Party Chest Drop Table
|Item|Weight|Drop Rate|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
|Carnival Necklace|5|1.04%|Found exclusively in Party Chests|
|Carnival Belt|5|1.04%|Found exclusively in Party Chests|
|Vampire Mask|5|1.04%|Found exclusively in Party Chests|
|Carnival Top Hat|2|0.42%|Found in both Party Chests and Party Gifts|
|Carnival Ticket|100|20.83%|Found in both Party Chests and Party Gifts|
|Trick or Treat Chest Items|363|75.6%|All existing loot from Trick or Treat Chests|
Party Gifts Party Gifts are earned by killing Winter Sea Creatures while on Jerry Island. Party Gifts upgrade the rewards found in Red Gifts, making them more worthwhile to open up.
- 2% from COMMON Winter Sea Creatures
- 5% from UNCOMMON Winter Sea Creatures
- 10% from RARE Winter Sea Creatures
- 20% from EPIC Winter Sea Creatures
- 40% from LEGENDARY Winter Sea CreaturesDesigner's Note: These rates are subject to changes throughout testing. The Reindrake also currently doesn't drop any Party Gifts while the event is active, but we'll definitely consider it!
Party Gift Drop Table
|Item|Weight|Drop Rate|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
|Carnival Gloves|15|0.7%|Found exclusively in Party Gifts|
|Carnival Cloak|15|0.7%|Found exclusively in Party Gifts|
|Snowman Mask|15|0.7%|Found exclusively in Party Gifts|
|Carnival Top Hat|5|0.2%|Found in both Party Chests and Party Gifts|
|Carnival Ticket|25|1.2%|Found in both Party Chests and Party Gifts|
|Red Gift Items|2060|96.5%|All existing loot from Red Gifts|
Buffed Loot On top of the new rewards, Party Chests and Party Gifts also buff the existing loot found in Trick or Treat Chests and Red Gifts respectively. What might've been 4 Ectoplasm in a Trick or Treat Chest might now be 6 Ectoplasm, for example.
### ⭐ Chivalrous Carnival
Foxy’s Chivalrous Carnival introduces a bunch of carnival games to the world of Hypixel SkyBlock! Designer's Note: We highly recommend enabling SkyBlock music while in the Carnival, @ShinkoNet made some incredible songs for this update!
#### ➥ Carnival Tickets
The Chivalrous Carnival has 3 games within it, each requiring 5 Carnival Tickets per play. Every day the Carnival Leader will give out 25 Carnival Tickets for free, just for talking with him. He is located at the entrance to the Chivalrous Carnival while the event is active. Carnival Tickets can also be earned by participating in many of SkyBlock's calendar events. Currently they can be earned through Party Chests, Party Gifts, Jacob's Farming Contests, Spooky Festivals, and Fishing Festivals. Foxy's Chivalrous Carnival perk does not need to be active for players to earn Carnival Tickets from these places. Additional Notes:
- By release, we will add a way to store Carnival Tickets in your sacks (so you won't need to take them out to use them)
- All Carnival Tickets are co-op Soulbound
#### ➥ Carnival Ticket Rates
- Jacob's Farming Contest
  - Diamond Medal: 3 Carnival Tickets
  - Gold and Platinum Medals: 2 Carnival Tickets
  - Silver and Bronze Medals: 1 Carnival Ticket
- Spooky Festival
  - Top 50: 50 Carnival Tickets
  - Emerald Bracket (5000 Points): 35 Carnival Tickets
  - Diamond Bracket (2000 Points): 25 Carnival Tickets
  - Gold Bracket (1750 Points): 20 Carnival Tickets
  - Iron Bracket (1500 Points): 15 Carnival Tickets
  - Stone Bracket (1000 Points): 10 Carnival Tickets
  - Wood Bracket (100 Points): 5 Carnival Tickets
- Fishing Festival
  - 0.1% drop rate from Nurse Sharks: 1 Carnival Ticket
  - 0.15% drop rate from Blue Sharks: 1 Carnival Ticket
  - 0.25% drop rate from Tiger Sharks: 1 Carnival Ticket
  - 0.5%%: Great White Shark: 1 Carnival Ticket
- Party Chests and Party Gifts
  - 20.83% from Party Chests
  - 1.2% from Party Gifts
Additional Notes:
- We aren't retroactively granting Carnival Tickets for Jacob's Farming Contests
- The Carnival Ticket drop rate from Sharks is affected by Looting and Magic Find
#### ➥ Carnival Games
There are three Carnival Games available at the Chivalrous Carnival. The Carnival Games will be available for as long as the perk is active.
#### Fruit Digging
Dig up 10 fruits from a sandpit to earn a high score. Each fruit gives you a different amount of points, and most of them have a unique effect to earn you even more (or less!) points.
#### Catch a Fish
The objective of Catch a Fish is to...catch fish. Catch as many as possible before time runs out by hooking onto them and holding right click to reel them in! There are different types of fish that give differing amounts of points, some are also harder to catch!
#### Zombie Shootout
Using your trusty Carnival Dart Tube, kill as many Zombies as you can in the shooting gallery before time runs out. Higher tier Zombies award more points, but of course run faster. Zombies have a chance of spawning as a Baby Zombie, who award double the points but run even faster. Redstone Lamps will occasionally light up in the gallery as well, make sure to shoot them for extra points!
Designer's Note: There is no SkyBlock XP tied to each individual minigame. You can pick your favorite carnival minigame to grind out your Carnival Tokens if you wish!
#### ➥ Scoring System and Carnival Tokens
Each of the three Carnival Games are scored in points. Earning 100 points gets you 1 Carnival Token. Carnival Tokens can be spent at the Mask Shop or the Event Shop, both found within the Carnival. Designer's Note: This conversion rate is subject to change throughout Alpha testing.
#### ➥ Carnival Shop
The Carnival Shop contains both the Mask Shop and the Event Shop, which use exclusively Carnival Tokens as currency!
#### Mask Shop
The Mask Shop sells six additional Carnival Masks on top of the ones you can already find throughout SkyBlock. Each mask has a niche ability, collect them all! Masks can also be kept in your Carnival Mask Bag for storage and ease of access, granting you Intelligence if you do so!
#### Event Shop
The Event Shop sells perks for various SkyBlock calendar events. Purchase them for permanent buffs! Designer Note: The perk costs are to be finalized during testing.
#### Spooky Festival
- Candy Scavenger I - V
  - Gain +2/4/6/8/10 more Candy from all sources during the Spooky Festival
  - Costs 50/100/150/200/250 Carnival Tokens
- Spooky Hook I - V
  - Increases your chance to catch Spooky Sea Creatures by +2/4/6/8/10%
  - Costs 50/100/150/200/250 Carnival Tokens
- Purple Vogue I - III
  - Grants +1/2/3% chance for Green Candy drops to be Purple Candy instead
  - Costs 100/200/400 Carnival Tokens
- Trick or Treat Enthusiast I-II
  - Increases your chances of finding Trick or Treat Chests and Party Chests by +0.5/1%
  - Costs 100/1000 Carnival Tokens
#### Jerry’s Workshop
- Present Connoisseur I - V
  - Gain +2/4/6/8/10% more Gifts from all sources during the Season of Jerry
  - Costs 50/100/150/200/250 Carnival Tokens
- Icy Hook I - V
  - Increases your chance to catch Winter Sea Creatures during the Season of Jerry by +2/4/6/8/10%
  - Costs 50/100/150/200/250 Carnival Tokens
- Red Fad I - III
  - Grants a +1/2/3% chance for Gift drops to be Red Gifts
  - Costs 100/200/400 Carnival Tokens
- Professional Gifter I-II
  - Gain +5/10% more coins and Skill EXP from Gifts
  - Costs 100/1000 Carnival Tokens
#### Mining Fiesta
- Refined Mind I - V
  - Grants +2/4/6/8/10% extra chance to find Refined Minerals and Glossy Gemstones during Mining Fiesta
  - Costs 50/100/150/200/250 Carnival Tokens
- Fortunate Festivity I - V
  - Gain +5/10/15/20/25 Mining Fortune during the Mining Fiesta
  - Costs 50/100/150/200/250 Carnival Tokens
- Powder Hoarder I - III
  - Gain +2/4/6% more Mithril Powder, Gemstone Powder, and Glacite Powder during the Mining Fiesta
  - Costs 100/200/400 Carnival Tokens
- Titanium Tenacity I-II
  - Increases your chances of finding Titanium by +3/6% during the Mining Fiesta
  - Costs 100/1000 Carnival Tokens
#### Fishing Festival
- Fishing Prodigy I - V
  - Gain +2/4/6/8/10 Fishing Wisdom during the Fishing Festival
  - Costs 50/100/150/200/250 Carnival Tokens
- Shark Sonar I - V
  - Increases your chance to catch Sharks by 2/4/6/8/10% during the Fishing Festival
  - Costs 50/100/150/200/250 Carnival Tokens
- Denture Collector I - III
  - Increase your chance of dropping Shark Teeth by +10/20/30% during the Fishing Festival
  - Costs 100/200/400 Carnival Tokens
- Elasmobranchii Affinity I-II
  - Grants a 50/100% chance for Sharks to drop +1 Shark Fin during the Fishing Festival
  - Costs 100/1000 Carnival Tokens
#### ➥ Stats and Rewards
We are tracking stats for all of your Carnival Game attempts! Talk to our rewards gal' Chantelle to check out your cumulative and yearly stats for all of the Carnival Games. Each time a Carnival is held, Chantelle offers refreshed objectives for players to complete. These give players significant rewards for your first few plays of each of the Carnival Games, resetting with each new Carnival.
#### ➥ Commemorative Items
If you played a Carnival Game at least once each time it's in town, you will earn that game's commemorative item. These will make it easy to keep track of all of your high scores and positions from year to year. Once the Carnival ends, these are automatically sent to your Event Rewards Menu.
Click to enlarge image
Diaz
### ► Diaz
Diaz was our most disappointing Mayor, so we have almost entirely reworked her. She now offers a new event, improvements to Shen's Auction and Special, and a unique perk that affects future elections!
#### New Perk: Volume Trading
Double the item quantity from Shen's Auction, Shen's Special, and Rift Shen's on the year Diaz is elected. Two additional Shen’s Special auctions will be available for the duration of Diaz being elected as Mayor.
#### New Event Perk: Stock Exchange
Diaz is bringing more Stock of Stonks into circulation. Stock of Stonks can be obtained from the Stonks Auction and winning bids for items in the Dark Auction and Shen’s Auctions. Talk to Diaz while she’s elected with this perk to bid for Stock of Stonks and trade in your Stock of Stonks in for some cool new items!
#### New Perk: Long Term Investment
The elected minister will appear in the next election with all of their available perks.
#### ➥ Moar Shen’s!
With this update, Shen's Auctions will be offered twice a year rather than just once. Mayor Diaz's Volume Trading perk will do three things:
- Double the number of Shen's Auctions yet again for that year (for a total of 4)
- Double the number of items at all Shen's Auctions for that year
- A new permanent item will be added to Shen's Special (if there are items available)Designer's Note: Only Shen's Auctions that end during Diaz's term will have doubled items.
#### ➥ Stock of Stonks Usage
Back in early 2020 we introduced the Stock of Stonks, a test item that coincided with the release of the Bazaar. This item has never had any use, only speculative value, at least until today. Mayor Diaz's Stock Exchange perk will introduce significantly more Stock of Stonks into the economy, but it will also allow you to trade them in for some new items:
- Golden Fragment
  - Costs x3 Stock of Stonks
  - Use x8 Golden Fragments to upgrade one of the following:
    - Daedalus Axe
    - Midas' Sword
    - Midas Staff
- Avaricious Chalice
  - Costs x50 Stock of Stonks
  - Use x1 Avaricious Chalice along with other items to upgrade the Emerald Ring to the Emerald Artifact
  - Use x4 Avaricious Chalice along with other items to upgrade the Crown of Greed into the Crown of Avarice
    - Item Idea Credit: ChocolateTimbits
- Freshly-Minted Coins
  - Costs x1 Stock of Stonks (capped at 5,000 global purchases every 4 hours)
  - Used in various amounts to obtain the following:
    - Scavenger Ring/Artifact
    - Ring/Artifact/Relic of Coins
- Scavenger VI
  - Costs x20 Stock of Stonks
Designer's Note: The price of each item in terms of Stock of Stonks cost is subject to change.
#### ➥ Obtaining Stock of Stonks
As the Stock of Stonks is receiving new uses, we only thought it would be fair to finally give it some more permanent methods of obtaining.
#### Stonks Auction
Located in the Stonks Exchange, Richard is how you will access the brand new Stonks Auction. These Auctions are global, with one happening each SkyBlock month (every ~10 hours). The Top 100 bidders each receive 25 Stock of Stonks, followed by 15 Stock of Stonks for Top 500, 10 for Top 1000, and so on.
#### Auction Bidding
Winning an item at any of Shen's Auctions will additionally reward 10 Stock of Stonks. Designer's Note: This amount is subject to change. Please give us feedback on the new obtaining methods for this item!
#### Relic of Coins
The Relic of Coins has a 1/40,444 chance of dropping a Stock of Stonks every 60 seconds.
#### ➥ Aurelia
Aurelia is an NPC that allows you to increase the amount of coins you put in your Midas Sword and Midas Staff. This allows players to upgrade their existing Midas items with Golden Fragments then sink coins accordingly to make the item’s ability stronger. You can also find her at the Trade Center!

### ► Campaign Poster
During this Alpha test, players will be able to complete certain tasks across the server to help test the update. Upon completion of all tasks, players will earn the Campaign Poster on the main network. You can access the list of tasks and your completion towards them via the Centauri NPC in The Hub.
