# Changelog/2024/June 4
Categories: 2024 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[June 4] Better Mayors Changes
Updated: June 4, 2024
Hello,
We would like to thank everyone for the overwhelmingly positive reception on the release of the Better Mayors update to the Alpha Network yesterday. We have implemented several changes after reading through player feedback:
- Fine Flour can now be used as a material in the Sprayonator
  - It attracts Pests of all types
  - Plots sprayed with Fine Flour grant +20 Farming Fortune while farming within them
- Foxy's Sweet Benevolence perk now grants +30% Chocolate from duplicate rabbits during Hoppity's Hunt
- Midas Jewel now grants Ability Damage instead of Intelligence
  - LEGENDARY: +350 Intelligence > +8% Ability Damage
  - MYTHIC: +400 Intelligence > +10% Ability Damage
- Added an Upgrade Fragments section to the Bazaar, and moved the following items to that section:
  - Bonzo Fragment
  - Dark Queen's Soul
  - Golden Fragment
  - Livid Fragment
  - Scarf Fragment
  - Thorn Fragment
  - Winter Fragment
- The streak mechanic on the Catch a Fish Carnival game has been slightly nerfed
  - Will likely be further adjusted during testing
- Nerf to Gemstone Equipment
  - Full Set Bonus: +75 > +25 Mining Fortune
  - Recipe: 1 Flawless + 64 Fine > 2 Flawless
- Buff to Dwarven Handwarmers
  - +15 > +30 Mining Fortune
We have also fixed a few bugs:
- Divan's Powder Coating now correctly mentions the "Armor of Divan" rather than "Divan's Armor"
- Fixed a soft lock issue in the Zombie Shootout Carnival game
- Fixed Pet particles showing during Carnival games
On one last note, Diaz is retaining her Shopping Spree perk (10x Daily NPC Buy Limits). This was always the case, but our original forum post implied that she was getting an entirely new set of perks (replacing the old ones). The post has since been edited to clarify this.

# Changelog/2024/June 6
Categories: 2024 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[June 6] Better Mayors Changes 2
Updated: June 6, 2024
Hello!
We're back with another series of changes and fixes with Better Mayors.
## Midas Sword Changes
- Base Stats: +150 > +170 Damage
- Greed Ability: +175 > +225 Max Damage/Strength (Fragged only)
  - Note: The cost to attain the Max Damage/Strength is remaining the same, the scaling was just buffed.
- Upgrade Stars now scale with the Greed Ability
  - Note: We are aware of an issue on Alpha where the Gilded Reforge and Hot Potato Books are also scaling based on Upgrade Stars, this is unintentional behavior and means the weapon is slightly overtuned
Designer’s Note: These changes mean that the Midas Sword is approximately 1-2% better than Dark Claymore with a Golden Dragon equipped.
## Zombie Shooting Enhancements
- Lamps now give +100 points and a 1.2x points boost for 6s upon getting hit
- Removed Accuracy from the scoreboard
  - Hovering over your Total Kills in the reward summary shows your accuracy now
- Added a particle indicator when a Zombie is 1.5s away from despawning (30 ticks)
- Zombies now wear a corresponding chestplate so it's easier to distinguish their rarity
- Reduced the number of Zombie kills you need in a single match from 75 > 60 for a specific Carnival Goal
## Other Changes
- The Carnival now has a zone discovery message to improve player guidance
- Updated the Bee Mask's item lore to mention that it only boosts Bee Pet's base stats (not perk stats)
  - This is only a visual change, this was already the functionality
- Added further clarification to the Golden Bounty's item lore
- Adjusted the warp coordinates of /warp stonks
- Jerry's Perkpocalypse rotation no longer includes Diaz
- Music now starts instantly when a Carnival minigame begins
- Ravenous Rhino now requires 50 Visitors served to unlock
- Updated the reward summary of Carnival minigames to include the number of Tokens won
## Bug Fixes
- Fixed a typo in Clerk Seraphine's dialogue
- Fixed Fine Flour being included in the Gemstone Sack
- Fixed Glossy Gemstone not being supported by the /getfromsacks command
- Fixed Mayor perks being unintentionally active in The Rift
- Fixed players being sometimes visible while in Carnival minigames
- Fixed the Bee and Frog Mask's ability names being the same
- Fixed the bonus +35 coins from Daedalus Axe not being consumed by the Crown of Avarice
- Fixed the coins message not showing up when killing Goblins while wearing a Crown of Avarice
- Fixed the Frost Walker enchantment being enabled in the Carnival area, allowing players to grief the Catch a Fish minigame
- Fixed the Mineral Armor and Glossy Mineral Armor's abilities not visually stacking with each other in item lore
- Fixed the pets menu being empty while initiating a search

# Changelog/2024/March 21
Categories: 2024 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[March 21st] Glacite Tunnels Alpha Testing
Updated: March 21, 2024
Hey all!
Today we're opening alpha complete with a new late-game mining area known as the Glacite Tunnels.
You will need Heart of the Mountain Tier 7 to participate in this content. If you do not already meet this requirement, please speak to Centauri in the Hub. While there, you should also pick up the featured items for this update.
Once ready, you should talk to Gwendolyn in the Dwarven Mines, who will direct you to meet Dulin over at the Dwarven Palance's Hanging Court. Complete his quest and you can take the Dwarven Railroad System down to the Glacite Tunnels.
Once you reach the tunnels, you should talk to the NPCs in the Dwarven Base Camp before mining around the tunnels in search of Glacite Mineshafts, which are a new kind of instanced content you can teleport right on into and mine in until your heart’s content.
As usual, you should report any bugs at https://hypixel.net/bug-reports/create, making sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!
We look forward to hearing your feedback!
Currently Planned Features:
Planned features are features that are currently in the works and may be seen soon. Not all upcoming features are on this list; gotta leave some surprise!
- Fossils obtained from Suspicious Scraps will eventually be a method to obtain new Pets.
- Some Tablist Widgets will be added to give you some more information about Mineshafts.
- Some HOTM 6-7 perks will work in the Glacite Tunnels + Glacite Mineshafts.
Currently Known Issues:
Issues in this list are major non-conclusive issues and will be frequently edited to reflect the current state of Alpha. If you have more that you would like to add to a currently existing issue- feel free to do so by making a new bug report with the extra information. (Please still mark it as an alpha issue!)
- All Forge times are set at 5 seconds. This is intentional for this Alpha session.
- Mineshaft Portals spawn very frequently. This is intentional for this Alpha Session.
- Warping out of a cold zone will keep cold in the scoreboard.
- Only the owner of a Glacite Mineshaft will get notified about the current active Mining Event.
- Chisels can be used without meeting their HOTM requirements.
- Scatha's 'Wormhole' perk can affect Tungsten and Titanium.
- Umber is not mineable in the Umber Mineshafts
- Onyx gemstone is currently giving Crit Chance - should be Crit Damage.
- The HOTM XP requirements for HOTM 8, 9, and 10 are currently lower than intended.
- The Fossil Excavator does not display the amount of Glacite Powder gained during rewards summary.
- Daily Grind HOTM perk does not currently apply to Glacite Tunnels commissions.
- Loot from Frozen Corpses, specifically Skeleton Corpses, is not as valuable as intended.Reporting major issues may be rewarded!

# Changelog/2024/May 21
Categories: 2024 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[May 21] Chocolate Factory 2.0
Updated: May 21, 2024
Hello!
Hoppity’s event was well received, so we added a few features to enhance its gameplay. This Alpha testing session is centered around it.
## ► 2 New Rabbit Family Towers
We noticed that five towers weren’t providing enough gameplay depth, even during the first few prestiges, so we added 2 new family members:
- Rabbit Uncle grants 6 chocolate per second per level. You unlock them after the first prestige. They clearly peaked in high school and can’t stop talking about that infamous game they lost during the championship. Not the most riveting character to talk to.
- Rabbit Dog grants 7 chocolate per second. You unlock them after the second prestige. They are the pet of the family, but because he’s a dog, he still performs better than the rabbits.
## ► Shop Milestones
To add a strategic layer, we’ve introduced shop milestones. This system rewards 24 new rabbits based on your total chocolate spent in the shop. Players now have an extra reason to invest chocolate in the shop, as it contributes to factory progression.
- This system grants mythic rabbits earlier in the reward tree.
- Each rabbit represents a letter from the Greek alphabet, so you are all going to gain immense general knowledge. Unless you already speak Greek.
Unfortunately the milestones are not retroactive! You will have to spend the chocolate again!
## ► Prestige 5
Introducing a 5th Prestige, unlocking a new rabbit rarity: Divine. Five Starborn Rabbits increase the max level of your rabbit family members by 1.
Here are the other perks of prestige 5:
- Level up rabbit family towers to level 220 (And 225 if you have all 5 Starborns)
- Unlock new shop items
- Increase max storage from 25B to 60B.
- Prestige multiplier goes from +1x to +1.5x.
## ► New Shop items and yearly shop cap
Each Update of the chocolate factory will increase the max amount of chocolate per day, therefore we are adding yearly caps on shop items balanced around the production you should have around the time you unlock those items.
We also added a few extra things to spend all your chocolate on:
- Prestige 4
  - Chocolate Dye - A chocolate color Dye.
    - Cost: 4B - Max 4 per SkyBlock year.
  - Chocolate Barn - A fantastic looking barn!
    - Cost: 5B - Max 1 per year.
- Prestige 5
  - Chocolate Fortune: Grants 1 Cocoa Fortune per level.
    - Cost: 2B per level (25 level)
  - Fish Chocolat a la Vapeur - Used to obtain Hoppity’s Abiphone Contact.
    - Cost: 50B - Max 1 per year
    - His contact allows you to buy his 7 rabbits later in the year if you missed the event. Hoppity might also sometimes call you…
## ► Unlockable Rabbits
We added a short list of achievements that once done, will add new rabbits to the Egg pool of rabbits. There are a few rabbits related to the chocolate factory gameplay, such as:
- Hub (Common Rabbit)
  - Find 15 different egg locations in the Hub
We also added a few rabbits that are unlocked after doing various activities in the game, for example:
- Necron (Epic Rabbit)
  - Defeat Necron in the Catacombs
Let us know what you think of this system!
Your feedback during alpha has an impact!
## ► General Changes
- We added a few Crystal Hollow Nucleus egg locations. It might seem like an annoying change but we feel like the spirit of the event is lost too much in the Nucleus, it still remains a simpler zone to collect the eggs.
- Rabbit the Fish and Fish the Rabbit are now obtainable…somehow.
- Stray rabbits now stay 20 seconds inside the menu instead of 5, and are twice as common.
- During Hoppity’s Event, right after collecting an egg, the first stray rabbit you find has a small chance to be a Golden Rabbit! Those have a stronger effect than stray rabbits.
- Fixed a bug regarding duplicate rabbits. Finding/Crushing a duplicate should now correctly give 10% extra chocolate based on how many duplicates of those rabbit you found, capping at +100%.
## ► Small Glacite Tunnels Changes
We've made a couple small changes after the most recent Glacite update.
- Blocks broken with custom abilities, such as Efficient Miner or Pickobulus, now affect Mineshaft rates again. However, these blocks broken will be at 50% effectiveness towards Mineshaft pity.
- Scrap drop chances have been significantly buffed.
- Removed all Powder drops from corpses now that we give out a flat amount per corpse.
- Adjusted some Tungsten and Umber block strengths.
New Scrap Chance Changes:
|Scrap|Old Chance|New Chance|
|-|-|-|
|1st Scrap in Mineshaft|1/1k|1/200|
|2nd Scrap in Mineshaft|1/1.5k|1/400|
|3rd Scrap in Mineshaft|1/2k|1/800|
|4th Scrap in Mineshaft|1/2.5k|1/1400|
|5th Scrap in Mineshaft|1/3k|1/2200|
## ► Other Changes
We fixed a bunch of bugs this patch too. Here's one in particular that we thought you should be made aware of before we rolled to live.
- Fixed an issue where Ender Pearls could be used in Dungeon Trap Rooms.
  - We appreciate your feedback regarding this change. This change will be reverted by the time this update releases.
## ► Where is Better Mayors?
We are working hard on making the update look as good as possible before sending it for alpha testing, where you'll learn all the details on the Alpha thread. There's a lot to unpack, so stay tuned.
Enjoy this minigame teaser while we wait.
That’s it! We hope you will enjoy those additions to the Chocolate Factory minigame. If you encounter any bugs, whether they be small or large, please make sure to report them here. Make sure that you select alpha.hypixel.net as the server IP!

# Changelog/2024/May 3
Categories: 2024 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[May 3] Dragon Rush & Primal Dragons
Updated: May 3, 2024
Hello!
We are putting the Dragon Rush & Primal Dragons update back on the Alpha Network. Since our last alpha with this update, we have made a significant number of changes.
Here is a quick recap of what is included with this update in case you missed the last alpha:
- Craft Awakened Summoning Eyes using Summoning Eyes and Null Ovoids
- Use them to summon Dragon Rushes and powerful Primal Dragons
- Obtain brand new items:
  - Primal Dragon Armor (good for Magic Find and Pet Luck!)
  - Blade of Dragonfire (an upgrade to the Aspect of the Dragons!)
  - Primal Dragon Egg (a mythic upgrade to the Ender Dragon pet!)
## ► Primal Dragon Armor
One of the most significant changes that has been made since the last alpha is to Primal Dragon Armor.
- Primal Blood has been nerfed and now gives +4% armor stats per milestone instead of +20%
- Kill milestones are now at 50, 125, 250, 500, 1,000, and 2,000 Dragon Kills (rather than number of digits)
- Each piece of Primal Dragon Armor has been given +4 Pet Luck at base (+16 Pet Luck total)
Designer’s Note: The stats of Primal Dragon Armor were out of control from the previous alpha, and outclassed most other combat armor sets to an unreasonable degree. We are shifting the focus of the armor set to be more focused on giving lots of Magic Find and Pet Luck instead. Primal Dragon Armor will still be powerful, but we hope that these changes help the set find a niche rather than dominating every other combat armor set.
## ► Awakened Summoning Eyes
After playtesting last alpha we determined that Awakened Summoning Eyes were too difficult to obtain, so we have made some changes in hopes of resolving that.
- The craft now calls for 5 Summoning Eyes instead of 8, but 4 Null Ovoids instead of 2
- Voidgloom Seraphs now have a small chance to drop an Awakened Summoning Eye
  - Tier II: ~0.12%
  - Tier III: ~0.16%
  - Tier IV: ~0.20%
- Special Zealots now have a 4% chance to drop an Awakened Summoning Eye
## ► Other Changes
Several other miscellaneous changes and fixes have been made since the last alpha.
- The chances for a Primal Dragon to spawn after a Dragon Rush have increased to 20% (from 15%)
- Awakened Summoning Eyes are now mentioned in the Lone Adventurer's Dragon Guide
  - You can now see how many you have collected in this menu as well
- Added a Dragon Tablist Widget that shows the Health of alive Dragons and your Damage dealt to them
- The Dragon Egg build now changes color depending on the type of Dragon spawned
  - The animation has also been improved since the last alpha
- Dragonfuse Gloves now work with the Blade of Dragonfire
- Magic Find and Pet Luck no longer scales on dungeonized items while in Dungeons
- Adjusted the quality of certain drops, more desirable loot should now drop more often
- Primal Dragons now drop Primal Dragon Hearts, which are used to craft the Blade of Dragonfire
- Increased the drop chance of Primal Dragon Eggs to 0.25% (from 0.1%)
- Remnant of the Eyes can now be sold for 1 Coin to NPCs
- Fixed an issue where Dragon Protectors weren't absorbing the damage Primal Dragons took
- Fixed an issue where certain Dragon attacks were appearing simultaneously
We hope that these changes will alleviate the issues that were encountered in the previous alpha. If you have any further feedback, please leave it in this thread, as we will be actively monitoring it!
If you encounter any bugs, whether they be small or large, please make sure to report them here. Make sure that you select alpha.hypixel.net as the server IP!
That's all for this Alpha, happy dragon slaying!

# Changelog/2024/November 13
Categories: 2024 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[Nov 13th] Pesthunter's Wares, Chocolate Factory Additions, Crimson QOL and more!
Updated: November 13, 2024
Hello!
Today we're opening up the Alpha Network with a large amount of content spread across several areas of the game. If you're interested, take a look below!
UPDATE (18/11/2024): Changes related to Crimson Isles will not roll with this release as we are ironing out some details regarding item balancing, namely the Kuudra armors and spawning changes. The Crimson Isles changes are not cancelled and will release as soon as they're ready.
Changes mentioned here may not be on Alpha at the time of writing, but are planned!
- A Beginner's Guide to Pesthunting will receive a custom texture rather than a vanilla book.
- Biohazard Armor will have its rarity reduced from Rare -> Uncommon on account of its predecessor, Mushroom Armor, having a base rarity of Common.
- Guaranteed crop drops from pests will use farmingfortune+cropfortune for that crop instead of just farmingfortune in calculating the total crop drops.
- The Field Mouse pest will be given a random guaranteed crop drop when killed (can be any crop). This drop scales as it normally would when dropped from its resident pest.
- Removed base Farming Fortune from Biohazard Armor.
- Mushroom Armor & Biohazard Armor's tripling abilities now only work while in The Garden.And more to come!
Changes mentioned here may not be on Alpha at the time of writing, but are planned!
- Increase Slug’s RNG Crop Drops from 3x Red/Brown Mushroom Block to 15x
- Increase NPC sell price of Cactus from 3 -> 4
  - Increase NPC sell price of Enchanted Cactus Green from 480 -> 640
  - Increase NPC sell price of Enchanted Cactus from 76,800 to 102,400
- Decrease NPC sell price of Nether Wart from 4 -> 3
  - Decrease NPC sell price of Enchanted Nether Wart from 640 -> 480
  - Decrease NPC sell price of Mutant Nether Wart from 102,400 -> 76,800
    - Our rationale for this change was that Beetle was by far and away the best pest for NPC selling in terms of guaranteed crops from pests, but we will hold off on it for the time being seeing as it's not as effective when NPC selling via manual farming.
- Instead of granting +1 crop, Hedgehog Pet and Beady Bonus will instead now grant +100 Farming Fortune on Pests, allowing for scaling to occur on a crop-by-crop basis.
- Dung Dye odds now scale with Farming Fortune instead of Magic Find.
- Made further adjustments to guaranteed pest crop drops (see below). Please note that you can get additional bonus crops from the Hedgehog Pet and Beady Eyes reforge in addition to being able to have more pests overall (which we anticipate will be used when gunning for contest PBs) due to pest/mouse traps.EDIT 15TH NOV: THIS TABLE IS NOW OUTDATED - SEE DAY 3 PESTHUNTER'S WARES CHANGES

- Base coins from killing Pests (excluding Field Mice) increased from 25 --> 1,000.
- Base coins from killing Field Mice increased to 10,000
- NPC sell price of all Pest vinyls increased from 1,000 --> 50,000.

- NPC sell price of Bookworm's Favorite Book increased from 250 -> 10,000.

- NPC sell price of Clipped Wings increased from 1 -> 1,000
- George sell price of EPIC Slug Pet increased from 2,000 -> 500,000
- George sell price of LEGENDARY Slug Pet increased from 5,000 -> 5,000,000
- Increased Pest spawn chance from 0.1% to 0.2% as a result of fixing an issue allowing a single input that breaks multiple cane/cacti to roll twice for pests, effectively allowing these crops to spawn pests twice as fast.
- Like with offline pest messages, players will also be able to click on Pest/Mouse Trap messages to teleport to the plot in which the relevant trap is placed.
- Added a new texture for 'A Beginner's Guide to Pesthunting'

- A new tiered bonus will be added to Biohazard Armor:
  - 2/4 pieces grants +150 Farming Fortune on Pests.
  - 3/4 pieces grants +175 Farming Fortune on Pests.

    - We are holding off on this as we've instead updated the guaranteed pest crop drop tables. We reckon this will be the last pest-related change for the time being. See below!

- Added combat requirements and Kuudra clear requirements to all Kuudra armor sets.
  - Each tier of Kuudra Armor now requires the completion of that respective Kuudra difficulty.
  - Hollow Wand and Aurora Staff now requires the completion of Kuudra and Combat 22.
- Aurora Helmet has been buffed to match/exceed the strength of Wither Goggles at higher tiers, making it no longer useless.
- Magic Damage no longer interacts with Crimson Armor. You can no longer gain Crimson stacks through magic damage, and magic damage no longer procs swipes.
- The stat bonuses from Blazetekk Ham Radio are now only active in the Crimson Isle and Kuudra Hollow.
- More items have been unsoulbound:
  - Corrupted Fragments
  - Lava Shell
  - All Attribute Shards
- The Blaze Slayer Distillates have been added to the Slayer Sack.
- The Blaze Slayer Distillates now all have equal weights in the Blaze Slayer drop pool.
  - Gabagool Distillate will no longer have an effectively lower drop chance due to not being affected by Magic Find.
- Moved the Lava Fishing category to Woods and Fishes.
- Fixed an issue where Crimson Swipe dealt 1000x more damage than intended.
- Fixed Trophy Fish Chance being boosted by sources that buff all stats.
- Adjusted the recipes for Empty Storm and Empty Hurricane Bottles
- Adjusted the recipe for the Travel Scroll to the Smoldering Tomb
- Made some changes to mob spawning in the Crimson Isle. (Looking for feedback!)
#### Planned Changes
- Diamond Trophy Hunter Magmafish Reward will be claimable retroactively.
- The abilities and damage scaling of all Kuudra sets will be adjusted as necessary.
- Fervor and Aurora Armor abilities to be activated on sneak instead of auto activate.
- Add Tentacle Meat to the Hellwisp loot pool.
## ► Pesthunter's Wares
Our goal with this part of the update is to flesh out Philip's offerings in the Pesthunter's Wares menu, which previously only had an accessory line. Rather than using Coins, this shop now uses Pests as a currency.
### ➥ Pest & Mouse Traps
Traps are new infinitely reusable items that you can place within your Garden Plots to catch Pests while you are offline! All you have to do is check in on them every now and then to ensure they're filled with bait. Bait can be placed in the orange slot, while trapped Pests are kept within the Iron Bars! Pests can be lured by placing Bait in the trap (Plant Matter, Dung, etc.). Once caught, Pests consume a Bait from the trap and you are allowed to release them from the trap to be vacuumed. Pest Traps can hold up to 2 pests, essentially allowing you to come back to more pests in your garden after partaking in other content.
Pest Traps can be purchased from Pesthunter Phillip for 50 Pests. An improved version of the Pest Trap, the Mouse Trap, can also be purchased from him, costing 125 Pests. Compared to the standard Pest Trap, these can hold an additional Pest and are x3 as likely to catch a new Pest: the Field Mouse!
### ➥ New Pest: Field Mouse
The Field Mouse is a new Pest that is only found within Pest and Mouse Traps. Field Mice are considered "elusive" mobs, meaning they are affected by Enderman Slayer LVL 9's perk. Field Mice drop 1 of every type of Bait when vacuumed, but they also have two rare items that are unique drops: the Squashed Toy and the Squeaky Mousemat.
### ➥ New Items
- Squashed Toy: Equipment Reforge that grants Farming Fortune, Bonus Pest Chance, and a decreased spawn cooldown for Pests (dropped rarely from Field Mice)
- Squished Mousemat: A fairly unique item that lets you quickly set your pitch and yaw (dropped rarely from Field Mice)
- Biohazard Armor: An upgrade to Mushroom Armor that gives Bonus Pest Chance and increases Pest crop drops (purchased from Pesthunter Philip)
- Pesthunter's Gloves: Equipment piece that grants Bonus Pest Chance and a decreased spawn cooldown for Pests (purchased from Pesthunter Philip)
- A Beginner's Guide to Pesthunting: When applied to an armor piece upgrades Pesterminator V to VI (purchased from Pesthunter Philip)
- Hedgehog Pet: Farming Pet that lets you kill Pests faster (even during Derpy) and makes them drop more crops (purchased from Pesthunter Philip)
- Pesthunter's Lair Barn Skin: A cool new Barn Skin for the most determined of Pesthunters! (purchased from Pesthunter Philip)
### ➥ Changes to Wheat
- Enchanted Hay Bales (old version) and Tightly-Tied Hay Bales have been replaced with Enchanted Wheat and Enchanted Hay Bales (new version)
- They are crafted with x160 Wheat and x160 Enchanted Wheat respectively (like most other recipes)
- All places in the game that previously used Enchanted Hay Bales (old version) and Tightly-Tied Hay Bales now use Enchanted Wheat and Enchanted Hay Bales (new version)
- Added a legacy trades menu accessible through /ltrades that allows players to trade in the old versions of items for new ones (right now it only has Enchanted Hay Bales (old version) and Tightly-Tied Hay Bales)
### ➥ Balancing
- Swapped the Pesterminator Enchant's Farming Fortune and Bonus Pest Chance bonuses
- The Buzzing Reforge (from Clipped Wings) now grants Farming Fortune instead of Speed
- The Beady Reforge (from Beady Eyes) now only drops 1 additional crop instead of 3
- The T-Rex Pet's Tyrant perk now only increases combat stats
In addition to the above, we have rescaled how Farming Fortune interacts with guaranteed Pest drops on a per-pest basis, and also buffed Pest RNG drops across the board, though we have also made them rarer.
The aim here is for all Pests to contribute relatively equally towards Garden Milestones, which is much healthier than the current rates which massively favor some pests more than others.

Both tables are not final and are more than likely going to change throughout this Alpha as we observe how the new rates feel, and if they're sustainable in the long-run.
### ➥ Other Changes
- For every 100 Bonus Pest Chance you have, one more Pest can be on your Garden before you start to lose Fortune
- Fine Flour can no longer be used in the Sprayonator, but can now be composted
- Decreased the delay before Pests spawn when entering a Garden Plot
- The Dung Dye drop chance is now affected by Magic Find
- Added a confirmation menu to all purchases made in Anita's Shop
- Added the /pesthandbook command to view the Pest Handbook (alias /phb)
- When your Pest Repellent expires a chat message is now sent
- Added Jack O' Lanterns to the Agronomy Sack
- The Pesthunter Accessory line is no longer Soulbound
- Added an Autopet rule for talking to a Visitor
- Added a 1 Coin NPC Sell Price to the Mysterious Crop
## ► Chocolate Factory
We are introducing a slew of new features and changes to the Chocolate Factory with this update. Our goal is to make the collecting experience feel less grind-y and to allow you to target specific Rabbits!
### ➥ New Tower: Rabbit Hitman
Unlocked right at Chocolate Factory I, the Rabbit Hitman collects Chocolate Eggs you miss during Hoppity's Hunt.
The Rabbit Hitman has 28 slots for eggs, purchasable for coins. Once you miss an egg during Hoppity's Hunt, the Rabbit Hitman will automatically gather it for you if he has an open slot. Eggs can then be opened through the Hitman's menu. Once you open an egg from a slot it goes on cooldown (stacks in increments of 20 minutes). Lime Dye are open slots while Red Dye are slots on cooldown
### ➥ Rabbit Hotspots
Every Hoppity's Hunt each Rabbit will be assigned a Hotspot, which is an island that they will have a 50% higher chance to appear in. You can see which Rabbits have what Hotspots from within your Hoppity's Collection menu.
We have also introduced many new Rabbits called "Residents" which can ONLY be found on their given Hotspot/island. Rabbits that are considered Residents can be easily identified in the Hoppity's Collection menu. The Achievement Rabbits that have requirements tied to specific islands are now also Residents of that island.
### ➥ Other Changes
- Added two new items to the Chocolate Shop
  - Choco Minion Skin: Applies a Choco Rabbit Skin to any Minion (costs 2.5B Chocolate)
  - Hot Chocolate Mixin: Grants +15 Pet Luck and +0.05x Chocolate Multiplier (costs 1.5B Chocolate)
- Buffed the Rabbit Shrine from 1 > +2% chance to find a new rabbit (per level)
- Buffed the Chocolate accessory line from 1 > +2% chance to find a new rabbit (per tier)
- Chocolate Eggs will now persist for an extra SkyBlock Day during Hoppity's Hunt
  - This effectively means that there are 6 Eggs available at any given time, rather than 3
- Added two new Achievement Rabbits
  - Morty: Find 15 different eggs in the Dungeon Hub
  - Carrot: Defeat the Carrot King Sea Creature
- Stray Rabbits are now guaranteed to spawn within the first 30s after opening a Chocolate Egg
- Added a chat message for when you have met the requirements for an Achievement Rabbit
- You are now able to shift-click towers to level them up 10 times (once you are Chocolate Factory II)
- Added a "Base Chocolate" statistic to the Chocolate Production button
- Added a short description to all of the Rabbit Towers
## ► Crimson Isle
To round out this update we are introducing a ton of improvements and balance changes to content found on the Crimson Isle. In the future we would like to continue improving this island.
### ➥ Lava & Trophy Fishing
- Added 'Trophy Fish Chance' as a profile stat
- Increased Vanille's catch chance from 3 > 4%
- Added +15 Fishing Speed to Hot Bait
- Added a new Full Set Bonus to all tiers of Trophy Hunter Armor which grants Fishing Speed based on Upgrade Stars
  - Bronze Hunter: +0.2 Fishing Speed per Upgrade Star
  - Silver Hunter: +0.4 Fishing Speed per Upgrade Star
  - Gold Hunter: +0.7 Fishing Speed per Upgrade Star
  - Diamond Hunter: +1 Fishing Speed per Upgrade Star
- Buffed all pieces of Finwave, Ichthyic, and Gillsplash equipment by +2 Fishing Speed
- Buffed the regular and treasure pools for Lava Fishing
- Collecting Trophy Hunter Rewards now also rewards 20 Magmafish of that tier
- You can now view how many Trophy Fish of each tier you have caught in Odger's menu
- Reduced the # of Gold Magmafish needed to craft a Diamond Magmafish from 80 > 20
- Added an instance-wide announcement when a player gets any Diamond Trophy Fish for the first time
- Added an instance-wide announcement when a player collects their Diamond Hunter Rewards
### ➥ Kuudra
- Reduced the amount of Enchanted Red Sand/Mycelium respectively needed to purchase Kuudra Keys
- Kismet Feathers can now be used to re-roll Kuudra Chests
- Buffed Kuudra Pet's Kuudra Specialit Perk from 5 > +20% towards Kuudra and his minions
- All Kuudra Armor Set's Tiered Bonuses now scale based on the prestige
  - The scaling is determined by the lowest prestige for all of the pieces you are currently wearing
- Aurora Armor Changes
  - Can now only gain stacks at most every 3/2/1 seconds (based on pieces equipped)
  - Stacks are now earned for whenever you hit an enemy with a magical attack
  - Reaching 10 stacks now shoots a scaling amount of Homing Missiles to the nearest enemy
- Crimson Armor Changes
  - Can now only gain stacks at most every 1.5/1/0.5 seconds (based on pieces equipped)
  - Stacks are now also earned from just hitting a mob
  - Stacks now also grant Ferocity and +% Damager (in addition to the Swing Range it already gave)
- Fervor Armor Changes
  - Can now only gain stacks at most every 1.5/1/0.5 seconds (based on pieces equipped)
  - Reaching 10 stacks now performs a ground pound whose damage scales based on Defense
  - Added a new Full Set Bonus that grants a 50% chance on mob hit to redirect 3 mob's aggro towards you
- Hollow Armor Changes
  - Can now only gain stacks at most every 3/2/1 seconds (based on pieces equipped)
  - Stacks are now earned by up to 5 nearby players attacking mobs
  - Maximum stacks are now dictated by the equipped lowest prestige armor piece
  - The Hollow Wand now casts spells using stacks from Hollow Armor
  - Revamped all of the Hollow Wand's spells
- Terror Armor Changes
  - Can now only gain stacks at most every 1/0.7/0.5 seconds (based on pieces equipped)
### ➥ Factions
- You no longer lose Reputation by doing Quests from the other Faction
- You can no longer have negative Reputation with a Faction
- Faction Quests now start automatically
- SkyBlock XP is now earned you reach a Reputation Tier in each Faction
- Completing a higher tier of Kuudra than the one you have for your Faction Quest will now complete it regardless
### ➥ Other
- Added new Storm and Hurricane Bottles that can hold 500,000 and 5,000,000 Charge respectively
  - They are crafted with a Bottle and 32/384 Orbs of Energy
- You can now stack the Smoldering Polarization effect by consuming multiple Re-Heated Gummy Polar Bears
- Matriarch's Perfume can now be used up to 4 times per day (up from 2)
- Blazetekk™ Ham Radio Changes
  - Range: 55 > 110 Blocks
  - Combat Wisdom: 1 > +5
  - Vitality: 2 > +10
  - A warning now displays in chat if more than two players are on the same channel
  - Alone players now gain half of the stat buffs
- You now receive a chat message when your Slayer Miniboss spawns
- Slayer Minibosses now have particles to help distinguish them
- The Magma Necklace's Piece Bonus now gives Crit Damage instead of Health
- The Glowstone Gauntlet's Piece Bonus now gives Intelligence instead of Health
- Chocolate Dye now costs 40B Chocolate (was 4B) and has an annual stock of 4 -> 1
### ➥ The Great Un-Soulbinding
- Every Upgraded Item From The Crimson Isle
- Bezos
- Blaze Ashes
- Burning Eyes

- Moogma Pelts

- Thunder Shards
- X
- Y
- Z
Many of these items were also added to the Bazaar.
## ► Bug Fixes
As always there are many bugs that have also been fixed. If you run into any new bugs for this alpha session, please report them at https://hypixel.net/bug-reports/create (make sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!).
- Fixed upgrading a Trick or Treat Bag to a Spooky Sack not carrying over Dark Candy
- Fixed Stonks (the pickaxe) preventing Endermite Nests from spawning while in your inventory
- Fixed the Fleet Reforge's passive working on unintended blocks
- Fixed Starfall Seasoning Drill Part not working outside the Dwarven Mines
- Fixed most blocks not counting towards the Rock Pet's progression
- Fixed some Sky Mall perks working outside of Mining Islands
- Fixed the SkyBlock Guide's Master Skills category showing 0 Total XP
- Fixed Sorrow Armor having a HOTM requirement on Stranded
- Fixed Combat Minions being unable to reach mobs within their spawn range when they have a Minion Expander
- Fixed the "Use Baits From Bag" setting not toggling properly
- Fixed Frozen Corpse's missing key message being inaccurate
- Fixed spraying Garden Plots with Fine Flour canceling out Stereo Harmony's ability
- Fixed being able to swap from a Daedalus Axe to a ranged weapon to still get the Daedalus Axe's coin bonus
- Fixed the Endermite Pet's Mite Bait perk not functioning properly
- Fixed the Frog Mask's ability only updating when opening your inventory
- Fixed the Frog Mask's item lore only updating when you re-equip the mask
- Fixed Minion Storage Chests now showing the opening animation when opened by a player
- Fixed the rewards message for bulk-claiming Jacob's Contest rewards not compacting Carnival Tickets
- Fixed the Day Saver not working during the Great Spook
- Fixed not being able to answer the Math Teacher Primal Fear if you have chat disabled
- Fixed the Deadline Primal Fear not starting its no moving countdown until you've moved at least once
- Fixed the Shadow Fury's ability being able to damage Primal Fears and Slayers Mobs spawned by other players
- Fixed the Boo Stone's Passive claiming it gave more Fear than it actually did
- Fixed sometimes being able to skip the Public Speaking Demon Primal Fear's ability
- Fixed the overcoming fear message still being sent in situations it shouldn't have
That's all for today!

# Changelog/2024/November 21
Aliases: Changelog/2024/November 24
Categories: 2024 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[November 21st] Crimson Isle Changes and Design Feedback
Updated: November 21, 2024
Hello!
The changes related to the Crimson Isle are now back on the Alpha network for testing. I’d like to take this opportunity to firstly apologize for the delay and to also address some questions, concerns, and design issues. These changes are not final and are open to change upon feedback.
The changes relating to Crimson Isle on the previous alpha thread are accurate unless superseded by information on this thread.
# New Changes
We’ve updated the abilities of the Kuudra Armor sets since the last alpha session, as follows:
### Crimson Armor
Issues
Crimson Armor has a significant issue where the swipe damage hits for far too high.
With this rework, your expected swipe damage will be much less than before.
By making this change, we hope that exploits that allow you to 1-tap enemies will cease to exist due to swipe damage being much more controlled.
From a balancing perspective, it doesn’t make sense for an armor set to buff your damage by that much either. It means any other armor set would have to inflate it’s damage to compete with Crimson Armor as an alternative.
Changes
The new damage calculation is as follows:
Your Input Damage is the damage you dealt to a mob. If I smack a mob for 10,000 damage, that is my input damage.
Swipe Strength is a multiplier that affects the damage of swipe. It scales from being 1x at Basic tier to 2x at Infernal, incrementing by 0.25x between tiers.
The Input Multiplier is your additive damage bonuses (from Combat Skill’s Warrior perk, damage-boosting enchantments, Golden Dragon etc.) as a multiplier.
If my additive damage bonus is 530%, my multiplier is 5.3x.
The new damage calculation essentially soft-caps the amount of damage Crimson Swipe can deal. The more additive damage bonuses you have, the less damage swipe is dealing in proportion to your initial hit’s damage.
That does not mean higher additive damage means less swipe damage, as your additive damage would have increased the raw input damage in the first place - the damage is only proportionally less.
Expected Damage
At Infernal Tier, we’re expecting swipe to deal 15 - 25% of your melee damage dealt, though results will vary depending on how many additive damage modifiers you have.
This damage ratio is mostly the same at the lower tiers. While their swipe multiplier is lower, it’s also likely you have less additive modifiers when using lower tiers of armor, resulting in your swipe damage to still be around the 15 - 25% range of your melee damage hit.
Impact of Change
There are two direct impacts of this change:
Instead of being to 1-shot them with swipe, most players will need to grapple blazes at the Smoldering Tomb to one-shot them.
We have decreased the HP of Blazes in the Smoldering Tomb to make one-shotting the blazes easier:
Smoldering Blaze: 5.5m HP → 4m HP
Millennia-Aged Blaze: 30m HP → 20m HP
Players who relied on swipe’s damage bonus to get them over the hump of one-shotting ghosts may no longer be able to do so.
This is somewhat intended, the way Crimson Armor used to be allowed players to one-shot ghosts with little investment.
So, what’s the point of Crimson Armor’s swipe now if the bonus is way less substantial? The armor is still useful as a small damage bonus on single-target enemies. It also provides melee enjoyers with minor AoE capabilities.
I think it’s true that there aren’t many existing uses of Crimson Armor due to lack of non-instanced, applicable combat content in the game, but that will definitely change in the future.
Design Thoughts: Can Melee keep up with Bow/Magic Damage?
Melee certainly does not deal as much damage as Magic Damage in its current state and it’s not likely to keep up with Terminator’s DPS either.
However, I think that the lack of power of melee shouldn’t be compensated by one armor set’s ability alone. When the time comes for a combat revamp, either melee damage will be buffed, mage damage will be tuned down, or mage abilities will come with caveats like having stronger initial DPS but you can actually run out of mana, making them not continuously spammable. All of the above may happen!
### Terror Armor
Issues
There are two main issues with Terror Armor that we tried to address:
Terror Armor offered a substantial DPS boost at base tier due to two bonus arrows.
The stack gain is so comically fast it might as well not exist.
As such, we released a change last alpha where the number of bonus arrows scaled depending on your Terror Armor tier, and there’s now a cooldown to gaining stacks.
The end result was Burning and Fiery armor were now shooting weirdly with only one bonus arrow on Alpha, so we’re implementing some adjustments on how the tiered upgrades work.
Changes
|Prestige|Previous|New|
|-|-|-|
|Base|+4% Damage per stack, +1.0% Arrow speed per stack.|+4% Damage per stack, +1.0% Arrow speed per stack. Shoot 2 extra arrows at 10 stacks that deal 20% arrow damage.|
|Hot|+6% Damage per stack, +1.4% Arrow speed per stack.|+5.5% Damage per stack, +1.4% Arrow speed per stack. Shoot 2 extra arrows at 10 stacks that deal 25% arrow damage.|
|Burning|+6% Damage per stack, +2.0% Arrow speed per stack. Shoot +1 arrow at 10 stacks.|+7% Damage per stack, +2.0% Arrow speed per stack. Shoot 2 extra arrows at 10 stacks that deal 30% arrow damage.|
|Fiery|+8% Damage per stack, +2.4% Arrow speed per stack. Shoot +1 arrow at 10 stacks.|+8.5% Damage per stack, +2.4% Arrow speed per stack. Shoot 2 extra arrows at 10 stacks that deal 35% arrow damage.|
|Infernal|+10% Damage per stack, +3% Arrow speed per stack. Shoot +2 arrows at 10 stacks.|+10% Damage per stack, +3% Arrow speed per stack. Shoot 2 extra arrows at 10 stacks that deal 40% arrow damage.|
Expected Damage
At Infernal Tier, you can expect your DPS to be increased by ~27% while using Terminator, putting it somewhat in line with Crimson Swipe.
Impact of Change
We’re aware that the stack gain change has a significant impact in Kuudra and are open to slightly reducing that stack gain cooldown if necessary, but, perhaps the following changes are better for the purpose of preserving the same run efficiency:
Decrease the amount of hits required for Kuudra’s hit phase by 20%.
Decrease the amount of damage required to kill Kuudra final phase by 25%.
Please test this on the Alpha Network and let us know if these changes are sufficient!
### Aurora Armor
Aurora Armor is now equipped with launch-able missiles that target enemies.
We are aware that the missiles were underwhelming both due to them not properly scaling with any damage bonuses and having a low base damage overall.
We have fixed the stack gain to scale, as follows:
|Pieces Equipped|Stack Gain Cooldown|
|-|-|
|2/4|1s|
|3/4|0.7s|
|4/4|0.5s|
- The missiles have been adjusted to have a base damage of 4,000 for all set tiers.
- The ability damage scaling is 0.3.
This change intends for Aurora Armor to be useful at an earlier stage but a lot less impactful for overall DPS when you have strong weapons with Wither Impact.
Higher tiers of Aurora Armor still fire more missiles than lower tiers, thus dealing more damage than their lower tier counterparts.
Please let us know how this change feels and whether adjustments are required.
### Crimson Isle Mob Spawning
We introduced a slight change to mob spawns as an attempt to reduce lag in the Crimson Isle. It is not our intention to impact gameplay with this change.
We have adjusted the settings to make the system less intrusive. Please let us know how this adjustment feels now.
### Other Changes
- Fixed Hollow Wand’s “Ichor Pool” increasing damage by 20% additive.
  It is now 1.2x multiplicative.
- Fixed Hollow Wand’s “Raging Wind” increasing damage by literally +30 damage to your hit.
  It is now +30% additive damage.
- The Aurora Armor and Fervor Armor abilities now activate on sneak.
- The activation patterns for Hollow Wand abilities have been shortened to two inputs instead of three.
- The Diamond Magmafish reward for Diamond Trophy Hunter is now retroactively claimable.
- Tentacle Meat has been added to the Hellwisp loot pool.
- Buffed the amount of Red Sand and Mycelium in Lava Fishing loot pool due to those materials being much easier to obtain.
### Relevant Questions
Will Attribute Shards be put on the Bazaar?
We do intend to add attribute shards to the Bazaar, but this is not a quick change to make as implementing Bazaar functionality requires an extensive amount of testing to make sure no issues arise.
It’s on the shortlist of things we want to do but will likely take quite a bit to implement.
Where is Kuudra Croesus?
Kuudra Croesus is another planned feature we intend to implement, but again it’s one that requires extensive development time/resources and testing to ensure that no issues arise.
What will happen to Aurora Staff?
Due to an oversight, we missed that the Aurora Armor change renders Aurora Staff’s rune functionality moot.
The planned implementation is that you can do the exact functionality as before the update just by holding the Aurora Staff itself. This change may not make this update, but will be implemented in the near future.
Why is the Blazetekk Ham Radio now area restricted?
While the stat gain from the Blazetekk Ham Radio isn’t particularly substantial, we believe the item is much more balanced to be meant as an item within Crimson Isle’s progression rather than one that buffed your stats in all islands.
The Blazetekk Ham Radio is definitely useful when it comes to doing Blaze Slayer fairly, but perhaps it’s crafting cost no longer reflects it’s power post-nerf. We are open to nerfing it’s crafting cost if deemed necessary.
The design of having the Blazetekk Ham Radio as an individual item that granted stats without occupying an armor/equipment slot was a mistake.
Changing this item into an accessory leads to museum complications (as it’s currently donate-able to the Museum), would give way too much stats as an accessory, and the designers think it simply shouldn’t be an accessory anyway - it’s current niche is more than fine.
What other Crimson Isle changes are planned?
Aside from some changes mentioned above, we do have a few more things we'd like to revisit in the future for the Crimson Isles:
- Boss Fight “revamps” for Crimson Isle Minibosses.
- Changes to Reputation Quest rewards to make them more appealing.
- Dojo changes to make the experience smoother and make Test of Control slightly more lenient.
- … and many more minor adjustments and bug fixes.
We have a lot of amazing content planned for 2025, so we may not be able to get back to the Crimson Isle for some time.
Please note that these ideas are still very early in the design phase; they may or may not make it to the next update, and it will take some time before we can fully commit to them.
I hope this answers many questions that you may have about the Crimson Isle update, and looking forward to hearing your feedback regarding the changes.
# [November 24th] Changes to current Alpha Session
These changes are to be rolled, but we might as well discuss them beforehand.
### Trophy Fishing Changes
- Implemented the following Trophy Fishing changes:
  Pity now triggers at 600 catches for all fishes.
  The catch rates of the following fish have now changed:
  Vanille: 4 (alpha) → 8%
  Karate Fish: 1 → 2%
  Slugfish: 10 → 15%
  Blobfish: 15 → 35%
  Golden Fish Appearance Rate: 15 to 20 minutes → 8 to 12 minutes
Design Notes
- The Blobfish change is so that it rolls after all the other trophy fish has rolled.
  Though 35% may admittedly be too high, probably targetting ~25%.
- The Golden Fish adjustment should make it much more likely you catch it’s Diamond before you catch all the other fish.
- With the adjusted rates, Trophy Fishing should be less than 100 hours total for most players.
### Bestiary Changes
The kills required for the following mobs has been reduced from 1,000 → 500:
- Barbarian Duke X
- Mage Outlaw
- Bladesoul
- Ashfang
- Magma Boss
The kills required for the following mobs has been reduced from 250 → 100:
- Lord Jawbus
The kills required for the following mobs has been reduced from 4,000 → 1,000:
- Pyroclastic Worm
### Slayer Changes
General Changes
- Increased the XP contribution cap to a slayer quest from a single mob from 50% to 75%.
  If you have enough Combat Wisdom, something e.g Burningsoul Demon can give you 75% XP progress.
  Requires testing, may revert.
Blaze Slayer
- Rolled the attempted fix of capping Burning Vengeance’s (ability) damage at 50m.
- Reduced the HP of Burningsoul Demon Minibosses:
  Tier 3: 12,000,000 → 9,000,000
  Tier 4: 25,000,000 → 17,500,000, 75,000,000 → 50,000,000
Enderman Slayer
- Increased the base weapon damage of Voidgloom Katanas by ~25%
  Also slightly increased their Crit Damage.
- Slightly increased the delay of Voidgloom Seraph going into his beams phase.
The intention here is to retain melee viability for Crimson Armor by allowing players to deal enough damage to reach hits phase during beams.
### Kuudra Armor Changes
Fervor Armor
- Changed the damage scaling of Fervor Armor’s Ground Pound to scale off of your EHP (HP and Defense), and is no longer affected by your melee/weapon damage.
- The Ground Pound ability now activates an additional time from Burning Tier and above (changed from Infernal only).
### Other Changes
- Reverted the Blazetekk Ham Radio location restriction.
  We’ll find a better way to solve the Blazetekk Ham Radio’s design “gripes”…
- Added crafting shortcuts to Blessed Bait and Whale Bait
### Planned Changes
- These changes are to compensate for Trophy Fish Chance now being an additive stat.
  Increased the Trophy Fish Chance of all Magma Rods by 5%.
  Increased the Trophy Fish Chance of the Trophy Hunter Armor, as follows:
  Silver: 5 → 10%
  old: 10 → 20%
  Diamond: 20 → 30%
### TBD Changes
- Adding Combat Wisdom to Slayer Weapons (means melee spawns bosses faster)
- Cap Endstone Sword’s damage buff at something.
# [November 25th] Changes to current Alpha Session
Hi, quick changes (some were already live on alpha, some are to be rolled):
- Blobfish 35 -> 25%
- Increased Trophy Fish Chance of Trophy Hunter armor sets, as follows:
  Bronze: 0 -> 5%
  Silver: 5 -> 10%
  Gold: 10 -> 20%
  Diamond: 20 -> 30%
- Added conditional Combat Wisdom to various Slayer weapons.
- Added a recipe to craft Hellfire Rod with 4 Diamond Magmafish.
- Added upgrades to the Crimson Isle Fishing Equipment line, with the final few upgrades requiring Diamond Magmafish.
- Standardized the upgrade cost of Trophy Hunter armor, with the final two upgrades requiring Diamond Magmafish
# [November 26th] Last Changes to current Alpha Session
The last roll actually came with a 20% reduction on Wolf Slayer spawning requirements, forgot to mention it.
Quick small and last patch for now:
- Burning Vengeance should now be properly capped at 50m.
- Fixed a minor wording issue regarding Combat XP from Slayer weapons.
- Slightly tinkered to the amount of XP rewarded by slayer bosses and minibosses.
  Will adjust again after Alpha.

# Changelog/2024/October 2
Categories: 2024 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[Oct 2nd] Museum & Minions Alpha!
Updated: October 2, 2024
Hello!
We've got more alpha content for you! The Museum rework is nearing completion which means it's time to go to alpha to get feedback.
## Museum Changes
- After an intense arm-wrestling match, Eleanor has replaced the Museum Curator. You can now simply speak to her.
- Instead of each item providing the same progress, items now grant different amounts of SkyBlock XP based on their difficulty to obtain and their usefulness in progression.
- Museum progression is now based on the SkyBlock XP obtained from each item and viewable in the new Museum Milestones system.
- Museum Milestones include many new rewards ranging from items to bank interest and bits boosts. Along with the fame perk, we also added extra fame ranks.
- NOTE: Some of your museum items in the "Special Items" category have had their categories changed. As such, they have been removed from your museum will be in your Jerry Deliveries.
Your feedback The goal of this alpha is also to balance the update, and for this we need your input. Let us know if you believe an item should give more or less XP, or if you encounter any other issues with this update.
## Minion Backend Changes
This alpha also contains major backend changes to minion spawning and functionality. The first person of a profile that goes to their private island will receive a message that their minions have updated. Everything else should be unchanged from the main server. Please report any new issues, from issues with item generation yields to as small as a minion holding the wrong tool.
Report any bugs HERE (remember to select alpha.hypixel.net as the Server IP!)

# Changelog/2024/October 24

[October 24] Backend Skin Changes
Updated: October 24, 2024
Hello!
Today we are opening the Alpha Network for several backend changes relating to skin textures in SkyBlock.
## ► Backend Skin Changes
This is a technical change to how we display skins in a variety of contexts - items, mobs, cosmetics, and much more. This is a relatively large backend change and requires dedicated testing on the Alpha Network.
Please help test by playing the game normally and reporting any issues as they arise, especially relating to items with skins or mobs that use skin textures.
That's all for today!
